### [1] [Prompt Chaining in Practice: A Case Study in Automated Scholarly Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27210)
*Andrei Lazarev*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出并实证评估了一种多阶段提示链方法，用于自动生成学术摘要（AI SciBrief 系统），并与经过优化的单次提示基线进行对比实验。结果显示，提示链方法在可靠性（100% 成功率 vs 50% 失败率）和质量（ROUGE-L F1 0.507 vs 0.486，主要得益于更高精确率）上均显著优于单次提示基线，结论认为提示链是复杂多步生成任务更可靠、更有效的工程模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术出版物呈指数级增长，急需自动化工具进行有效的信息综合。然而，简单的单次提示方法往往缺乏复杂综合任务所需的可靠性和质量。

Method: 设计并实现了一种多阶段提示链方法论，构建了 AI SciBrief 系统自动生成学术摘要；在“教育”领域，以人工撰写的“金标准”报告为基准，将提示链方法与经过精心优化的单次提示基线进行对比实验。

Result: 提示链方法实现 100% 成功率，而优化基线 50% 运行失败；在 ROUGE-L F1 分数上，提示链达到 0.507，基线为 0.486，优势主要来自更高的精确率。

Conclusion: 提示链是一种更可靠、更有效的工程方法，适用于复杂、多步骤的生成任务，能显著降低单片式提示固有的失败和不一致风险。

Abstract: The exponential growth of scholarly publications requires automated tools for effective information synthesis. However, simple, single-shot prompting methods often lack the reliability and quality required for complex synthesis tasks. This paper introduces and empirically evaluates a multi-stage prompt chaining methodology as a more reliable architectural pattern for such tasks. This approach is implemented in our system, AI SciBrief, which automatically generates scholarly digests. We conducted a comparative experiment, measuring the performance of our prompt chaining method against a carefully optimized single-shot baseline. Both systems were evaluated against a human-authored "gold standard" report for the "Education" domain. The results demonstrate a significant difference in reliability: our prompt chaining method achieved a 100% success rate, whereas the optimized baseline failed in 50% of its runs. In terms of quality, the proposed method also demonstrated a clear advantage, achieving a superior ROUGE-L F1-score (0.507 vs. 0.486), driven primarily by higher precision. We conclude that prompt chaining is a more dependable and effective engineering approach for complex, multi-step generative tasks, significantly mitigating the risks of failure and inconsistency inherent in monolithic prompts.

</details>


### [2] [AI-assisted pre-review of open-source software submissions: an experience report from BOSC 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27228)
*Tazro Ohta,Nomi L. Harris,Seth Carbon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BOSC 2026 会议尝试使用生成式 AI 辅助志愿者审稿人预审摘要，构建了 bosc-pre-review 智能体评估六项标准，以及 Runabilly 工具在 Docker 容器中构建测试项目。AI 仅提供证据供人工参考，最终决策由人类做出。审稿人调查显示多数认为有用，但倾向于自行核对 AI 结论而非盲目采纳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式 AI 使提交材料准备变得更容易，会议面临投稿激增的问题，希望利用 AI 减轻志愿者审稿人的负担，提高预审效率。

Method: 开发 bosc-pre-review 智能体技能评估六项审稿标准（开放性、有效开源许可证、可运行性等），并开发 Runabilly 在一次性 Docker 容器中构建和测试每个项目以确保安全。AI 仅收集证据呈现给审稿人，所有录用决策由人类做出。审稿结束后通过调查收集审稿人反馈。

Result: 大多数响应调查的审稿人认为预审有用，但他们更倾向于将 AI 的结论与自己的判断进行核对，而不是无条件接受 AI 结果。

Conclusion: 生成式 AI 可以作为辅助工具提高会议预审效率，但人工审核仍不可或缺，审稿人倾向于将 AI 作为参考而非替代。

Abstract: Most conferences rely on peer-review of submissions, but as generative AI makes it easier than ever to prepare submission materials, some conferences are seeing an overwhelming surge of submissions. We wanted to see if generative AI could help our conference's volunteer reviewers by pre-reviewing abstracts for certain criteria. The Bioinformatics Open Source Conference (BOSC) was well-positioned to experiment with this, as we already had a detailed rubric used by reviewers to evaluate submitted abstracts on multiple criteria, including openness (public availability of the code or other content associated with the project), valid open source license, and "runnability" (how easy it is to download, build, and run the project - an important measure of reusability). For BOSC 2026, we built bosc-pre-review, an agentic skill that assessed six review criteria, and Runabilly, which builds and tests each project in a disposable Docker container for safety. The AI only gathered evidence to present to the reviewers; humans made every decision regarding the acceptance of the abstracts. After the review period, we surveyed the reviewers to determine how useful they found the pre-review. Most of those who responded said they found it useful, but they preferred to check the AI's conclusions against their own, rather than accepting the AI results unquestioningly.

</details>


### [3] [Sympathetic Framing: Evaluating AI Alignment across Sociodemographic Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27232)
*Haran Shani-Narkiss,Michael Fire,Oren Tsur*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估大语言模型（LLMs）对新闻标题情感框架的理解与人类情感感知的对齐程度。通过英国代表性样本（n=3011）与七个LLMs对政治/地缘政治冲突新闻标题是否引发对特定一方同情的判断，发现模型间相关性差异大（0.789至0.4），头部模型在各人口统计学亚群中总体与人类判断一致，但存在统计学显著差异。研究揭示即使聚合性能高，AI对齐也非普遍一致，可能随人口特征和文化规范差异化，对伦理AI开发有重要启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLMs日益塑造信息消费和世界观，除偏见外，需关注其是否能把握文本框架传达的情感细微差别。现有研究缺乏大规模、人口统计学多样化的人类基线来全面评估LLMs对新闻框架情感感知的理解。

Method: 选取涉及政治和地缘政治冲突的新闻标题，招募3011名英国成年人（YouGov代表性样本）及七个主流LLMs，判断标题是否唤起对冲突特定一方的同情。计算AI与人类评价的相关性，并按年龄、性别、教育、地缘政治先验知识及冲突倾向等亚群分析差异。

Result: 模型与人类判断相关性从极高（GPT-5.2为0.789）到中等（Mistral Large 2512为0.4）不等。领先模型在所有人口统计学亚群中总体与人类一致，但组间存在统计学显著差异。聚合性能高并不意味着对齐普遍一致，对齐程度随人口特征和文化规范而异。

Conclusion: 该研究提供迄今最全面的LLMs新闻框架理解评估，揭示差异化对齐这一常被忽视的关键问题：即使整体表现优异，AI与人类的对齐可能因人口统计学特征和文化规范而不同。开发伦理且实用的AI系统时，必须考虑或忽略这种差异化对齐的需求，将产生重大影响。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly shaping how we consume information and form our worldview. This raises concerns beyond bias in AI: do LLMs grasp the emotional nuances conveyed via textual framing? In this work, we empirically evaluate how well an array of LLMs aligns with human emotional perception. Considering news headlines covering political and geopolitical conflicts, both human participants (n = 3011, a representative sample of the U.K. adult population, via a YouGov survey) and seven LLMs answered whether headlines evoked sympathy for a specified side in a conflict. We find that the correlation between AI and human evaluations varies across models, ranging from very high (0.789, GPT-5.2) to medium (0.4 ,Mistral Large 2512). Crucially, the leading models are broadly aligned with human judgments across all demographic subgroups, including age, gender, level of education, prior geopolitical knowledge, and participants' predispositions regarding the conflict, although there are statistically significant differences between groups. This research, with its robust design and large, demographically diverse dataset, offers the most comprehensive evaluation of LLMs' comprehension of news framing to date. Findings highlight an important, often-ignored aspect of differential alignment: even when aggregate performance is high, AI alignment is not universal -- it may correspond differently with demographic features and cultural norms. Considering or ignoring the need for differential alignment may therefore have significant implications for the development of ethical and useful AI systems.

</details>


### [4] [LayerRAG-Bench: A Cross-Layer Reliability Benchmark for Agentic Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27353)
*Musa Shams*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 LayerRAG-Bench，一个跨层可靠性基准，用于评估智能体 RAG 系统在证据、工具契约、授权和会话状态四层的故障表现；实验表明模式归一化能修复模式漂移但无法解决陈旧证据、缺失工具输出、权限拒绝和错误会话上下文等问题，且仅基于 groundedness 的评估会产生大量假阳性，支持分层评估原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体 RAG 系统常在表面上看似有依据，实则在证据、工具契约、授权或会话状态层失效；缺乏系统性、跨层的可靠性基准来量化和定位这些故障。

Method: 构建 LayerRAG-Bench 基准，包含 8 个企业领域、240 个任务、9 种故障场景、2 种契约模式，跨 9 个模型（OpenAI、Anthropic、Gemini）产生 38,880 条实时任务级记录；对比模式归一化、groundedness 评估等干预措施在各层的修复效果。

Result: 模式归一化将模式漂移成功率从 0.000 提升至 0.913，但对陈旧证据、缺失工具输出、权限拒绝、错误会话上下文无修复作用；仅基于 groundedness 的评估在陈旧和错误会话证据下产生大量假阳性。

Conclusion: 可靠性干预应针对特定层进行评估和归因，不能将单层修复误认为通用解决方案；需建立分层评估原则以指导智能体 RAG 系统的鲁棒性提升。

Abstract: Agentic retrieval-augmented generation systems can produce answers that appear grounded while failing at the evidence, tool-contract, authorization, or session-state layer. We introduce LayerRAG-Bench, a controlled cross-layer reliability benchmark with 8 enterprise domains, 240 tasks, 9 fault scenarios, 2 contract modes, and 38,880 live task-level records across nine models from OpenAI, Anthropic, and Gemini. Schema normalization raises schema-drift success from 0.000 to 0.913, but stale evidence, missing tool output, denied permissions, and wrong-session context are not recovered by schema normalization. Groundedness-only evaluation also produces substantial false positives under stale and wrong-session evidence. These results support a layer-specific evaluation principle: a reliability intervention should be credited for repairing its target layer without being mistaken for a universal fix.

</details>


### [5] [Same Facts, Different Diagnosis: Measuring and Mitigating Narrative Anchoring in Clinical Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27384)
*Prabhjot Singh,Pritam Deka,Vijay Chennareddy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了“叙事锚定”现象，即大语言模型在临床诊断推理时对社会语言语体敏感，而非仅关注临床内容；构建了事实保持的USMLE病例数据集，评估七大模型并提出NarrativeShield三智能体流线，将叙事锚定差距降至近零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有偏见研究多聚焦显式人口统计标记（种族、收入等），忽略了语体作为独立变异通道的影响。本文旨在隔离语体对临床诊断推理的因果效应，填补语体偏见基准缺失。

Method: 1) 构建1,000条USMLE临床病例，各改写为三种社会语言学人设，经独立审计模型验证事实保持。2) 在跨三架构家族、七规模的LLM上测试直接提示、思维链、显式去偏指令。3) 提出NarrativeShield：三智能体流线先结构化提取验证临床事实，再进行诊断推理。4) 以非指令微调基座模型做压力测试，考察零样本指令遵循能力对去偏干预的门控作用。

Result: 所有模型在直接提示下均表现出统计显著的叙事锚定（Gap 0.064–0.151）。思维链与去偏指令仅部分缓解偏差，且常伴随准确率崩塌。NarrativeShield将Gap压缩至-0.004–0.037，实现所有模型中最低的严重不稳定决策率（DSS<0.8），准确率损失适度且符合机制预期。压力测试表明去偏干预的生效取决于模型的零样本指令遵循能力，而非提示内容本身。

Conclusion: 语体是临床诊断推理中独立且可量化的偏见来源；结构化事实提取验证流线能有效消除叙事锚定，且不依赖模型规模或架构，但要求模型具备基础指令遵循能力。发布的经过人工验证的数据集可作为研究语体偏见的独立基准资源。

Abstract: Large language models used for clinical diagnostic reasoning are sensitive to sociolinguistic register, not just clinical content. We term this failure mode Narrative Anchoring: identical clinical facts expressed in different registers cause diagnostic outputs to diverge. Unlike prior demographic-bias work, which manipulates explicit identity tokens such as race or income, our benchmark isolates register as the sole channel of variation, with no demographic marker present in any form. We construct a dataset of 1,000 USMLE clinical vignettes, each rewritten into three sociolinguistically distinct personas under an independently audited fact-preservation guarantee, verified by a separate model that never sees the generation prompt. Across seven language models spanning three architecture families and scales, Narrative Anchoring is statistically significant under direct prompting in every model tested, with a Narrative Anchoring Gap of 0.064 to 0.151. Chain-of-thought reasoning and explicit debiasing instructions reduce the bias only partially, and their apparent gains are frequently confounded by accuracy collapse. We introduce NarrativeShield, a three-agent pipeline that structurally extracts and verifies clinical facts before diagnostic reasoning begins, reducing the Narrative Anchoring Gap to near-zero ($-0.004$ to $0.037$) and achieving the lowest rate of severely unstable decisions (DSS $<$ 0.8) of any method across all models, at a modest and mechanistically expected accuracy cost for most models. A stress test using a non-instruction-tuned base model shows that executing a debiasing intervention at all is gated by zero-shot instruction-following ability, not prompt content alone. We release our dataset, human-validated for fact preservation, as a standalone resource for studying register-based clinical bias.

</details>


### [6] [Benchmarking LLM Competence on Logical Inference over Probability Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27405)
*Nayera Hasan,Jack Greff,Alvin Grissom*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文推出了一个关于概率算子推理的基准测试，包含14,320个程序生成的英文提示，覆盖15种推理模板，系统变换问句形式、否定策略和表面内容。评估29个大语言模型后发现，多数模型存在与逻辑形式无关的回答偏好（倾向Yes或No），引入“能力下限”指标（Yes正确和No正确项中准确率较低者），仅9个模型超过随机猜测水平。同时在问句形式、动词短语、人名性别和来源等维度均发现偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言中不确定性表达与推理无处不在，对高风险领域（医疗、法律）至关重要。现有大模型在逻辑推理任务上的评估难以区分符号化推理与表层模式匹配，因此需要一个系统化的基准来考察模型对概率算子（如probably, might, must）的推理能力，并揭示潜在偏见。

Method: 构建基准：程序化生成14,320个英文提示，涵盖15种推理模板，系统变化问句形式、否定策略、表面内容（动词短语/活动、人名性别与来源）。评估29个大语言模型，计算其在Yes正确和No正确项目上的准确率，定义能力下限为两者中的较低值，并随机基线对比。同时沿多个轴（问句形式、动词短语、人名性别、人名来源）分析偏见。

Result: 多数模型表现出与逻辑形式无关的回答偏好，系统性倾向Yes或No。仅9/29模型的能力下限超过随机猜测（50%）。在问句形式、动词短语/活动、人名性别、人名来源四个变异维度上均观测到显著偏见。

Conclusion: 当前大语言模型在概率算子推理上仍缺乏稳健的符号化推理能力，普遍存在答案偏好与多维度偏见。提出的基准与能力下限指标可为未来改进提供量化标尺。

Abstract: Both expressions of uncertainty and inferences are ubiquitous in natural language, and valid inferences over natural-language expressions of uncertainty are necessary for not only everyday conversations but also for high-stakes domains such as medicine and law. While large language models are increasingly evaluated on logical reasoning tasks, disentangling principled, symbolic reasoning from clever surface-level pattern matching is fraught with difficulty. We introduce a benchmark for reasoning over probability operators--inference over sentences with gradable epistemic modals (e.g., probably, might, must) containing 14,320 procedurally-generated English prompts across fifteen inference templates, systematically varying question form, negation strategy, and surface content. Evaluating 29 models, we find that most show answer biases independent of the logical form, a systematic preference for Yes or No. We summarize this with a competence floor: the worse of a model's accuracy on Yes-correct and No-correct items. Only 9 of 29 models exceed random chance. We also test variations in question form, verb phrases/activity, and both the gender and origin of names used in the prompts, finding biases across every axis.

</details>


### [7] [Selecting Open-Weight Language Models for Zero-Shot Intent Classification: A Systematic Evaluation of 41 Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27421)
*Parishruthi Ganesh,Gerry Dozier,Cheryl Seals*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对41个开源语言模型在8个英语单标签意图分类数据集上进行系统性零样本评估，分析准确率、置信度校准、鲁棒性、统计可靠性、部署效率和基准饱和度，发现指令微调的3B模型可超越部分7B基座模型，MASSIVE上领先模型差异无统计显著性，SNIPS等基准已饱和失去区分度，指令微调对校准影响不一致。为意图分类模型选择提供实用指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务型对话系统中意图分类是核心组件，但从业者缺乏在算力、延迟和鲁棒性约束下选择可部署开源语言模型的系统性指导。

Method: 对涵盖15个模型家族、参数量135M-9B的41个开源语言模型，在8个英语单标签意图分类数据集（含标准基准、大规模语音助手语料库、生产级电商数据集）上进行零样本评估；ATIS数据集用5-shot辅助评估。除准确率外，还分析置信度校准、输入扰动鲁棒性、模型排名统计可靠性、部署效率和基准饱和度。

Result: 1) 指令微调的3B模型能超越部分7B基座模型；2) MASSIVE上领先模型差异经McNemar检验无统计显著性；3) SNIPS等常用基准已饱和，不再能有效区分当前开源模型；4) 指令微调对置信度校准的影响不一致，而非一致有害。

Conclusion: 研究结果为意图分类任务中开源语言模型的选择与评估提供了实用指导，揭示了模型规模、指令微调、基准饱和度等关键因素的实际影响。

Abstract: Intent classification is a core component of task-oriented dialogue systems, yet practitioners have limited systematic guidance for selecting deployable open-weight language models under compute, latency, and robustness constraints. We present a systematic zero-shot evaluation of 41 open-weight language models spanning 15 families and the 135M--9B parameter range across eight English single-label intent-classification datasets. A ninth dataset, ATIS, uses five labeled demonstrations and is reported as an auxiliary five-shot result. The evaluation includes standard benchmarks, a large-scale voice-assistant corpus, and production-derived e-commerce datasets. Beyond exact-match accuracy, we analyze confidence calibration, robustness to realistic input perturbations, statistical reliability of model rankings, deployment efficiency, and benchmark saturation. Our results show that instruction-tuned 3B models can outperform several evaluated 7B base models, that differences among leading models on MASSIVE are statistically indistinguishable under pairwise McNemar tests, and that widely used benchmarks such as SNIPS have become saturated and no longer meaningfully discriminate among current open-weight models. Instruction tuning's effect on confidence calibration is inconsistent rather than uniformly harmful. These findings provide practical guidance for selecting and evaluating open-weight language models for intent classification.

</details>


### [8] [SkillSmith: Learning to Compose Parametric Skills and Textual Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27497)
*Lucio M. Dery,Benedict Aaron Tjandra,Siavash Samiei,Adhiguna Kuncoro,Zohar Yahav,Jiajun Shen,Arthur Szlam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 SkillSmith，一种将模型权重作为额外模态融入大语言模型的框架，通过前缀调优实现指令引导的参数合成，直接输出新的前缀权重以掌握目标技能，显著优于纯文本或纯权重空间的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将文本知识组织与参数技能巩固视为正交任务，缺乏文本与模型权重的无缝集成，限制了针对性性能提升。

Method: 采用前缀调优实例化参数学习，增强大语言模型同时摄入前缀权重和丰富文本数据，构建 SkillSmith 进行指令引导的参数合成，直接输出新的前缀权重以体现目标技能。

Result: 实验表明该方法显著优于纯文本和纯权重空间基线，解锁了单模态适应无法达到的性能增益。

Conclusion: 通过将模型权重视为大语言模型可原生推理的额外模态，实现了文本与参数空间的深度融合，为智能体系统的自主技能习得提供了新范式。

Abstract: Agentic systems driven by large language models (LLMs) regularly feature two key mechanisms to autonomously solve complex problems: synthesizing text-based knowledge and procedures from past experiences and building parametric (weight-space) skill libraries for recurring sub-goals. To date, research has largely treated these as orthogonal pursuits: either organizing textual knowledge through composition and reflection, or consolidating parametric skills via weight-space merging. Consequently, the seamless integration of text and model weights for targeted performance improvements remains largely unexplored. This work bridges this modality gap by treating model weights as an additional modality that an LLM can natively reason over. We instantiate parametric learning via prefix-tuning and augment an LLM to ingest both prefix weights and rich textual data which capture relationships to a target capability. Our augmented LLM, which we call SkillSmith, synthesizes these inputs to perform instruction-steered parametric synthesis, directly outputting new prefix weights that manifest the target skill. We demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms both text-only and weight-space-only baselines, unlocking performance gains that are out of reach for uni-modal (text-only or weight-only) adaptations.

</details>


### [9] [Belief Coevolution in a Social Network of Generalist and Specialist Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27512)
*Germans Savcisens,Samantha Dies,Courtney Maynard,Tina Eliassi-Rad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CoevolveSim框架研究LLM群体中的信念扩散，发现领域专业化（微调专家LLM）比角色提示和网络结构更能显著改变群体共识，真实模拟多智能体信念动力学需要多样化的底层LLM，而非仅靠人设提示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）越来越多地部署在多智能体环境中，但相互作用的LLM之间信念如何形成和传播的过程仍知之甚少，需要系统性研究领域专业化、社会角色分配和社会网络结构对信念扩散的影响。

Method: 构建CoevolveSim框架，让通用型和专家型LLM智能体在网络中轮次交换并修正信念；开展1280次受控模拟，涵盖四种场景、两种网络结构和20条医疗指征陈述；对比全通用型LLM群体与异构LLM群体的集体动力学，并用基于坚持的观点动力学模型拟合结果。

Result: 人设风格的角色分配和网络结构重塑个体信念修正，但对群体层面共识影响微乎其微；引入微调专家LLM使共识偏移量翻倍以上，并产生一致的影响力不对称性；简单坚持模型能复现全通用型LLM群体的集体结果，但异构LLM群体需要群体级信念组合来复现共识、需要智能体身份来预测个体信念跃迁。

Conclusion: 在多智能体LLM系统中实现真实的信念扩散模拟，需要一组多样化的底层LLM（如领域微调的专家模型），仅靠提示词赋予人设是不够的。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in multi-agent environments. However, the processes by which beliefs form and propagate among interacting LLMs remain poorly understood. We introduce CoevolveSim, a framework for studying belief diffusion within networked LLM populations. CoevolveSim allows us to isolate and study three factors: domain specialization, social-role assignment, and social network structure. Within this framework, generalist and specialist LLM agents exchange and revise beliefs. In each round, an LLM agent observes a summary of its neighbors' beliefs before updating its own. We run 1,280 controlled simulations spanning four scenarios, two network structures, and 20 medical-indication statements. We find that persona-style role assignment and network structure reshape individual belief revision but have minimal effect on population-level consensus. In contrast, introducing (finetuned) specialist LLMs more than doubles the shift in consensus and gives rise to consistent asymmetries in exerted influence. We further show that simple persistence-based opinion-dynamics models reproduce collective outcomes in all-generalist LLM populations, whereas heterogeneous LLM populations require population-level belief composition to reproduce consensus and agent identity to predict individual belief transitions. Our results indicate that realistic simulation of belief diffusion in multi-agent LLM systems requires a diverse set of underlying LLMs, not persona prompting alone.

</details>


### [10] [Gradient-free Task-Conditioned Retrieval for On-Device In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27766)
*Xinyu Luo,Hui Liu,Yihua Shao,Junyi Yang,Arindam Basu,Haoliang Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CoRA是一个无梯度框架，将冻结编码器转化为任务条件检索器，通过配对的候选输入输出，利用闭式岭回归和低秩分解构建紧凑检索基础，实现端侧上下文学习的高效检索，无需微调、反向传播或目标模型调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端侧上下文学习（ICL）依赖预推理检索来选择演示，但检索必须在有限计算、内存和数据暴露预算下利用任务特定信息操作本地记忆。现有方法往往需要检索器微调或目标模型调用，不适合资源受限的端侧部署。

Method: 提出条件检索对齐（CoRA）：1）选择互补编码器层；2）从候选记忆构建输出衍生的条件空间；3）通过闭式岭回归将候选输入表示对齐到该空间；4）低秩分解生成紧凑检索基础，候选输出仅用于离线索引构建，查询时仅需查询输入和预计算索引；5）推导精确的双流式构建避免物化完整拟合矩阵；6）扩展至多模态示例检索，融合视觉表示到条件和检索空间。

Result: 在十个文本数据集和四个多模态基准上，使用Llama-3.2-1B、MobileLLM-Pro、OpenFlamingo-3B、Qwen3.5-2B等模型实验表明，CoRA支持有效的任务条件检索，无需检索器微调、反向传播或目标模型调用；树莓派5端到端部署验证了其实用性。

Conclusion: CoRA通过无梯度、低秩的条件检索对齐框架，解决了端侧ICL检索在资源受限下利用任务特定信息的问题，实现了高效、免微调的任务条件检索，并成功扩展至多模态场景，具有实际部署价值。

Abstract: On-device in-context learning (ICL) relies on pre-inference retrieval to select demonstrations for useful context before downstream model inference. This retrieval must exploit task-specific information while operating over local memories under limited computation, memory, and data-exposure budgets. We propose Conditional Retrieval Alignment (CoRA), a gradient-free framework that converts a frozen encoder into a task-conditioned retriever using paired candidate inputs and outputs. CoRA selects complementary encoder layers, constructs an output-derived conditioning space from candidate memory, and aligns candidate input representations to this space through closed-form ridge regression. Low-rank factorization then produces a compact retrieval basis where candidate outputs are used only during offline index construction, whereas query-time retrieval requires only the query input and precomputed index. We show that CoRA's rank-constrained basis is the optimal low-rank compression of the output-conditioned fitted representation, and derive an exact two-pass streaming construction that avoids materializing the full fitted matrix. We further extend the framework to multimodal exemplar retrieval by incorporating visual representations into the conditioning and retrieval spaces. Experiments across ten textual datasets and four multimodal benchmarks with Llama-3.2-1B, MobileLLM-Pro, OpenFlamingo-3B, and Qwen3.5-2B, as well as end-to-end Raspberry Pi~5 deployment demonstrate that CoRA supports effective task-conditioned retrieval without retriever fine-tuning, backpropagation, or target-model calls.

</details>


### [11] [Training Skills Like Parameters via Self-Supervised Semantic Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27557)
*Mo Li,Zixin Yin,Ting Cao,Yunxin Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种无监督自进化智能体框架，受扩散模型损坏-重构范式启发，利用高质量人类作品构建自监督信号，通过对比智能体重构与人类原文的差异，更新外部文本技能库而非模型权重，在短剧编剧任务上实现自主提取泛化技能，显著提升领域生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在专业开放领域（如创意编剧）表现不如人类专家；现有后训练方法需模型权限、算力大、技能绑定单一检查点且不可检视；智能体持续学习依赖昂贵专家标注或不可靠LLM评判反馈，存在瓶颈。

Method: 提出无监督自进化智能体框架：借鉴扩散模型损坏-重构范式，利用现有高质量人类作品构建自监督信号；训练循环遵循前向-损失-反向，损失来自对比智能体重构与人类原文；更新的不是模型权重而是外部文本技能库。

Result: 在短剧编剧任务实验中，该方法使智能体自主提取并内化高泛化技能，显著增强领域特定生成能力；自对比反思范式为智能体自学复杂高质量人类作品生产提供可扩展路径，无需外部监督。

Conclusion: 该框架通过自监督对比学习实现技能库自进化，解决了闭源模型无法微调、专家标注昂贵、技能不可检视等问题，为专业领域智能体自主学习提供新范式。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate remarkable general instruction-following capabilities, they often fall short of human experts in highly specialized, open-ended domains such as creative screenwriting. Prior approaches typically adopt post-training, yet both supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning require weight access that closed-source frontier models do not offer, and demand heavy compute. Moreover, what is learned is tied to a single checkpoint and cannot be inspected by humans. Recent advancements in agentic continual learning instead attempt to bridge this gap by accumulating external textual skills. However, these methods heavily rely on costly human expert annotations or unreliable LLM-as-a-judge feedback for reflection. To overcome this bottleneck, we propose a novel, unsupervised self-evolving agent framework inspired by the corruption-and-reconstruction paradigm of diffusion models. Instead of relying on explicit external scoring, we leverage existing high-quality human artifacts to construct self-supervised signals. Training then follows the familiar loop of neural network training, forward, loss, and backward, with the loss coming from contrasting the agent's reconstruction against the human original. What is updated is not model weights but an external library of textual skills. We evaluate our framework on the challenging task of short drama screenwriting. Experimental results demonstrate that our method enables the agent to autonomously extract and internalize highly generalizable skills, significantly enhancing its domain-specific generation capabilities. Furthermore, this self-contrastive reflection paradigm offers a scalable pathway for agents to teach themselves the production of complex, high-quality human artifacts, without requiring external supervision.

</details>


### [12] [EMBL AI Librarian: Life-Sciences Knowledge Layer for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28229)
*Luigi Sigillo,Matteo Silvestri,Francesco Tabaro,Rajat Bhatnagar,Syed Irtaza Mubashar,Matt Jeffryes,Daljit Nijjer,Vittorio Perera,Ola Spjuth,Julio Saez-Rodriguez,Melissa Harrison,Fabio Petroni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出EMBL AI Librarian，一个为AI智能体升级Europe PMC文献检索接口的知识层，支持自然语言查询并返回证据片段，显著提升生命科学领域智能体在文献综合、声明验证、开放域问答及生物学下游任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献资源（如Europe PMC）面向人类设计，需关键词和复杂语法，返回整篇论文，AI智能体需自行学习语法、多次检索并阅读全文以定位证据，效率低且门槛高。

Method: 构建单一LLM编排的知识检索流程：接收自然语言查询，规划互补子查询，调用Europe PMC实时搜索引擎执行，阅读筛选论文并定位相关证据片段返回给智能体。

Result: 在四个基准测试中表现优异：ScholarQABench上Citation F1提升16分以上；作为声明验证管道检索层，提高与专家共识一致性；LitQA2基准上GPT-5.4智能体得分比网页搜索高约8分。

Conclusion: 为生命科学智能体配备Librarian知识层可跨任务提升性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: The web is increasingly accessed by AI agents rather than humans. Every agent needs knowledge, especially in the life-sciences, where agentic pipelines are growing fast. Access to the literature is a crucial part of that need, and resources such as Europe PMC, with over 40M indexed records, are widely used to meet it. Yet these resources were not built for AI agents: they take keywords and complex syntax and return whole papers, so every agent must learn the syntax, issue several searches, and read full papers to find the evidence it needs. We introduce EMBL AI Librarian, a knowledge layer that upgrades the Europe PMC interface for AI agents: an agent asks in natural language and receives evidence that answers it. A single LLM orchestrates the whole knowledge retrieval process: it plans complementary subqueries executed by the live Europe PMC search engine, then reads the selected papers and locates the relevant evidence. We evaluate Librarian across four benchmarks: literature synthesis, claim verification, open-domain question answering, and downstream biology tasks such as protocol questions and sequence manipulation. On ScholarQABench, Librarian improves Citation F1 by more than $16$ points over strong recently published baselines. Used as the retrieval layer of an existing claim-verification pipeline, it increases agreement with expert consensus; and on the open-form LitQA2 benchmark, a GPT-5.4 agent scores about $8$ points higher when grounded in Librarian than with web search. Overall, our results show that equipping life-science agents with the Librarian knowledge layer improves performance across a range of tasks. We release our code publicly at https://github.com/petroni-lab/librarian

</details>


### [13] [AWARE-FX: An Auditable Knowledge-Guided AI System for Measuring Corporate Foreign-Exchange Hedging Disclosure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27611)
*Qi Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AWARE-FX 是一个可审计的 AI/NLP 决策支持系统，从企业年报文本中提取并量化外汇风险管理披露，结合专业词典、否定与会计状态逻辑、渠道特定编码器、精确证据门控、保守聚合和审计账本，经多维度验证，严格外汇评分与外汇风险敞口负相关，提供外部结构效度验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业年报包含弱结构化的外汇风险管理、衍生品使用、自然对冲和明确非使用等证据，缺乏可追溯、可审计的量化披露度量，需要构建决策支持系统将文本转化为可追踪的公司年度对冲披露指标。

Method: 开发 AWARE-FX 系统，融合专业来源词典、否定与会计状态逻辑、渠道特定金融编码器、精确证据门控、保守聚合和审计账本；在 2008-2025 年 24,909 家港股公司年度报告中检索评分 543,527 个片段；通过消融实验、分层 300 片段人工审计、三种子 FinBERT-ModernBERT 对比、严格 2023-2025 时间测试、概率校准、选择性预测及固定提示生成式模型基准评估可靠性。

Result: FinBERT 在八个编码器任务分割对比中七个平均 F1 更高，时间 F1 为 0.702-0.872；放弃 20% 最低置信度时间观测可使保留样本 F1 提升 0.050-0.077；Qwen3-8B 在商品与否定证据上表现强但外币债务和会计上下文标签较弱，表明通用大模型不能完全替代领域约束；严格外汇评分与基准及压力期外汇风险敞口负相关，而宽泛评分无显著相关，提供外部结构效度验证。

Conclusion: AWARE-FX 贡献了一个可审计的决策支持架构，其中检索、状态逻辑、分类、不确定性处理、聚合和外部验证均可独立审计，为企业外汇风险管理披露的量化分析提供了可靠工具。

Abstract: Corporate annual reports contain weakly structured evidence about foreign-exchange risk management, derivative use, natural hedging, and explicit non-use. This study develops AWARE-FX, an auditable AI/NLP decision-support system that converts report text into traceable firm-year hedging-disclosure measures. The system combines a professional-source lexicon, negation and accounting-status logic, channel-specific financial encoders, exact evidence gates, conservative aggregation, and an audit ledger. Across 24,909 Hong Kong firm-years from 2008-2025, it retrieves and scores 543,527 snippets. Reliability is evaluated through ablations, a stratified 300-snippet human audit, three-seed FinBERT-ModernBERT comparisons, strict 2023-2025 temporal tests, probability calibration, selective prediction, and fixed-prompt generative-model benchmarks. FinBERT has the higher mean F1 in seven of eight encoder task-split comparisons; its temporal F1 ranges from 0.702 to 0.872. Abstaining on the 20% least-confident temporal observations raises retained-sample F1 by 0.050-0.077. Deterministic Qwen3-8B performs strongly on commodity and negation evidence but poorly on foreign-debt and accounting-context labels, showing that a general-purpose LLM does not uniformly replace domain constraints. The strict FX score is negatively associated with linked baseline and stress-period FX exposure, whereas the generic broad score is not. These associations provide external construct validation, not causal estimates of hedging effectiveness. AWARE-FX contributes a tested decision-support architecture in which retrieval, status logic, classification, uncertainty handling, aggregation, and external validation remain separately auditable.

</details>


### [14] [AskChem: Claim-Centered Infrastructure for Chemistry Literature Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28618)
*Bing Yan,Gregory Wolfe,Stefano Martiniani,Kyunghyun Cho*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AskChem 是一个以声明为中心的跨论文化学搜索基础设施，将检索单元从论文转变为带有来源 DOI 和原文引用的原子化、类型化声明，建立包含 240 万声明的共享声明库，提供分面分类法、证据图谱和探索性活体分类法，实现 100% 可解析 DOI 的检索效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献检索系统主要返回排序后的文档列表，科学家和 AI 代理需手动定位、验证来源并组装跨论文答案，效率低下且难以追踪 provenance。

Method: 将每篇论文转化为带有 DOI 和逐字引用或显式证据定位器的原子化、类型化声明，构建共享声明存储，在此基础上建立稳定的分面分类法用于层级检索浏览、证据图谱关联声明关系、探索性活体分类法将论文置于科学原理下。

Result: AskChem 索引 240 万声明来自 14.7 万论文，提供 Web、REST、SDK、MCP 接口。在 AskChem-Bench 上，基于 AskChem 的 GPT-5.5 读取器实现 100% 可解析 DOI，优于无检索时的 88.3%，且在五个测试系统中引用密度最高。

Conclusion: AskChem 通过声明级检索和结构化知识组织，显著提升了跨论文化学信息检索的准确性、可追溯性和 AI 代理的可用性，已上线服务。

Abstract: Chemistry literature synthesis often requires assembling specific findings scattered across many publications, yet existing literature-search systems primarily return ranked document lists. As a result, scientists and AI agents need to locate relevant information, verify their provenance, and assemble cross-paper answers manually. We present AskChem, a claim-centered infrastructure for cross-paper chemistry search. AskChem changes the unit of retrieval from the paper to the provenance-carrying claim: each paper is converted into atomic, typed claims, each grounded by a source DOI and a verbatim quote or an explicit evidence locator. Over this shared claim store, AskChem exposes complementary structures for search and synthesis: a stabilized faceted taxonomy for hierarchical retrieval and browsing, an evidence graph linking claims through relations, and an exploratory living taxonomy that situates indexed papers under scientific principles. AskChem currently indexes 2.4M claims from 147K papers and provides a web interface, as well as REST, SDK, and MCP access for AI agents. On AskChem-Bench, grounding a GPT-5.5 reader in AskChem yields 100% resolvable DOIs, compared with 88.3% without retrieval, and the highest citation density among five tested systems. AskChem is live at https://askchem.org.

</details>


### [15] [DualAnchor: Preserving Language Priors and Improving Lexical Fidelity in Gloss-Free Sign Language Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27614)
*Hongbin Zhang,Junhao Liu,Xuefeng Bai,Youcheng Pan,Yang Xiang,Kehai Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 DualAnchor 框架，通过 Token-level Prior Anchoring (TPA) 保持 LLM 语言先验，结合 Optimal Transport Alignment (OTA) 实现视觉-文本软对齐，解决 LLM-based SLT 中的语言先验退化与词汇保真度缺失问题，在 PHOENIX-2014T 和 CSL-Daily 上取得 SOTA 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM-based SLT 方法虽利用 LLM 作为文本骨干，却破坏而非利用其语言先验，导致译文不流畅（语言先验退化）；同时句级对齐无法保证词级细节，产生词汇保真度鸿沟。需同时解决流畅度与细粒度词汇准确性。

Method: DualAnchor 为无 gloss 的 LLM-based SLT 训练框架，包含两个互补锚点：(1) Token-level Prior Anchoring (TPA)：在每一解码步，将多模态解码器的 next-token 分布正则化向冻结 LLM 在相同自回归前缀条件下的分布，保留语言先验。(2) Optimal Transport Alignment (OTA)：将视觉-文本匹配建模为熵正则化部分最优传输，利用 Sinkhorn 算法在余弦代价下诱导视觉 token 与文本内容 token 的软对齐，提升词汇保真度。

Result: 在 PHOENIX-2014T 和 CSL-Daily 基准上整体性能优异。消融分析表明：TPA 显著提升译文流畅度，OTA 有效降低细粒度词汇错误，两者互补作用带来整体增益。

Conclusion: DualAnchor 通过双锚点设计成功缓解语言先验退化与词汇保真度鸿沟，为 LLM-based SLT 提供了兼顾流畅性与视觉忠实性的有效训练范式。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have led sign language translation (SLT), the task of converting sign-language videos into spoken-language text, to increasingly adopt LLMs as textual backbones. However, despite their strong language modeling capabilities, existing LLM-based SLT methods often undermine rather than exploit this language prior, producing disfluent translations, a failure we term language-prior degradation. Meanwhile, existing methods typically align videos and text at the sentence level, which does not ensure accurate lexical details and creates a lexical fidelity gap. To address both issues, we propose DualAnchor, a gloss-free LLM-based SLT training framework that couples two complementary anchors for linguistically fluent and visually faithful generation. Token-level Prior Anchoring (TPA) preserves the LLM's language prior by regularizing the multimodal decoder at each decoding step toward the next-token distribution of a frozen LLM conditioned on the same autoregressive prefix. Optimal Transport Alignment (OTA) improves lexical fidelity by formulating visual-textual matching as entropy-regularized partial optimal transport, with Sinkhorn optimization inducing a soft alignment between visual tokens and textual content tokens under a cosine cost. DualAnchor achieves strong overall performance on both PHOENIX-2014T and CSL-Daily. Targeted analyses attribute these gains to the complementary effects of the two anchors: TPA improves fluency, whereas OTA reduces fine-grained lexical errors.

</details>


### [16] [Harness-G: A Graph-Structured Harness for Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27652)
*Yanning Hou,Haoyuan Chen,Sihang Zhou,Xiaoshu Chen,Xirui Liu,Duanyang Yuan,Lingyuan Meng,Quan Liu,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Harness-G框架，将检索重构为图结构下的有限动作选择，并引入结构化非近视信用分配，解决检索等价塌缩问题，在六个QA基准上超越Graph-R1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前RL搜索代理将检索建模为自由形式自然语言查询生成，存在检索别名现象：同一问题的多轮轨迹生成不同查询但证据集高度重叠，导致检索决策间缺乏有效对比信号，称为检索等价塌缩。

Method: Harness-G重新设计策略-环境接口：将自由查询生成改为有限动作选择（选择证据句/实体或回答），环境构建菜单、跟踪状态、验证执行；在此基础上引入结构化非近视信用(SNC)，利用冻结答案评分器比较所选动作与备选动作，将下游收益归因到早期启用动作。

Result: 在六个QA基准上，Harness-G在1.5B和3B两个规模模型上均取得最高平均F1，分别比最强基线Graph-R1高出10.74点和3.98点。

Conclusion: 通过图结构化检索接口和结构化信用分配，有效缓解检索等价塌缩，显著提升多轮检索问答性能，为RL检索代理提供了更 principled 的接口设计。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) search agents commonly model retrieval as free-form natural-language query generation and optimize multi-turn interactions using final-answer rewards. Current studies mainly improve training with denser or more structured credit signals, but rarely examine whether retrieval is properly formulated at the policy-environment interface. We observe pronounced retrieval aliasing during Search-R1 training: rollouts for the same question continue to generate distinct query strings, yet their accumulated evidence sets increasingly overlap. We call this phenomenon retrieval-equivalence collapse; in this regime, trajectories approach utility equivalence with respect to retrieval decisions, leaving within-group returns with little effective retrieval contrast. To address this problem, we propose Harness-G, a graph-structured retrieval framework that redesigns this interface. It reformulates free-form query generation as finite action selection: the policy selects an evidence sentence or entity, or chooses to answer, while the environment constructs the menu, tracks retrieval state, and validates and executes each choice. This interface reduces linguistic aliasing and makes same-state alternatives directly comparable. Building on this interface, we introduce Structured Non-myopic Credit (SNC), which uses a frozen answer scorer to compare the selected action with its alternatives and assigns downstream gains to the earlier actions that enabled them. Across six QA benchmarks, Harness-G achieves the highest average F1 at both evaluated model scales, outperforming the strongest baseline, Graph-R1, by 10.74 points at 1.5B and 3.98 points at 3B.

</details>


### [17] [Recall Before You Rank: Similarity-Guided Top-$K$ Reuse for Efficient Long-Context Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27692)
*Wenshuai Yao,Wenyong Zhou,Hanyong Shao,Yizhe Chen,Zhiyuan Ning,Yuannuo Feng,Ru Huang,Kechao Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ReTopK是一种无需训练的方法，通过复用历史检索决策加速动态Top-K稀疏注意力，在保持精度的同时显著降低长上下文解码的计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Top-K稀疏注意力虽减少Softmax和值聚合成本，但识别Top-K子集仍需对全KV缓存评分并进行全局Top-K选择，选择器成本随上下文长度线性增长，限制了长上下文解码的实际效率。

Method: 为每个注意力头维护有界的历史查询-支持对缓存；为新查询检索最相似的缓存查询，将其存储的支持集与近期窗口取并集，仅对该紧凑候选集使用当前查询的精确分数重排序；引入基于相似度的回退机制，在复用不可靠时调用全历史Exact Top-K；定期精确刷新以限制缓存漂移。仅复用选中的索引，保留完整KV缓存。

Result: 在16K-128K上下文长度下，ReTopK在评估的近似方法中实现最低PG19困惑度、最高NIAH和LongBench得分。在128K、K=512设置下，相对Exact Top-K困惑度仅增加0.50%，注意力计算加速3.07倍。

Conclusion: ReTopK通过历史检索决策复用有效解决了动态Top-K注意力的选择器瓶颈，在无需训练、保持完整KV缓存的前提下，为长上下文解码提供了高效且精度损失极小的稀疏注意力加速方案。

Abstract: Top-$K$ sparse attention reduces the cost of Softmax and value aggregation by attending to only a small subset of key--value (KV) entries. However, identifying this subset still requires scoring the current query against the full KV cache and performing global Top-$K$ selection, leaving selector cost linear in context length and limiting the practical efficiency of sparse attention for long-context decoding. In this paper, we introduce ReTopK, a training-free method that accelerates dynamic Top-$K$ attention by reusing historical retrieval decisions. ReTopK builds on the observation that similar queries often attend to overlapping supports and that partially overlapping supports can still preserve most of the Exact Top-$K$ attention mass. For each attention head, it maintains a bounded cache of historical query--support pairs, retrieves the most similar cached queries for each new query, unions their stored supports with a recent window, and reranks only the resulting compact candidate set using exact current-query scores. A similarity-based fallback invokes full-history Exact Top-$K$ when reuse is unreliable, while periodic exact refreshes limit cache drift. ReTopK retains the complete KV cache and reuses only selected indices, rather than historical scores, attention weights, or outputs. Across 16K--128K contexts, ReTopK achieves the lowest PG19 perplexity and the highest NIAH and LongBench scores among the evaluated approximate methods. At 128K with $K=512$, ReTopK incurs only a 0.50\% perplexity increase over Exact Top-$K$ while accelerating attention computation by $3.07\times$.

</details>


### [18] [ChronoMem: Version Control and Semantic Rollback for Large Language Model Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27773)
*Yongye Su,Wujiang Xu,Chaoji Zuo,Elisa Bertino*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ChronoMem 为 LLM 代理提供语义版本控制层，支持全内存快照、版本历史和自然语言回滚，解决前向演进导致的脆弱性，实验显示其在回滚一致性问答和历史摘要上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理记忆系统仅支持前向演进（累积、整合、覆盖），缺乏检查、版本化或回滚先前状态的机制，导致代理在纠正、概念漂移和记忆损坏时表现脆弱，尤其是已暴露于后续信息后。

Method: ChronoMem 在每次记忆写入时提交全内存快照，维护结构化版本历史；通过混合词法和语义检索、秩融合和重排序，将自然语言撤销意图映射到具体历史版本；引入后暴露评估协议测试回滚后的反事实行为。

Result: 在增强了演进记忆状态和回滚任务的长时程对话基准上，ChronoMem 在回滚一致性问答和历史摘要方面显著优于仅提示和仅检索基线，且在语义版本选择上表现强劲。

Conclusion: ChronoMem 是首个用于 LLM 代理系统性语义全局记忆回滚的开源系统和基准，有效解决了代理记忆的版本控制和回滚问题，提升了代理在修正和概念漂移下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly rely on long-term memory to support multi-session interaction and personalization. However, existing agent memory systems are designed around forward-only evolution, continuously accumulating, consolidating, and overwriting knowledge, with no principled mechanism to inspect, version, or revert prior states. This makes agents brittle under corrections, concept drift, and memory corruption, particularly after they have already been exposed to subsequent information. We present ChronoMem, a semantic version-control layer for agentic memory integrated into the production-ready, open-source Agent Development Kit by Google. ChronoMem commits whole-memory snapshots at each memory write, maintains structured version histories, and supports natural-language rollback requests by mapping undo intents to concrete historical versions through hybrid lexical and semantic retrieval, rank fusion, and reranking. We further introduce a post-exposure evaluation protocol that tests whether an agent can behave counterfactually after rollback by answering queries and summarizing history as if future updates had never occurred. On long-horizon conversational benchmarks augmented with evolving memory states and rollback tasks, ChronoMem substantially improves rollback-consistent question answering and history summarization relative to prompt-only and retrieval-only baselines, while achieving strong performance in semantic version selection. To our knowledge, ChronoMem is the first open-source system and benchmark for systematic semantic global memory rollback in LLM agents.

</details>


### [19] [Reasoning Consensus: Structural Ensembling of LLM Reasoning via Weighted DAG Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27783)
*Amruta Parulekar,Jinu Lee,Dilek Hakkani-Tür,Hari Sundaram*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于有向无环图（DAG）加权合并的多LLM推理结构集成框架，通过统计独立推理轨迹对每个步骤的证明频次来构建“共识推理图”，在六个基准测试上优于同预算多数投票基线，并提供可检视的推理结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM的思维链推理以非结构化文本形式呈现，用户难以判断哪些步骤有充分支撑、是否认真考虑了替代方案，以及最终结论与被丢弃路径的对比情况，亟需一种能显式展示并集成推理结构的方法。

Method: 从多个LLM的推理链中提取有向无环图（DAG），按独立轨迹对每个推理步骤的证明次数加权合并，生成共识推理图；并以此图指导最终答案选择，同时支持单模型下的自一致性对比。

Result: 在法条解释、研究生级科学、叙事多跳推理、一阶逻辑等六大基准上，集成方法比同预算多数投票最高提升3.1%（MuSR-MM）；单模型下匹配或超越自一致性；集成权重与LLM裁判的推理质量排序Spearman ρ为0.30-0.51；共识子图在五个数据集上以54.4%-65.4%胜率优于通向多数投票答案的替代路径。

Conclusion: 该框架成功将非结构化推理转化为可检视、可集成的结构化共识图，既提升了准确性，又提供了推理过程的透明度和多视角分析能力，为高风险任务下的LLM推理可信度奠定了基础。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) explore problems through chain-of-thought, but this exploration is buried in unstructured prose. On high-stakes tasks, users cannot tell which steps are well-supported, which alternatives were seriously considered, or how the final conclusion compares to those the model discarded. We propose a framework that ensembles the reasoning structure, not just the answers, of multiple LLMs by weighted merging of Directed Acyclic Graphs (DAGs) extracted from reasoning chains. We weight each step by how many traces independently attest to it, to return "Consensus Reasoning". Across six benchmarks spanning statutory interpretation, graduate-level science, narrative multi-hop reasoning, and first-order logic, our ensemble outperforms a matched-budget majority-vote baseline, with a maximum accuracy gain of 3.1% on MuSR-MM (narrative multi-hop reasoning). On a single model, the framework matches or exceeds self-consistency at the same trace budget while additionally exposing an inspectable consensus reasoning graph. Ensemble weights correlate with LLM-judge rankings of reasoning quality at Spearman $ρ= 0.30$-$0.51$, and consensus subgraphs are preferred over alternatives leading to the majority-vote answer in 54.4-65.4% of head-to-head comparisons across five of six datasets. We observe that our framework can also be used to analyze diverse reasoning perspectives for a problem.

</details>


### [20] [Semantic-Aligned Structural Abstraction for Multimodal Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27790)
*Wei Chen,Junkai Li,Tongguan Wang,Hui Liu,Feiyue Xue,Chuanxiang Ma,Ying Sha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 SentiLLM 框架，通过语义对齐的结构抽象将非语言模态转为文本式 token，引入双流显著性-上下文校准机制，分离焦点流与环境流，校准动态情感变化与背景状态，显著提升多模态情感分析性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 LLM 的多模态情感分析方法主要捕捉低级表层特征，难以建模由结构变化和上下文交互产生的情感语义，需要一种能将非语言模态映射到统一语义空间的方法。

Method: 设计语义对齐的结构抽象，将连续原始信号提炼为紧凑、语义化的 token；引入双流显著性-上下文校准机制：焦点流在文本先验引导下捕捉显著情感变化（如面部表情），环境流刻画稳定背景状态；通过校准这两者将非语言模态投影到统一语义空间，作为即插即用模块接入 LLM。

Result: 在 MOSI、MOSEI、CH-SIMS、CH-SIMS v2 四个数据集上均取得优于现有方法的性能，且仅需少量可训练参数。

Conclusion: 结构抽象范式在多模态情感分析中有效，SentiLLM 通过双流校准机制实现非语言模态与文本的语义对齐，为 LLM 理解复杂情感序列提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Multimodal Sentiment Analysis (MSA) aims to interpret complex human emotions by integrating natural language with non-verbal modalities. Non-verbal modalities share a structural isomorphism with natural language, as both can be viewed as feature sequences evolving over time. This isomorphism enables the transformation of non-verbal modalities into text-like tokens for unified semantic reasoning. Large Language Models (LLMs), designed to understand and generate sequential data, can thus be utilized to interpret complex affective sequences. However, existing LLM-based methods primarily capture low-level superficial features, failing to model affective semantics arising from structural variations and contextual interactions. To address this limitation, we propose \textbf{SentiLLM}, a unified framework that leverages \textit{Semantic-Aligned Structural Abstraction} to distill continuous raw signals into compact, semantically meaningful tokens. Specifically, we introduce a \textit{Dual-Stream Salience-Context Calibration Mechanism}, which disentangles non-verbal feature sequences into a focus stream and an ambient stream. The focus stream captures salient sentiment shifts (e.g., facial expressions) guided by textual priors, while the ambient stream characterizes stable background states. Through calibrating these dynamic sentiment shifts against background states, SentiLLM effectively projects non-verbal modalities into a unified semantic space, making them naturally understandable for LLMs. Serving as a plug-and-play module, SentiLLM significantly improves discriminative performance with only a small number of trainable parameters. Our method achieves superior performance on four datasets, MOSI, MOSEI, CH-SIMS, and CH-SIMS v2, demonstrating the effectiveness of the structural abstraction paradigm in MSA. Our code is available at: \href{https://github.com/especiallyW/SentiLLM}.

</details>


### [21] [Beyond Borrowed Histories: Person-Aligned User Simulation for Interactive Role-Playing Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27816)
*Yuhang Zhu,Mingxuan Du,Benfeng Xu,Jie Gao,Lingyun Yu,Hongtao Xie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出PALATE基准，用于评估角色扮演代理（RPA）。现有基准存在固定对话历史和固定评分标准的局限，无法科学评估多轮对话中的角色扮演能力，且用户体验因人而异。PALATE采用用户模拟器，在300个预冻结角色档案上进行自由多轮对话，结合通用质量评分和个性化评分，实现对特定用户-RPA对的可解释评估，而非单一用户无关排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RPA评估基准通常要求模型延续固定对话历史，并使用脱离用户的固定评分标准。这种设计有两个主要局限：1) RPA输出受前序对话历史影响，无法科学评估其在真实多轮设置中的角色扮演能力；2) 用户体验因人而异，固定评分标准不一定与用户满意度对齐。因此需要一种可扩展、个性化、基于用户模拟器的评估方法。

Method: 构建PALATE基准：包含300个角色档案池。训练五个个性化用户模拟器，让它们与候选RPA在预冻结角色档案上进行自由形式的多轮对话。采用通用质量评分标准和个性化评分标准（针对每个用户模拟器定制）来衡量用户满意度。在保留的标注数据上验证个性化评分标准与人类判断的一致性高于通用评分标准。对16个候选系统进行主评估，分别表征通用轮次质量、长程会话能力和每用户体验。

Result: 在16个候选RPA的主评估中，PALATE能够分别量化通用轮次质量、长程会话能力和每用户体验。个性化评分标准在保留的标注数据上表现出比通用评分标准更高的人类判断一致性。评估结果为特定用户-RPA对提供了可解释的评价，而非将系统压缩为单一用户无关的排名。

Conclusion: PALATE通过引入个性化用户模拟器和定制化评分标准，解决了现有RPA基准的局限，实现了在真实多轮对话设置中对角色扮演能力的科学评估，并能捕捉不同用户的差异化体验，为RPA系统的比较和改进提供了更细粒度、更符合用户满意度的评估框架。

Abstract: Role-playing agents (RPAs) have become one of the most important consumer applications of large language models. Users engage in multi-turn conversations with RPAs for experiences such as emotional comfort, making reliable evaluation essential for measuring capability, comparing systems, and guiding further improvement. Existing benchmarks, however, typically require an RPA to continue a fixed dialogue history and then evaluate the continuation using a fixed rubric detached from the user. We identify and empirically demonstrate two limitations of this design. First, an RPA's output is shaped by the preceding dialogue history, preventing a scientifically grounded assessment of its role-playing ability in real multi-turn settings. Second, user experience varies substantially across individuals, and conventional fixed rubrics need not align with user satisfaction. We therefore introduce PALATE (Person-Aligned LLM-Simulated-User Assessment with Tailored Evaluation), a scalable RPA benchmark built on user simulators. PALATE is accompanied by a pool of 300 character profiles. Its main evaluation trains five per-user simulators and lets them engage candidate RPAs in free-form, multi-turn conversations over a pre-frozen panel of character profiles. Alongside a general quality rubric, we construct personalized rubrics to measure user satisfaction; on held-out annotated data, the personalized rubrics show higher agreement with human judgments than the general rubric. In the main evaluation of 16 candidates, PALATE separately characterizes generic turn quality, long-horizon session capability, and per-user experience on multi-turn trajectories co-constructed by each candidate. It thereby produces interpretable evaluations of specific user-RPA pairs rather than compressing systems into a single user-independent ranking.

</details>


### [22] [AutoSupervision: Closing the Feedback Loop in Scientific Workflows with Grounded Revision Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27845)
*Haobo Li,Eunseo Jung,Wenxiao Zhao,Feng Liu,Jiong Wang,Kaiyi Xu,Zijie Guo,Zixin Chen,Ben Fei,Fenghua Ling,Lei Bai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AutoSupervision 利用透明同行评审记录作为监督信号，构建大规模数据集评估大语言模型验证论文修改是否真正回应审稿人意见的能力，实验显示模型擅长表征审稿关切但证据支持验证仍是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 AI 辅助科研工作流缺乏验证审稿人反馈是否导致实质性、有证据支持的论文改进的可靠机制，亟需自动化监督手段填补这一空白。

Method: 从 56,000 篇 Nature Communications 文章及对应评审记录构建 AutoSupervision 数据集，任务包含表征审稿人关切、判断关切是否已解决、定位修订稿中的支持证据；在多个 LLMs 上开展实验、消融研究与案例分析。

Result: LLMs 在表征审稿人关切上表现优异（GPT-5.5 得分 0.754），但在基于证据的验证任务上表现欠佳，最佳模型仅达 0.501，证据支持验证是核心瓶颈。

Conclusion: 自动化监督科学论文修改过程可行，但需显著提升模型对修订证据的细粒度理解与验证能力，才能实现可靠的 AI 辅助同行评审与科研工作流。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have enabled AI systems to assist scientific research and peer review. However, an essential capability for reliable AI-assisted scientific workflows remains underexplored: verifying whether reviewer feedback leads to meaningful and evidence-supported manuscript improvements. We introduce AutoSupervision, which evaluates whether scientific manuscript revisions genuinely address reviewer concerns through grounded evidence. AutoSupervision leverages transparent peer-review records as a natural source of supervision, where reviewer comments specify scientific concerns, author responses describe claimed resolutions, and revised manuscripts provide evidence of changes. Given reviewer comments, author responses, and revised manuscripts, models must characterize reviewer concerns, determine whether concerns have been addressed, and identify supporting manuscript evidence. We construct AutoSupervision from 56,000 Nature Communications articles and corresponding review records. Then we conducted experiments on LLMs, the ablation study, and the case study. Our results show that while LLMs perform well in characterizing reviewer concerns, with GPT-5.5 achieving a score of 0.754, evidence-based verification remains the primary bottleneck, with the best-performing model reaching only 0.501.

</details>


### [23] [Beyond Feeling Better: Capability-Sustaining Emotional Dialogue as a Longitudinal Research Paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27851)
*Ming Wang,Jiaqi Wu Young,Wenfang Wu,Daling Wang,Shi Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出能力维持型情感对话（CSED）作为纵向研究范式，旨在将支持策略与维持用户情绪调节、应对、自主决策和社会连接能力相一致，并通过文献与语料库审计发现现有系统多关注缓解导向目标，缺乏能力与纵向结果评估，发布审计协议并构建过程模型，推动CSED在数据、策略设计、训练、评估和治理中的可测试性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感对话研究主要关注共情对话和情感支持对话，但缺乏对长期使用中用户情绪调节、应对、自主决策和社会连接能力维持的关注；现有系统构建论文多追求缓解导向目标，未评估能力或纵向结果，也极少考虑依赖、自主性或终止风险。

Method: 采用PRISMA-ScR指导的文献审计（60篇系统构建论文）和语料库审计（300轮ESConv支持者回合），分析目标、能力评估、依赖/自主/终止风险考量，以及能力相关功能、通用建议、重评、自我效能支持、边界行为的分布；发布审计协议扩展至模型行为；构建说明性过程模型，将潜在用户能力与六项设计承诺、四个评估时间尺度及生命周期约束相连接。

Result: 95%的系统构建论文追求缓解导向目标，无论文评估能力或纵向结果，仅1篇考虑依赖/自主/终止风险；ESConv中能力相关功能占43.0%，通用建议22.0%，重评4.0%，自我效能支持6.7%，边界行为0.3%；提出CSED范式、审计协议及过程模型，形成可跨数据、策略设计、训练、评估和治理测试的议程。

Conclusion: CSED作为纵向研究范式，将支持策略与维持用户能力目标对齐，围绕重复使用、非使用、过渡和终止组织数据、模型、系统设计、评估和治理，通过审计揭示现有研究缺口，并提供可操作的协议和模型，推动情感对话系统从短期缓解转向长期能力维持。

Abstract: Emotional dialogue research includes two influential strategy traditions. Empathetic dialogue prioritizes understanding a speaker's emotional experience. Emotional support conversation selects and sequences support for the seeker's current needs. Sustained use introduces a further goal. Effective support should sustain users' capacities for emotion regulation, coping, self-endorsed decisions, and social connection across the interaction lifecycle. We propose capability-sustaining emotional dialogue (CSED) as a longitudinal research paradigm that aligns supportive strategy with this goal and organizes data, models, system design, evaluation, and governance around repeated use, non-use, transition, and termination. A targeted literature-and-corpus audit motivates this position. In a PRISMA-ScR-guided sample, 95% of 60 system-building papers pursue relief-oriented goals. None evaluates capability or longitudinal outcomes, and only 1 considers dependency, autonomy, or termination risk. In 300 ESConv supporter turns, capability-relevant functions appear in 43.0%, while generic suggestions account for 22.0%, compared with 4.0% reappraisal, 6.7% self-efficacy support, and 0.3% boundary behavior. We release a protocol for extending the audit to model behavior. An illustrative process model connects latent user capability to six design commitments, four evaluation timescales, and lifecycle constraints. The resulting agenda makes CSED testable across data, policy design, training, evaluation, and governance.

</details>


### [24] [FinanceHarness: Autonomous Financial Deep Research Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27853)
*Yijia Xiao,Rujun Han,Yanfei Chen,Zifeng Wang,Ke Jiang,Zhongying CuiZhu,Vishy Tirumalashetty,Wei Wang,Burak Gokturk,Tomas Pfister,Chen-Yu Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 FinanceHarness 和 FinanceGym，用于自动化金融深度研究和基准测试，显著提升开源模型在金融研究任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度研究系统多为通用报告，不足以满足金融研究需求；金融研究需要专业知识分析历史模式和预测事件，需分层驱动研究代理的工具链和可验证的时间点基准以防止未来信息泄露。

Method: 构建 FinanceHarness，包含面向金融的工具、实践者指导的工作流，自动化环境与数据构建、代理执行循环、奖励建模；提出 FinanceGym 基准，包含论文驱动的研究问题和结合预截止和后截止标准的评分细则，经专业专家验证。

Result: 专业专家验证通过率 82%；主流 LLM 和代理在细则上得分低于 40%，显示基准极具挑战性；使用相同开源骨干模型，FinanceHarness 将整体细则得分从 25.3% 提升至 32.4%。

Conclusion: FinanceHarness 和 FinanceGym 为金融深度研究自动化提供了完整工具链和基准，显著提升开源模型性能，但仍有巨大提升空间，未来工作可进一步优化代理能力和基准覆盖面。

Abstract: Powered by advances in LLMs and autonomous agents, deep research has become one of the most widely adopted agentic products. However, most deep research systems write general-purpose reports, which are inadequate for financial deep research. Financial research demands specialized knowledge to analyze historical patterns and forecast upcoming events. Automating financial deep research therefore requires both a layered harness to drive the research agent and a verifiable, point-in-time benchmark that prevents leakage of future information. We present FinanceHarness, a harness that runs finance-oriented tools and practitioner-guided workflows, automating financial deep research end to end: environment and data construction, the agent execution loop, and reward modeling. We further propose FinanceGym, comprising thesis-driven research questions and rubrics that combine pre-cutoff and post-cutoff criteria. Professional expert validation yields an 82% pass rate. Even leading LLMs and agents score below 40% on the rubrics, showing that FinanceGym is challenging and leaves substantial headroom. With the same open-weight backbone, FinanceHarness improves the overall rubric score from 25.3% to 32.4%. FinanceHarness is available at https://github.com/Yijia-Xiao/FinanceHarness.

</details>


### [25] [Memory Decoder at Scale: A Pretrained, Parametric Long-Term Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27919)
*Rubin Wei,Jiaqi Cao,Jiarui Wang,Junming Zhang,Qipeng Guo,Bowen Zhou,Zhouhan Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Memory Decoder at Scale，将记忆模型扩展至6.9B参数并在300B tokens上预训练，证明独立扩展预训练记忆比单独扩展基础模型更参数高效。通过分布式Faiss索引检索管道解决大规模数据下的检索瓶颈，实验表明：Pythia-410M配合6.9B记忆平均分从29.86提升至37.34，超越Pythia-12B且总参数减少39%；Qwen3 Base模型(0.6B-14B)配合1.7B领域记忆在三个领域平均分均提升9分以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Decoder-only语言模型将长期记忆和推理能力纠缠在同一参数集中，难以独立扩展记忆容量。先前的Memory Decoder仅在小规模下研究，缺乏大规模验证。

Method: 将Memory Decoder扩展至6.9B参数，在300B tokens上预训练。针对Faiss索引检索在大规模数据下的计算瓶颈，设计分布式Faiss索引与检索管道，配合稀疏批次式kNN分布加载。将记忆模块与不同规模的基础模型(Pythia-410M、Qwen3 Base 0.6B-14B)配对评估。

Result: 跨模型规模实验显示，分配更多参数给记忆模块比单独扩展基础模型获得更好的参数-性能权衡。Pythia-410M+6.9B记忆平均分37.34，超越Pythia-12B(37.24)且总参数少39%。Qwen3 Base各规模模型加1.7B领域记忆后，三领域平均分均提升9分以上。

Conclusion: 独立扩展预训练记忆模块提供了比单独扩展基础模型更参数高效的语言模型性能提升路径，记忆与推理的解耦设计在大规模下展现出显著优势。

Abstract: Decoder-only language models entangle long-term memory and reasoning in a single parameter set, making it difficult to scale memory capacity independently. Memory Decoder introduces a parametric long-term memory module but only studies it at a relatively small scale. In this work, we present Memory Decoder at Scale, scaling memory models up to 6.9B parameters and pretraining them on 300B tokens. At this data scale, the combined cost of indexing and search makes a standard Faiss pipeline infeasible. We address this bottleneck with a distributed pipeline for Faiss indexing and retrieval, together with sparse, batch-wise loading of kNN distributions. Across model scales, we find that allocating more parameters to memory yields a better parameter-performance tradeoff than scaling the base model alone. On 17 benchmarks, pairing a 6.9B general memory with Pythia-410M raises its average score from 29.86 to 37.34, surpassing Pythia-12B (37.24) with 39% fewer total parameters. For Qwen3 Base models ranging from 0.6B to 14B, 1.7B domain memories improve the average score across the three domains by more than 9 points at every scale. Overall, our results demonstrate that independently scaling pretrained memory offers a more parameter efficient path to improving language model performance.

</details>


### [26] [RepBench: Compiling Benchmarks into Capability Representations for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28008)
*Yanshi Li,Xueru Bai,Shuman Liu,Long Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RepBench 是一个基于基准测试的数据层，用于能力对齐的表示探测，通过多基准设计减少对单一来源的依赖，发布了可复用的闭环工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表示工程方法通常在特定论文的合成数据上评估，导致度量难以比较或复现，且可能反映表面模式而非真实能力。

Method: 爬取 13,427 篇基准论文构建 182 个能力聚类的分类法，收集 353 个公开基准数据集得到 46,149 个经审计的探测文本，覆盖 94 种能力，每种能力至少有两个独立基准支持；采用多基准池化设计，在 12 个模型上进行跨基准迁移评估，比较四种读出方法。

Result: 原始文本向量无自然聚类粒度，基准池化能力向量在所有 12 模型上显示内部聚类最优，与人工分类法一致性低；差分均值法在 10 个模型上达到最高模型级均值，逻辑回归在大多数能力-模型单元中获胜，表明读出方法和聚合标准是有意义的评估维度。

Conclusion: RepBench 提供了一个可复用的闭环工作流，包含流程、语料库和评估代码，为表示工程的标准化评估奠定基础。

Abstract: Representation engineering reads and steers capability directions in large language models, yet methods are typically evaluated on paper-specific synthetic data. The resulting measurements are difficult to compare or reproduce and may reflect surface patterns rather than capabilities. We present RepBench, a benchmark-grounded data layer for capability-aligned representation probing. Crawling 13,427 benchmark papers yields a taxonomy of 182 capability clusters in 13 families; harvesting 353 public benchmark datasets yields 46,149 audited probe texts covering 94 capabilities, each supported by at least two independent benchmarks. This multi-benchmark design reduces dependence on any single source: raw per-text vectors exhibit no natural cluster granularity, whereas benchmark-pooled capability vectors show an interior clustering optimum at a small number of clusters on all 12 evaluated models, with low agreement to the human taxonomy. Under cross-benchmark transfer evaluation across twelve models completed by all four readouts, difference-in-means attains the highest model-level mean on ten models, while logistic regression wins the most capability-model cells. This disagreement shows that the readout method and aggregation criterion are meaningful evaluation dimensions. The pipeline, corpus, and evaluation code are released as a reusable closed-loop workflow.

</details>


### [27] [LEEPS: Latent-Guided Explore-Exploit Prompt Sampling for Efficient RLVR in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28077)
*Shuang Liang,Haoyang Zhou,Yifan Gong,Guowei Wang,Xiting Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 LEEPS（Latent-Guided Explore–Exploit Prompt Sampler），一种基于潜在空间引导的探索-利用提示采样器，用于在可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）中自适应平衡对历史信息提示的复用与对不确定提示的探索，从而在不增加显著开销的情况下提升大语言模型的数学推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 RLVR 中，相同滚动奖励的提示组会消耗生成预算却不提供有效学习信号。现有的预滚动提示选择方法难以在利用（重复使用历史信息提示）与探索（尝试新提示）之间取得平衡：过度利用会缩小训练覆盖，过度探索会降低信息提示比例。因此需要一种自适应、低开销的方法来动态分配滚动预算并有针对性地探索高潜力提示。

Method: LEEPS 将候选提示划分为利用组合与探索组合，并根据近期非平凡奖励比例自适应分配滚动预算。它利用表示空间中的邻近提示及历史滚动结果，优先选择可能产生非零奖励方差的不确定提示，使探索更具针对性，且无需额外滚动。

Result: 在六个数学推理基准上，LEEPS 在 Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B 和 7B 两种规模下均取得最高平均分，相对最强基线分别提升 2.6% 和 3.7%，且训练过程收敛更快。在三个 OOD 通用推理基准上也达到两种规模下的最高平均分。在线采样每步仅增加约 2 秒开销。

Conclusion: LEEPS 通过潜在空间引导的自适应探索-利用策略，有效解决了 RLVR 中提示选择的预算浪费与覆盖权衡问题，在数学推理与泛化推理任务上均显著超越现有基线，且计算开销极低，为大模型推理能力训练提供了实用的高效方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves the reasoning capabilities of large language models, but prompt groups with identical rollout rewards consume generation budget without effective learning signals. Pre-rollout prompt selection can reduce this waste by screening prompts before rollout generation. However, existing pre-rollout methods struggle to balance exploitation and exploration: repeatedly exploiting historically informative prompts can narrow training coverage, whereas broader exploration can lower the fraction of informative prompts. To address these limitations, we introduce LEEPS, a Latent-Guided Explore--Exploit Prompt Sampler that adaptively balances the reuse of previously observed informative prompts with continued exploration of uncertain ones. LEEPS partitions candidates into exploit and explore portfolios and adaptively allocates rollout budget according to their recent non-trivial ratios. It further uses representation-space neighbors and historical rollout outcomes to prioritize uncertain prompts likely to yield non-zero reward variance, thereby making exploration more targeted without additional rollouts. Across six mathematical reasoning benchmarks, LEEPS achieves the highest average score at both model scales, with relative gains of 2.6\% and 3.7\% over the strongest baseline for Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B and 7B, respectively, and generally improves faster during the training process. It also achieves the highest average score across the three evaluated OOD general-reasoning benchmarks at both model scales and adds only about 2 seconds of online sampling overhead per training step. Code is available at https://github.com/ShuangLiangX/LEEPS.

</details>


### [28] [GGC: Selective Query Correction for Reliable Text-to-SPARQL Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28082)
*Ziyi Yang,Thanh-Son Nguyen,Tuan Anh Nguyen,Lihui Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Generator-Gate-Corrector (GGC) 框架，通过选择性修正机制提升大语言模型生成 SPARQL 查询的准确性、可靠性与效率。实验表明查询级准确率从 90.23% 提升至 98.33%，推理开销降低 45%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在 Text-to-SPARQL 任务中虽能生成可执行查询，但初始输出往往语义未对齐，导致检索错误；直接对所有查询进行修正会引入不必要的修改风险并增加开销。

Method: GGC 包含三个模块：Generator 生成初始查询，Gate 预测是否需要修正，Corrector 仅对高风险查询进行修正。这种选择性修正避免了对已正确查询的误改，并降低计算成本。

Result: 在 MCQA 数据集上，GGC 将查询级准确率从 90.23% 提升至 98.33%，相比全量修正基线推理开销降低 45%。消融实验显示 Gate 在不同阈值下鲁棒，Corrector 训练数据组成影响修正效果与稳定性。

Conclusion: 选择性修正策略能显著提升 LLM-based Text-to-SPARQL 的准确性、可靠性与效率，为知识图谱问答提供了更实用的生成范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in structured query generation, making them a natural choice for Text-to-SPARQL, which translates natural language questions into executable SPARQL queries over knowledge graphs. However, their initial outputs remain unreliable: generated queries may be executable yet semantically misaligned with input questions, leading to incorrect retrieval. To address this issue, we propose Generator-Gate-Corrector (GGC), a framework for reliable LLM-based Text-to-SPARQL generation. GGC first uses a Generator to produce an initial query, then applies a Gate to predict whether correction is needed, and finally invokes a Corrector only for selected high-risk queries. This selective correction mechanism avoids unnecessary modifications and reduces the risk of degrading originally correct queries. Experiments on MCQA show that GGC improves query-level accuracy from 90.23\% to 98.33\% while reducing inference overhead by 45\% compared with correcting all generated queries. Ablation studies show that the Gate is robust across thresholds and that Corrector training data composition affects correction effectiveness and stability. Overall, the results demonstrate that selective correction enhances the accuracy, reliability, and efficiency of LLM-based text-to-SPARQL generation.

</details>


### [29] [Challenges in annotations by humans and LLMs: A case study of evaluative language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28119)
*Mirela Imamovic,Aenne Cecilia Kristine Knierim,Khushi Pitroda,Ekaterina Lapshinova-Koltunski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文比较了训练中语言学家、训练有素语言学家和大语言模型（LLMs）在评价性语言标注任务中的表现，以英语TED演讲语料库为例，基于评价理论的态度子系统（情感、判断、鉴赏）进行分析。结果显示LLMs在与专家标注一致性方面表现最佳，而训练中语言学家一致性较低；微调模型达到F1分数0.77，表明LLMs可辅助复杂标注任务，为数字人文研究开辟新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究训练中语言学家、资深语言学家和大语言模型在处理复杂语言现象（如评价理论标注）时是否面临相似困难，以评价理论为例——这是一种高度主观的标注任务，适合研究复杂标注挑战。

Method: 选取英语TED演讲语料库作为口语科普话语样本，基于评价理论的态度子系统（情感、判断、鉴赏三类）进行句级标注。先评估人工标注一致性，再设计三种提示词比较LLMs自动分类性能，选用最佳提示词评估三个LLMs，并对模型进行微调。

Result: 微调后模型达到F1分数0.77。LLMs与资深语言学家标注的一致性最高，而训练中语言学家间一致性得分较低，未达到高度一致。

Conclusion: 大语言模型能够辅助解决复杂标注任务，为数字人文领域中复杂理论的标注与分析开辟了新途径。

Abstract: In this paper, we draw a comparison between linguists in training, a trained linguist, and annotations generated by large language models (LLMs) to find out if they struggle with complex linguistic phenomena in a similar way. For this purpose, we analyse evaluative language in spoken popular science discourse, with the example of a corpus of English TED talk transcripts. We focus on the Appraisal theory and its Attitude subsystem, including the categories (classes) of Affect, Judgement, and Appreciation. In this context, Appraisal theory is an example of a highly subjective annotation task, making it a suitable example for the study of complex annotation challenges. First, we assess human annotations on a sentence level in specific scientific domains. Then, we develop three prompts and compare them for model performance for the automatic classification of Appraisal classes. We assess the performance of three LLMs using the best-performing prompt and finetune the model, reaching an F1-score of 0.77. We find that models perform best compared to annotations conducted by the trained linguist, while linguists in training do not reach high agreement scores. We conclude that LLMs can aid in complex annotation task resolution, opening new pathways for the complex theories annotated and analyzed in digital humanities studies.

</details>


### [30] [FinSMART: Financial Sentiment Analysis for Algorithmic Trading through Market-Aligned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28127)
*Giorgos Iacovides,Wuyang Zhou,Danilo Mandic*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FinSMART 是首个面向金融情感分析的市场对齐强化学习框架，直接利用实现的市场结果优化情感信号，显著超越现有最优基线，并支持基于新观测金融文章及其实际市场结果的持续再训练，实现模型对不断变化市场动态的自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融情感分析方法局限于市场无关的监督学习范式，依赖有限、静态且人工标注的数据集，无法适应不断演变的市场条件。

Method: 提出 FinSMART 框架，包含信号提取管道，结合市场感知数据过滤与离散不对称交易奖励，利用强化学习直接从经济意义明确的市场反馈中优化情感信号。

Result: 实验表明 FinSMART 在盈利能力、风险调整后表现和情感信号质量上显著优于最先进方法，累计交易收益较最强基线提升 220%。框架天然支持市场感知再训练，可随时用新观测金融文章及其已实现市场结果替代昂贵人工标注，持续适应市场动态并带来稳定性能增益。

Conclusion: 研究证明了市场对齐强化学习的实际适用性，并凸显其作为开发自适应金融大语言模型下一代范式的潜力。

Abstract: Recent advances in Generative AI have substantially improved financial sentiment analysis through post-trained financial large language models (LLMs). However, existing approaches remain confined to a market-agnostic, supervised learning paradigm that relies on limited, static and human-annotated datasets, and thus are incapable of adapting to evolving market conditions. To address this limitation, we introduce FinSMART, the first market-aligned reinforcement learning framework for financial sentiment analysis, which directly optimizes sentiment signals using realized market outcomes. To deal with the noisy, non-stationary, and multifactorial nature of financial markets, FinSMART incorporates a signal extraction pipeline that combines market-aware data filtering with a discrete asymmetric trading reward, enabling stable reinforcement learning from economically meaningful market feedback. Experimental results demonstrate that FinSMART significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods in profitability, risk-adjusted performance, and sentiment signal quality, improving cumulative trading returns by 220% over the strongest baseline. Uniquely, the FinSMART framework naturally supports market-aware retraining, at any point in time, by replacing costly manual annotation with newly observed financial articles and their realized market outcomes. Such a retraining strategy enables the model to continuously adapt to changing market dynamics, resulting in consistent performance gains over its static counterpart. These findings demonstrate the practical applicability of market-aligned reinforcement learning and highlight its potential as a next-generation paradigm for developing adaptive financial LLMs.

</details>


### [31] [Rethinking LLM-Judged Helpfulness as a Pedagogy Signal: A Pre-Registered Audit Across Tutor Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28128)
*Shuyi Fan,Boyuan Deng,Mengyu Xu,Jiale Liu,Hongyang Zhang,Qiaoxin Yang,Chongyang Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM辅导中通用有用性评分是否能区分直接给答案与教学引导，发现有用性评分不可靠，教学评分需配合确定性过程指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决LLM辅导中的测量问题：通用有用性评分标准能否区分直接给答案和教学引导。

Method: 预注册研究，在三个导师基础模型中比较对话策略和教学策略，使用相同底层模型和固定弱模拟学生。确定性检测器测量答案泄露和下一轮独立作业。Claude Opus 4.8为冻结的、盲条件主要评判者。之后GPT-5.6 Sol对相同1179个确认性回答阶段导师轮次进行事后稳健性审计，使用冻结的有用性和教学评分标准。

Result: 在Opus主要基础模型上，策略在有用性上无显著差异，但在教学评分上完全等级分离（Cliff's |δ| = 0.10 vs 1.0）。跨两个评判者，教学对比保持方向，而有用性排序依赖评判者，在三个基础中的两个上反转。Opus单独消融实验中，七个主要基础策略在平均教学评分跨度2.3分，而平均有用性评分仅在0.25分带内。揭示答案的轮次在所有基础上后续学生独立作业减少，该结果因构造而评判者不变。

Conclusion: 在受控设置中，通用有用性不是可靠的教学信号。导师评估应将教学导向的评分标准与确定性过程测量相结合。

Abstract: LLM tutoring poses a measurement problem: can a general-purpose helpfulness rubric distinguish direct answer-giving from pedagogical guidance? We audit this signal in a pre-registered study. Within each of three tutor bases, we compare conversational and pedagogical policies instantiated with the same underlying model and paired with one fixed weak simulated student. Deterministic detectors measure answer leakage and next-turn independent work. Claude Opus 4.8 is the frozen, condition-blind primary judge. After the Opus scores were fixed, GPT-5.6 Sol was prospectively specified for a post hoc robustness audit of the same 1,179 confirmatory answer-phase tutor turns under the frozen helpfulness and pedagogy rubrics. On the primary base under Opus, the policies do not differ significantly in helpfulness but are perfectly rank-separated under the pedagogy rubric (Cliff's $|δ|{=}0.10$ vs. $1.0$). Across the two judges, pedagogy contrasts retain their direction where detected, whereas the helpfulness ordering is judge-contingent, reversing between judges on two of three bases. In an Opus-only ablation, seven primary-base policies span $2.3$ points in mean judged pedagogy within a $0.25$-point band of mean judged helpfulness. Separately, answer-revealing turns are followed by less independent student work on every base, a result that is judge-invariant by construction. In this controlled setting, general-purpose helpfulness is not a reliable pedagogy signal. Tutor evaluation should pair pedagogy-targeted rubrics with deterministic process measures.

</details>


### [32] [Can Agents Deceive? Evaluating Reasoning and Deception in ParliamentBench using a Social Deduction Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28146)
*Niklas Bauer,Lars Benedikt Kaesberg,Akiko Aizawa,Jan Philip Wahle,Bela Gipp,Terry Ruas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出开源基准框架 ParliamentBench，基于游戏 Secret Hitler 评估大语言模型在欺骗、说服和信息不对称下的推理能力。实验测试 16 个 LLM，共 1600 局模拟对战，引入三个新指标量化社会推演、推理和欺骗一致性。结果显示前沿模型在合作与欺骗角色均表现强劲，形成四强集群（GPT-5.4、Kimi K2.5、Grok 4.1 Fast、DeepSeek 3.1 Terminus），弱模型低于随机和简单算法基线。多数 LLM 难以在整局游戏中保持一致的欺骗人设，欺骗保持率低于 50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型被部署为医疗、法律等高风险场景中的智能体，理解其欺骗能力成为安全研究的基础。受控社交推演游戏提供了可复现的代理环境，能隔离并评估这些复杂的对抗行为。

Method: 基于桌游 Secret Hitler 构建开源基准 ParliamentBench，设计三个新指标分别度量社会推演、推理和欺骗一致性。选取 16 个 LLM 进行 1600 局模拟对战，包括模型间自博弈、模型对战人类，并与大规模在线人类对局数据对比。

Result: 前沿模型在合作与欺骗角色上均达到强性能，形成显著的四强集群；最弱模型胜率低于随机基线（33%）和简单算法基线（45%）。大多数 LLM 难以维持全程一致的欺骗人设，欺骗保持率跌破 50%。

Conclusion: ParliamentBench 为评估 LLM 在信息不对称下的欺骗、说服与推理能力提供了标准化、可复现的基准。实验揭示了当前顶尖模型已具备较强的对抗性社交能力，但欺骗一致性仍是普遍短板，提示在高风险部署中需进一步加强对欺骗行为的监控与缓解。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are deployed as agents in high-stakes settings, such as medical and legal systems, understanding their deceptive capabilities is fundamental to safety. Controlled social deduction games provide a reproducible proxy for isolating and evaluating these complex adversarial behaviors. We present the open-source benchmark framework ParliamentBench based on the game Secret Hitler to evaluate LLMs in scenarios that require deception, persuasion, and reasoning under information asymmetry. We evaluate 16 LLMs across 1,600 simulated matches playing each other, playing against humans, and compare them against a large set of online games. We introduce three novel metrics that isolate social deduction, reasoning, and deceptive consistency. Our experiments reveal that frontier models achieve strong performance across cooperative and deceptive roles, with a strong top-four cluster (GPT-5.4, Kimi K2.5, Grok 4.1 Fast, and DeepSeek 3.1 Terminus), whereas the weakest models fall short of random (33%) and simple algorithmic (45%) baselines. Most LLMs struggle to maintain a consistent deceptive persona throughout an entire game, with deception retention dropping below 50%.

</details>


### [33] [RRM: Experience-Driven Reflective Retrieval Memory for Long-Horizon Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28156)
*Jingxiang Fan,Junbao Zhuo,Bochao Zou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出反思检索记忆（RRM）框架，通过引入反思经验记忆从历史任务轨迹中提炼可迁移的程序性检索知识，解决现有多模态长期记忆代理在长视频推理中检索不准确且缺乏失败诊断与策略适应机制的问题，在多个基准上超越现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态长期记忆代理主要关注存储内容而非检索方式，当检索不准确或反复失败时，缺乏从历史任务轨迹诊断失败并调整未来搜索策略的机制，限制了长时程多模态推理的效果。

Method: RRM在实体中心的多模态记忆图中增设反思经验记忆，从历史任务轨迹中提炼可跨任务复用的程序性检索策略；将检索到的经验转化为查询级指导，答案生成仅依赖当前视频新检索的事实证据；引入生命周期管理机制，基于使用频率、复用反馈和时间衰减调控经验记忆，降低冗余与噪声。

Result: RRM在M3-Bench-Robot、M3-Bench-Web和Video-MME-Long三个基准上均稳定超越此前的最先进方法，验证了反思检索记忆对长时程多模态推理的有效性。

Conclusion: 反思检索记忆通过学习可迁移的检索策略而非仅存储事实证据，显著提升了多模态代理在长视频理解任务中的检索准确性和推理能力，为长时程多模态推理提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Existing multimodal long-term memory agents use external memory to overcome the limited context available for long videos. However, most methods emphasize what to store rather than how stored memory should be retrieved. When retrieval becomes inaccurate or repeatedly fails to obtain useful evidence, existing agents lack mechanisms to diagnose failures from previous task trajectories and adapt future search strategies.We introduce Reflective Retrieval Memory (RRM), a reflective memory framework for long-horizon multimodal reasoning. RRM augments an entity-centric multimodal memory graph with reflective experience memory, which distills transferable procedural retrieval knowledge from historical task trajectories. Unlike episodic and semantic memories that preserve factual evidence from the current video, reflective experience memory captures reusable search strategies across tasks. RRM converts retrieved experiences into query-level guidance, while answer generation remains conditioned only on factual evidence newly retrieved from the current video. A lifecycle management mechanism further regulates experience memory through usage frequency, reuse feedback, and temporal decay, thereby reducing redundancy and noise. RRM consistently outperforms previous state-of-the-art approaches on M3-Bench-Robot, M3-Bench-Web, and Video-MME-Long, demonstrating the effectiveness of reflective retrieval memory for long-horizon multimodal reasoning.

</details>


### [34] [Where and When to Commit: Candidate-Aware Decoding for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28166)
*Chia-Ming Lee,Ming-Ching Chang,Xin Li,Yu-Lun Liu,Chih-Chung Hsu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LATCH是一个无需训练的、候选感知的早期退出框架，用于扩散语言模型，通过置信度验证提交(CVC)和块级早期提交(BWEC)分离停止决策与加速决策，在保持准确率的同时实现显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有早期退出门控依赖固定区域置信度统计或调度依赖规则，证据过于粗糙，导致在长链式思维输出中过早退出；自适应采样虽能控制提交速度但不验证输出稳定性。需要一种能区分停止决策与加速决策、且无需后缀提示构建的方法。

Method: 提出LATCH框架，包含两个组件：1) Confidence-Verified Commit (CVC)：通过确定性解析器动态提取候选跨度，验证置信度和持续argmax稳定性来决定全局终止；2) Block-Wise Early Commit (BWEC)：对非最终块应用更廉价的局部规则加速，最终块和全局终止仍由CVC控制。该方法无需后缀提示构建，无提示锚点但感知格式。

Result: 在11个任务零样本设置下，使用LLaDA和Dream评估，LATCH在22个评估设置中准确率均在完整解码准确率2.0个百分点以内，单一冻结超参数集跨主干网络无需调优即可迁移。短答案任务端到端TPS加速9.3-17.8倍，长推理任务加速2.0-3.3倍。

Conclusion: LATCH通过分离停止决策与加速决策，利用候选感知的置信度验证和块级局部加速，在无需训练和后缀提示的情况下，实现了扩散语言模型的高效早期退出，显著提升推理速度同时保持高准确率，且超参数跨模型迁移性强。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) expose a provisional prediction at every denoising step, creating an opportunity for generation-time early exit that stops decoding before the schedule is exhausted. Existing early-exit gates decide termination from fixed-region confidence statistics or schedule-dependent rules, evidence too coarse for a decision that freezes every remaining position at once, so they fire prematurely on long chain-of-thought outputs whose answers stabilize only near the end.
