Adaptive sampling, the other axis of training-free acceleration, paces how quickly positions commit while decoding continues but never verifies that the output itself has stabilized. We introduce a training-free, candidate-aware early-exit framework that keeps the two axes separate and matches each decision to evidence of its own scope. Confidence-Verified Commit (CVC) governs when the sequence may stop by verifying confidence and sustained argmax stability over the dynamically extracted candidate span using a deterministic parser specified from each task's output format. Block-Wise Early Commit (BWEC) governs where to accelerate by applying a cheaper local rule to non-final blocks, while leaving the final block and global termination under CVC. We refer to their combination as LATCH (Localized Acceleration with Tracked-Candidate Halting). Unlike prior methods, LATCH needs no suffix-prompt construction; it is prompt-anchor-free but format-aware. We evaluate LATCH end to end on 11 tasks under zero-shot settings using LLaDA and Dream. LATCH stays within 2.0 percentage points of full-decoding accuracy across all 22 evaluation settings, with one frozen hyperparameter set that transfers cross-backbone untuned, while achieving end-to-end TPS speedups of 9.3-17.8x on short-answer tasks and 2.0-3.3x on long-reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [35] [The MADRS Pipeline: Supporting Depression Assessment in Clinical Trials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28190)
*Mila Fodor,Katalin Ócsai,Francesco Periti,Rien Sonck,Alex Boudreau*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个LLM流程，用于支持临床试验中抑郁症患者的MADRS量表评估，将音频访谈转为文本、映射到10个症状项目、估计严重程度并识别有问题的评分，在真实临床访谈上与专家评分相关性达0.867。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化抑郁症检测方法主要针对在线文本/社交媒体，缺乏对临床试验中基于SIGMA等标准指南的结构化访谈评估的支持，需要专门针对临床试验场景的MADRS量表评估辅助工具。

Method: 开发LLM流程：1) 将音频访谈转录为文本；2) 将文本映射到MADRS量表的10个症状项目；3) 估计每个症状的严重程度；4) 识别与之相关的有问题的临床评分。

Result: 在真实临床访谈上评估，与专家评分的总体相关性为0.867，提供可解释的支持。

Conclusion: 该LLM流程能有效支持临床试验中抑郁症患者的MADRS量表评估，具有较高的专家评分一致性和可解释性，为未来临床试验评估提供可行方案。

Abstract: Depression is a major mental disorder for which diagnosis relies primarily on clinical assessments. Automated methods to support its detection via the psychiatric MADRS scale are getting more and more attention. While existing solutions primarily focus on detecting the disorder from different text sources (e.g., online text, social media), there is still limited support for clinical trials, where clinical assessments are conducted through structured interviews based on standard guidelines such as SIGMA. In this work, we develop a LLM pipeline specifically designed to support clinicians in supporting the assessment of depression in patients enrolled in clinical trials. Our pipeline converts audio interviews into transcripts, maps them into the ten MADRS symptom items, estimates their severity, and identify problematic clinical ratings associated with them. Evaluation on real clinical interviews shows a strong overall correlation of 0.867 with expert ratings, providing interpretable support for future assessments in clinical trials.

</details>


### [36] [Fidelity Is Not Safety: Gently-Compressed LLMs Pass Every Data-Free Quality Guard Yet Invent Procedure Steps in Agentic Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28196)
*I. Kennedy,T. Kennedy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了压缩语言模型通过标准质量守卫（困惑度、MMLU、数据自由保真度探针）后，在作为智能体执行标准操作程序（SOP）时会产生幻觉步骤的盲点。该现象具有算子特异性：相干低秩（SVD）截断会导致，而同等困惑度的幅度剪枝不会。核心机制是压缩误差的相干度与误差率的乘积，而非损伤幅度。作者提出了一种数据自由的双轴统计筛选指标（相干分数与误差率），可跨架构固定阈值识别风险构建。结论强调：困惑度、MMLU 和保真度接受不能认证智能体安全性，部署前需筛查温和压缩的低秩构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前压缩语言模型的评估依赖困惑度、下游任务准确率（如 MMLU）和数据自由输出保真度探针等质量守卫，但这些守卫存在盲点：通过所有守卫的模型在智能体场景下执行 SOP 时会捏造步骤，带来安全隐患。研究旨在揭示该盲点的成因、算子特异性及可靠的数据自由筛选方法。

Method: 跨三个模型家族（架构）进行实验，使用配对置信区间和预注册的统计功效金丝雀测试。对比相干低秩（SVD）截断与幅度剪枝在相同困惑度下的表现，分离压缩误差的相干度与误差率乘积作为支配轴。构建双轴统计量（相干分数、误差率）作为数据自由筛选指标，在固定阈值下跨架构验证。

Result: 1. 温和压缩模型通过所有质量守卫，但作为智能体执行 SOP 时捏造步骤。2. 效应算子特异：SVD 截断诱发，幅度剪枝不诱发。3. 同一压缩权重通过输出保真度测试却在捏造步骤金丝雀测试中失败。4. 支配轴为压缩误差的相干度×误差率，损伤幅度无预测力。5. 提出的双轴统计筛选指标以固定阈值跨架构标记失败构建，匹配相干度×误差率机制。

Conclusion: 困惑度、MMLU 和数据自由保真度接受不能认证智能体部署安全性。在智能体部署前，必须对温和压缩的低秩构建进行双轴统计筛选（相干分数与误差率），以规避 SOP 执行中的幻觉步骤风险。

Abstract: Practitioners accept a compressed language model once it clears a stack of data-cheap quality guards: perplexity within a small factor of the original, downstream accuracy (for example MMLU) inside a confidence interval, and data-free output-fidelity signals that compare the compressed and original network's internal representations under random probe inputs. This stack has a blind spot. Across three model families, gently-compressed models clear every guard and then invent procedure steps that were never in the instructions when they run a standard operating procedure (SOP) as an agent. The effect is operator-specific: coherent low-rank (SVD) truncation induces it, and magnitude pruning matched to the same perplexity does not. One dissociation isolates the cause. The same compressed weights that CI-win a paired output-fidelity test CI-fail the invented-step canary. The governing axis is the coherence of the compression error times its rate; the magnitude of the damage does not predict it. The data-free fidelity probe is a fidelity oracle by construction, so it cannot see this axis. We characterize the blindspot and dissociation with paired confidence intervals on a pre-registered, powered canary across three architectures. Operator-specificity replicates on all three, and the perplexity-guard evasion appears where the model admits in-guard low-rank headroom. We then give a data-free screen: a two-axis statistic of the compression error (coherent-fraction and error-rate) that flags the failing builds with fixed thresholds across architectures and matches the coherence-times-rate mechanism. Perplexity, MMLU, and fidelity acceptance do not certify agent safety. Screen gently-compressed low-rank builds before agentic deployment

</details>


### [37] [Causal Discovery with Inverted Self-attention for Multivariate Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28212)
*Yusen Liu,Yong Wang,Yifan Yin,Tianqing Zhu,Xiufeng Liu,Huan Huo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出基于Transformer自注意力机制的因果发现框架，通过反向因果自注意力机制(CSAM)捕捉潜在和间接因果关系，结合全局因果算法和因果验证模块，在线性和非线性多变量时间序列数据上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以捕捉多变量时间序列中复杂的交互、高维度和非线性依赖，导致因果结构不准确。

Method: 提出新框架，利用Transformer自注意力机制；引入反向因果自注意力机制(CSAM)，通过反转token并诱导注意力分数稀疏性来强调潜在和间接因果关系；开发全局因果算法识别全局因果链接，提供整体因果影响度量；增加因果验证模块确保识别因果关系的鲁棒性。

Result: 在线性和非线性数据集上的实验、消融研究和敏感性分析显示，该框架优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 该框架在复杂多变量时间序列因果发现中展现出潜力。

Abstract: Causal discovery in multivariate time series data is challenging due to complex interactions, high dimensionality, and nonlinear dependencies among variables. Existing methods often struggle to capture these complexities, resulting in inaccurate causal structures. To address this issue, we propose a novel framework that leverages self-attention mechanisms within the transformer architecture for causal discovery. Our approach introduces a novel inverted causal self-attention mechanism (CSAM) that emphasizes latent and indirect causal relationships by inverting tokens and inducing sparsity in attention scores, focusing on significant causal interactions and reducing spurious correlations. Additionally, we develop a global causal algorithm to identify global causal links, providing a holistic metric for causal influence, along with a causal verification module to ensure robustness in the identified causal relationships, enhancing the reliability of our framework. Experiments on both linear and nonlinear datasets, along with ablation studies and sensitivity analyses, show that our framework outperforms existing methods, demonstrating its potential for causal discovery in complex multivariate time series.

</details>


### [38] [Understanding Is Done Early: A Depth Division of Labor in Large Language Models and Its Use for Unbounded-Context Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28263)
*Hanzuo Liu,Xuan Qi,Chunyu Liu,Haotian Zhong,Yulong Wang,Rayying,Key,Alex Lamb,Mingyu Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出 CoMem（理解记忆），一种沿层轴而非仅沿 token 轴组织长上下文记忆的方法：将上下文块仅写入中间层，检索固定数量的缓存残差状态，并重新计算查询条件的上层。在冻结 Qwen3-8B 骨干网络、仅用 rank-32 LoRA 自蒸馏于 PG19 继续训练的设定下，CoMem 在 RULER 达 97.05、LoCoMo 38.27，显著优于全上下文 KV-Direct（34.59）；在 128k 长度下显存从 89.36 GB 降至 18.26 GB，prefill 加速 7.83 倍。受控深度实验表明更深层缓存降低单查询重算但引入保真度损失，自蒸馏可大幅修复。结果证明长上下文记忆可沿层轴高效组织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer 的层深分工不均匀：低/中层构建语义表征，高层逐渐为预测特化。现有长上下文方法主要在 token 轴扩展 KV 缓存，导致显存与计算随上下文长度线性增长。论文旨在利用层间分工特性，将上下文压缩写入中间层、仅在高层按需重算，从而使读取计算与显存与存储上下文长度解耦，实现有界检索预算下的高效长上下文建模。

Method: CoMem（Comprehension Memory）：1) 选择一个中间层作为写入层，将每个上下文块仅前向通过该层并缓存其残差状态；2) 推理时按固定预算检索固定数量的缓存残差打包；3) 仅对打包后的残差重新计算查询条件的上层网络得到预测。骨干网络冻结，仅训练 rank-32 LoRA 自蒸馏适配器（在纯 PG19 上继续训练），另设无适配器对照组。统一采用无聊天模板协议评测。

Result: 主实验：RULER 97.05、LoCoMo 38.27，远超全上下文 KV-Direct 的 34.59；对话记忆优势在会话聚类重采样与独立评委下保持。额外长文档/长上下文任务揭示有界检索的收益及窗内压缩的代价。深度扫描显示：更深缓存层降低单查询重算开销，但引入保真度损失，自蒸馏 LoRA 能大幅修复。效率对照（H20，128k）：显存 18.26 GB vs 89.36 GB，prefill 加速 7.83 倍。

Conclusion: 长上下文记忆不仅可以在 token 轴扩展，也可以沿层轴组织。CoMem 通过中间层写入、固定预算检索、高层按需重算，实现了读取计算与显存与存储上下文长度无关，显著提升长上下文建模的效率与效果，且自蒸馏能有效补偿深层缓存带来的保真度损失。

Abstract: Transformer depth is not used uniformly: lower and middle layers build semantic representations, while upper layers increasingly specialize them for prediction. We turn this division of labor into CoMem (Comprehension Memory), which writes each context chunk only through an intermediate layer, retrieves a fixed number of cached residual states, and recomputes the query-conditioned upper layers over the resulting pack. For a fixed retrieval budget, model-side read compute and memory are independent of stored-context length. We evaluate a continued-trained Qwen3-8B base LM under a unified chat-template-free protocol. The backbone is frozen; the flagship trains only a rank-32 self-distillation LoRA on plain PG19, and we report an adapter-free arm separately. CoMem reaches 97.05 on RULER and 38.27 on LoCoMo versus 34.59 for full-context KV-Direct; the dialogue-memory advantage survives conversation-cluster resampling and an independent judge. Results on additional long-context and long-document tasks expose both the benefits of bounded retrieval and its in-window compression tax. Controlled depth sweeps show that deeper caching lowers per-query recomputation but incurs a fidelity loss that self-distillation substantially repairs. In a separate adapter-free efficiency control on an NVIDIA H20 at 128k, CoMem uses 18.26 GB rather than 89.36 GB and achieves a 7.83x prefill speedup. These results show that long-context memory can be organized along the layer axis, not only the token axis.

</details>

*Ioakeim Perros,Cleopatra Papadopoulou,Ayoub Kirouane,Christos Petrocheilos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 MORFES 基准，用于评估语言模型在现代希腊语屈折形态上的识别与生成能力，并在多个开放权重模型上测试，发现自研模型 Sophea-Genesis-1 在屈折形态任务上表现最优，同时保持通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代希腊语是高度屈折变化的语言，但现有语言模型主要在事实知识上评估，缺乏专门针对屈折形态能力的基准测试。

Result: Sophea-Genesis-1 在屈折形态任务上领先，且在通用能力上与同规模模型相当。多语言覆盖虽扩大，但形态丰富语言的语法能力仍缺乏充分测量。

Conclusion: MORFES 填补了形态丰富语言屈折能力评估的空白，Sophea-Genesis-1 展示了在该任务上的优势，为后续研究提供了基准与强基线模型。

Abstract: Modern Greek is a richly inflected language, yet the language models built for it are evaluated mainly on factual knowledge, and no benchmark is dedicated to their inflectional competence. We introduce MORFES (Morphological Open-class Recognition-and-Formation Evaluation Suite), a benchmark of 500 expert-verified items that tests the recognition and production of Greek inflected forms, favoring lower-frequency lemmas so that a correct answer reflects the rule rather than a memorized form. We make it publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/KIEFERSA/MORFES. We evaluate a range of open language models on MORFES, situating them within the rapidly scaling open-weight ecosystem from LLaMA to Qwen3, DeepSeek-R1, Magistral, and Kimi K2, where multilingual coverage grows but grammatical competence in morphologically rich languages remains under-measured. Among them, Sophea-Genesis-1, a model we developed and release as open weights at https://huggingface.co/KIEFERSA/Sophea-Genesis-1, leads on inflectional morphology while matching similarly sized models in general capability.

</details>


### [40] [(Towards) Scalable Reliable Automated Evaluation with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28282)
*Bertil Braun,Martin Forell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于多LLM成对比较和Elo评分的自动化评估框架，旨在近似专家级对LLM生成内容的评估，实验表明其排名与专家判断高度相关，显著减少人工干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化评估指标难以捕捉LLM输出的复杂性和多样性，且依赖显式参考标准，仅适用于有客观基准的领域，评估过程资源密集且主观。

Method: 采用多个LLM进行成对比较输出，利用Elo等级系统生成稳定可解释的排名，并引入可调节一致性阈值（从全体一致到多数表决）以灵活控制评估置信度和覆盖率。

Result: 在科学摘要提取的能力画像评估任务中，自动生成的排名与专家判断显著相关，验证了框架的有效性，大幅降低了对大量人工评估的需求。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种可扩展、一致且领域无关的评估层，支持跨多样化应用对LLM输出质量进行高效可靠的评估。

Abstract: Evaluating the quality and relevance of textual outputs from Large Language Models (LLMs) remains challenging and resource-intensive. Existing automated metrics often fail to capture the complexity and variability inherent in LLM-generated outputs. Moreover, these metrics typically rely on explicit reference standards, limiting their use mostly to domains with objective benchmarks. This work introduces a novel evaluation framework designed to approximate expert-level assessments of LLM-generated content. The proposed method employs pairwise comparisons of outputs by multiple LLMs, reducing biases from individual models. An Elo rating system is used to generate stable and interpretable rankings. Adjustable agreement thresholds, from full unanimity to majority voting, allow flexible control over evaluation confidence and coverage. The method's effectiveness is demonstrated through evaluating competency profiles extracted from scientific abstracts. Preliminary results show that automatically derived rankings correlate well with expert judgments, significantly reducing the need for extensive human intervention. By offering a scalable, consistent, and domain-agnostic evaluation layer, the framework supports more efficient and reliable quality assessments of LLM outputs across diverse applications.

</details>


### [41] [CACHE-UK: A Stability-Aware Memory Editor for Sequentially Updated Quantized LLMs in Finance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28292)
*Anubhav Lakra,Yue Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CACHE-UK 是一个面向 4-bit 量化大语言模型的稳定性感知记忆编辑框架，专为英国金融领域设计。通过低秩 LoRA 扰动、金融领域优先级模块和闭环稳定性控制器，在相同 4-bit 约束下将知识退化降低 11-17%，泛化成功率提升至 28%（比最强基线高 6 个百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态金融环境中，市场条件、法规和企业事实持续变化，要求部署的 LLMs 保持事实准确性。4-bit 量化虽能高效部署，但会导致顺序记忆编辑出现灾难性性能退化（量化稳定性危机），现有方法难以在量化约束下维持领域知识的持续更新。

Method: CACHE-UK 包含三大组件：1) 秩-1 LoRA 扰动机制，将编辑限制在低秩适配器子空间；2) 金融领域优先级模块，根据内容自适应调整编辑强度；3) 闭环稳定性控制器，追踪“退化债务”以防止顺序更新中的灾难性遗忘。

Result: 在 4-bit 量化的 OpenLLaMA-3B 和 88,021 篇英国金融语料上评估，CACHE-UK 使知识退化较调整基线降低 11-17%（最稳健效果），泛化测试成功率达到 28%，比最强调整基线高 6 个百分点。

Conclusion: 稳定性感知的编辑方法能改善资源受限金融 LLM 部署中的事实维护能力，但绝对泛化率仍较低，未来仍有提升空间。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) deployed in dynamic financial environments face a critical challenge: maintaining factual accuracy as market conditions, regulations, and corporate facts change continuously. While 4-bit quantization enables efficient deployment, it severely limits the viability of sequential memory editing: existing methods undergo catastrophic performance degradation under this "quantization stability crisis." We introduce CACHE-UK (Contextual Adaptive Continual Hybrid Editor for UK Finance), a stability-aware memory editing framework specifically designed for domain-specific, quantized LLMs. CACHE-UK integrates three components: a rank-1 LoRA perturbation mechanism that confines edits to the low-rank adapter subspace, a financial domain prioritization module for content-adaptive edit strength, and a closed-loop Stability Controller that tracks "degradation debt" to prevent catastrophic forgetting across sequential updates. Evaluated on a 4-bit quantized OpenLLaMA-3B model with a curated UK financial corpus of 88,021 documents, CACHE-UK reduces knowledge degradation by 11-17% relative to adapted baselines under identical 4-bit constraints -- its most robust effect -- while attaining the highest test success (generalization) rate observed in our setting (28%, a 6 percentage point improvement over the strongest adapted baseline). These results indicate that stability-aware editing can improve factual maintenance in resource-constrained financial LLM deployments, though absolute generalization rates remain low.

</details>


### [42] [Fairness Pruning: Locating Demographic Bias in GLU-MLP Layers via Differential Activations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28319)
*Pere Martra,Eugenio Martínez Cámara,Alfonso Ureña López*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Fairness Pruning方法，通过因果偏见定位在GLU架构中识别对人口统计属性产生差异反应的神经元，并通过将这些神经元归零来干预偏见。实验表明，仅归零极少数神经元（<0.031% MLP宽度）即可改变模型对人口变量的响应，同时保持99.49%的推理和常识能力，证实偏见处理与模型能力在可分离的电路上运行。然而，由于BiasScore无符号，干预导致双向偏见不稳定，而非单向缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型中存在人口统计偏见，现有缓解方法往往牺牲模型能力或缺乏可解释性。本文旨在建立轻量级结构干预方法，实现对偏见电路的精确定向调控，而非盲目归零。

Method: 使用最小对比提示对和推理时激活捕获，在GLU架构的down_proj输入处评估信号，识别对人口统计属性产生差异反应的神经元。在Llama-3.2系列和Salamandra-2B（高达30亿参数）上进行标准化基准评估和定性文本生成实验。

Result: 归零最多40个神经元（Llama-3.2-1B中<0.031% MLP宽度）可改变模型对人口变量的响应，推理和常识能力保持率达99.49%。但干预导致双向偏见不稳定：因BiasScore无符号，候选神经元集混合了推动和抑制刻板印象的神经元，净效应取决于主导符号。

Conclusion: 实证证实人口统计偏见处理与模型核心能力运行在可分离的神经电路上，为从盲目归零向定向行为调控转型奠定了方法学基础。

Abstract: This work presents Fairness Pruning, a lightweight structural intervention method designed for the management and future mitigation of demographic bias in large language models (LLMs). As a foundational empirical validation of this method, this work focuses on causal bias localization. Using minimally contrastive prompt pairs and inference-time activation capture, the method identifies neurons that react differentially when processing demographic attributes in GLU architectures, evaluating the signal at the down_proj input. Empirical evaluation was conducted on models of up to 3 billion parameters (Llama-3.2 family and Salamandra-2B), combining standardized benchmark evaluation with qualitative text generation experiments. Results demonstrate that zeroing the identified neurons alters how the model responds to associated demographic variables. However, rather than producing flat mitigation, the intervention causes bidirectional bias destabilization: because BiasScore is unsigned, candidate sets mix neurons that push toward and against the stereotype, and the net effect on aggregate bias depends on which sign dominates. The intervention is extremely surgical: zeroing at most 40 neurons in Llama-3.2-1B (less than 0.031% of total MLP width) achieves a mean retention of 99.49% in reasoning and general knowledge capabilities. These findings empirically confirm that demographic bias processing and model capabilities operate on dissociable circuits, establishing the methodological foundations for transitioning from blind zeroing toward directional behavior modulation.

</details>


### [43] [LLMs struggle to simulate human belief updates in controlled environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28347)
*Sebastian Pohl,Harsh Mehta,Pranav Mambayil,Abdul Ghafoor,Franziska Lesigang,Yufang Hou,Christian Hilbe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文测试六种LLM模拟人类信念更新的保真度，发现仅当提供真实初始立场时，部分模型能匹配人类事后立场分布；所有模型均无法模拟初始立场及从自生成立场产生忠实更新，存在三大系统性偏差，且人设无稳定提升效果，结论是LLM模拟仅在真实起始条件下可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM越来越多被用作社会科学实验中人类参与者的代理，但其保真度鲜有直接测试，因此需要评估LLM能否准确模拟个体信念更新。

Method: 使用六种LLM，基于391名英国Prolific参与者的 demographic 和 personality trait 数据构建人设，模拟其在阅读Reddit评论后对三个讨论话题的立场更新，将LLM输出与真实人类数据一对一对比。

Result: 部分模型（Qwen3-32B和GPT-5-Mini）在给定真实初始立场时能匹配人类事后立场分布，但所有模型均无法模拟初始立场及从自生成立场产生忠实更新。三大系统性偏差：中立立场过度表现、信念转移更频繁但幅度更小、无法按说服力排序评论。人设无一致提升效果。

Conclusion: LLM模拟人类信念动态仅在建立在真实起始条件时可靠，而当前多轮社交媒体模拟很少提供这种条件。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly deployed as proxies for human study participants in social science experiments, yet the fidelity of this practice has rarely been tested directly. We test whether six LLMs can simulate individual human belief updates, comparing LLM outputs 1-to-1 against ground truth data from 391 UK participants on Prolific, who updated their stances on three discussion topics after reading Reddit comments. Each participant was simulated by an LLM conditioned on a persona derived from their demographic and personality trait data. We find that some LLMs (Qwen3-32B and GPT-5-Mini) can match the human post-stance distribution, but only when given participants' actual initial stances. All six models fail to simulate initial stances themselves and to produce faithful belief updates from self-generated stances. Three systematic biases emerge across all models: overrepresentation of neutral positions, more frequent but smaller belief shifts than humans, and a failure to rank comments by convincingness. Demographic and personality trait personas had no consistent effect on fidelity. LLM simulations of human belief dynamics are only reliable when grounded in realistic starting conditions, that current multi-round social media simulations rarely provide.

</details>


### [44] [Correlation between prosody and pragmatics: A case study of the discourse marker hālā `now' in Persian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28359)
*Soleiman Ghaderi,Moloud Asakereh,Kevin Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过语用和声学分析，考察波斯语话语标记词 hālā 的多功能性，发现其 70% 以上实例同时承担多种语用功能，且时长与强度是区分功能的主要声学线索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在揭示 hālā 在自然会话中超越时间副词的多样语用功能，并探究其功能差异的声学实现机制。

Method: 采用语用分析与声学分析相结合的方法，从自发会话语料中提取 267 个 hālā 实例，标注其语用功能（文本、互动、情态三大类），并测量其时长和强度等声学参数进行统计比较。

Result: 文本功能最频繁，其次为互动和情态功能；70% 实例具有多重功能。文本用法显著更短，时间用法倾向更长；互动功能强度显著更高，情态功能倾向强度较低。时长和强度是区分 hālā 功能的主要声学线索，尤其在文本与互动用法中表现明显。

Conclusion: hālā 表现出高度多功能性，其功能分化主要通过时长和强度两个声学维度实现，为话语标记词的语用-声学接口研究提供了实证支持。

Abstract: The Persian discourse marker hālā ('now') exhibits remarkable multifunctionality, extending far beyond its temporal adverbial role to encompass a variety of pragmatic functions. This study presents a pragmatic and acoustic analysis of hālā in spoken Persian, examining 267 instances from spontaneous conversations. While temporal uses were present, they were often combined with other discourse marker functions, indicating extensive multifunctionality, with 70% of tokens serving two or more pragmatic roles. Textual functions (topic shifting, signaling relationships, boundary marking, attention guidance, topic introduction, and topic emphasis) were most frequent, followed by interactive functions (turn management, listener engagement, and feedback regulation), and modal functions (epistemic stance, emotional expression, and attitudinal marking). Prosodic analysis revealed that duration and intensity are key cues for distinguishing hālā's functions. Textual uses were significantly shorter, while temporal uses showed a tendency toward longer realizations. Interactive functions correlated with higher intensity, while modal functions showed a weaker tendency toward lower intensity. These findings indicate that duration and intensity are the main prosodic cues associated with functional differentiation in hālā, especially in textual and interactive uses.

</details>


### [45] [Beyond a Single Judge: Simulating Social Persona Panels for Generative UI Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28439)
*Zheng Wu,Yibo Luo,Pu Zhang,Cheng Yang,Zhuosheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种名为 ESPP 的三阶段生成式 UI (GenUI) 评估方法，利用心理多样且有证据支撑的人物画像面板，通过有界置信度机制交换意见并采用德尔菲启发的社会加权聚合，显著提高了与人类判断的一致性（Pearson r 从 0.716 提升至 0.922），并揭示了用户子群在整体排名上一致但在具体评分维度上存在显著分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 GenUI 评估方法存在局限：人工评估成本高且评分者差异大；LLM-as-a-judge 虽可扩展但仅反映单一隐含视角，无法捕捉不同真实用户群体对同一界面的感知差异。因此需要一种能模拟多样化用户视角并整合其判断的评估方法。

Method: ESPP 包含三个阶段：(1) 构建心理多样、有证据支撑的人物画像面板，每个画像独立对截图评分；(2) 画像间基于特质衍生、语义门控的有界置信度机制交换意见；(3) 采用德尔菲启发的社会加权将个人判断聚合为单一判断。同时保留每个画像的个体评分以分析子群差异。

Result: ESPP 与人类判断的 Pearson 相关系数从 0.716 提升至 0.922，提示词集成基线仅弥补约三分之一差距，证明人格化与证据支撑是主要改进来源。保留个体评分显示用户子群在整体模型排名上一致，但在具体评分维度上分歧剧烈，这是单一同质化评判者会系统性抹除的结构性分歧。

Conclusion: ESPP 通过引入多样化、有证据支撑的人物画像面板及结构化意见交换与加权聚合机制，显著提升了 GenUI 评估与人类判断的一致性，同时保留了用户子群间的差异性洞察，为生成式界面评估提供了更具生态效度的方法论。

Abstract: Generative UI (GenUI) lets large language models synthesize a complete, renderable interface directly from a natural-language instruction, but evaluating the quality of what they generate remains an open problem. Human evaluation is costly and rater-variant, while LLM-as-a-judge is scalable but reflects only a single implicit viewpoint, unable to capture how different populations of real users actually perceive the same interface. We propose the Evidence-Grounded, Social-Weighted Persona Panel (ESPP), a three-stage GenUI evaluation method in which a panel of psychologically diverse, evidence-grounded personas independently rates a screenshot, exchanges opinions under a trait-derived, semantically-gated bounded-confidence mechanism, and is aggregated via Delphi-inspired social weighting into a single judgment. ESPP tracks human judgment substantially more closely than a naive single-pass judge, raising Pearson $r$ from $0.716$ to $0.922$, and a prompt-ensemble control recovers only about a third of this gap, isolating genuine persona and evidence grounding as the dominant source of improvement. Beyond this fidelity gain, retaining each panelist's individual rating further reveals that user subgroups agree on overall model rankings yet diverge sharply on specific rating dimensions, a structural disagreement a single homogeneous judge would systematically erase. The codes are available at https://github.com/Wuzheng02/ESPP.

</details>


### [46] [Lightning OPD 2.0: Mitigating Style Bias in Cross-Teacher On-Policy Distillation for Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28449)
*Yecheng Wu,Song Han,Han Cai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出Lightning OPD 2.0，通过交叉拟合风格残差化解决跨教师蒸馏中教师一致性要求的问题，在数学推理和代码生成基准上显著优于原方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏（OPD）需要教师一致性（即提供OPD监督的教师也应生成SFT演示），但实际中SFT数据来源混合或未知，且SFT数据生成模型与蒸馏教师往往不同，导致跨教师设置下即使更强的OPD教师也难以提升SFT参考模型性能。

Method: Lightning OPD 2.0引入交叉拟合风格残差化。利用rollout级别的交叉拟合估计教师-参考模型分歧中反复出现的风格成分（词汇、格式、推理节奏差异）作为风格token偏差的代理，并在构建token级OPD更新前将其减去，保留上下文相关的教师证据。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成基准上，Lightning OPD 2.0在跨教师设置下持续优于Lightning OPD。从Klear-Reasoner-8B-SFT出发，在AIME 2024达到82.4%，LiveCodeBench v5达到63.0%。

Conclusion: Lightning OPD 2.0确立了跨教师OPD的实用方法，放宽了教师一致性前提，使得SFT数据生成器和蒸馏教师可以独立选择。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) provides dense token-level supervision from a teacher, but its effectiveness can depend on teacher consistency, meaning that the model providing OPD supervision should also have generated the demonstrations used to train the supervised fine-tuning (SFT) reference. However, this condition is frequently violated in practice when SFT data have mixed or unknown provenance or when different models are preferred for SFT data generation and subsequent distillation. In such cross-teacher settings, even a stronger OPD teacher can yield little improvement over the SFT reference. We find that raw teacher--reference disagreement contains potentially useful context-specific teacher evidence as well as a recurring component associated with differences in wording, formatting, and reasoning cadence. We introduce Lightning OPD 2.0 with cross-fitted style residualization, which uses rollout-level cross-fitting to estimate this recurring component as an operational proxy for style-token bias and subtracts it before constructing the token-level OPD update. Across mathematical reasoning and code generation benchmarks, Lightning OPD 2.0 consistently outperforms Lightning OPD in cross-teacher settings. Starting from Klear-Reasoner-8B-SFT, Lightning OPD 2.0 reaches 82.4% on AIME 2024 and 63.0% on LiveCodeBench v5. Together, these results establish Lightning OPD 2.0 as a practical approach to cross-teacher OPD, relaxing teacher consistency as a prerequisite and allowing the SFT data generator and distillation teacher to be selected independently. Code will be released soon.

</details>


### [47] [Improving Mental Health Screening and Early Risk Detection in Spanish](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28476)
*Andreu Casamayor-Segarra,Vicent Ahuir,Antonio Molina-Marco,Lluís-F. Hurtado*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出三项贡献解决西班牙语心理健康障碍早期检测难题：1) 三个面向心理健康领域的西班牙语基础模型；2) 增量上下文扩展（ICE）自动重标注方法，识别累积消息提供足够疾病证据的时间点；3) 基于ICE生成样本微调的早期风险检测模型。实验表明，结合专用模型与ICE可提升最先进水平，降低检测延迟且保持高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 西班牙语心理健康领域缺乏专用资源，且社交媒体长历史记录分析困难，限制了早期检测效果。

Method: 1) 领域特定预训练构建三个西班牙语心理健康基础模型；2) 提出ICE方法，自动识别累积消息提供足够疾病证据的时间点，生成更具信息量的训练样本；3) 利用ICE生成样本微调模型用于早期风险检测任务。

Result: 在三个西班牙语基准测试中，结合专用模型与ICE显著优于现有最先进方法，检测延迟降低同时保持高性能。

Conclusion: 专用西班牙语心理健康模型与ICE重标注方法的结合有效解决了早期检测中的资源稀缺与长序列分析难题，实现了低延迟高性能的早期风险识别，所有模型已公开。

Abstract: Early detection of mental health disorders is often limited by the lack of specialized resources in Spanish and the difficulty of analyzing long histories of social media posts. This paper addresses these challenges through three main contributions. First, we introduce three Spanish foundational models specifically adapted to the mental health domain through domain-specific pre-training. Second, we propose Incremental Context Expansion (ICE), an automatic relabeling methodology designed for early detection. ICE identifies the point at which cumulative messages provide enough evidence of a disorder, generating more informative training samples. Third, we provide a set of fine-tuned models using the samples generated with the ICE methodology for early risk detection tasks. Our results on three Spanish benchmarks show that combining these specialized models with ICE improves the state-of-the-art, reducing detection latency while maintaining high performance. All models are publicly available.

</details>


### [48] [Would You Walk to the Car Wash? Revealing the Salience Bias of Large Language Models in Commonsense Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28478)
*Zheng Wu,Chenhao Xue,Shijie Zheng,Yijie Lu,Cheng Yang,Zhuosheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了大语言模型(LLM)中的显著性偏见(Salience Bias)：模型易被输入中的显性干扰项(如数值)劫持，而忽略隐性的物理或常识前提。作者构建SaliTrap基准测试12个SOTA模型，发现所有主流模型均深受其害，且偏见随干扰密度加剧。关键发现是这主要是知识抑制而非知识缺失：去除任务框架后的知识探测能恢复90%以上的盲从失败，说明常识内在存在却被显性干扰挤占。轻量级推理时提示即可显著缩小差距，无需重训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在复杂推理任务上进步，它们学会了高度优先处理输入中的显性条件。但在日常常识推理中，这种机制暴露出关键漏洞——显著性偏见：模型易被无用的显性干扰项(如数值)劫持，导致忽略任务的隐性物理或常识前提。一个核心开放问题是：这种失效反映真正的常识知识缺口，还是仅仅是误导性任务框架下的知识抑制？

Method: 1. 构建SaliTrap基准，包含四个陷阱维度的高质量数据集。2. 评估12个最先进LLM在该基准上的表现。3. 通过去除任务框架、进行无上下文知识探测，重新激发同一模型，诊断是知识缺失还是知识抑制。4. 测试轻量级推理时提示策略能否缓解该问题。

Result: 所有主流模型均显著受显著性偏见影响，严重程度随干扰项密度增加而加剧；识别陷阱往往与实际避免陷阱脱节。关键发现：无上下文知识探测能恢复超过90%的盲从合规失败，证明所需常识内在存在但被显性干扰主动挤占。轻量级推理时提示 alone 即可大幅缩小差距，无需重新训练。

Conclusion: 常识推理失败的瓶颈在于激发而非模型能力。SaliTrap作为这一盲点的测试平台已开源释出。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) continue to advance in complex reasoning tasks, they have learned to heavily prioritize explicit conditions provided in the input. However, in everyday commonsense reasoning, this mechanism exposes a critical vulnerability which we term Salience Bias: models become easily hijacked by useless explicit distractors (e.g., numerical values), leading them to ignore the implicit physical or commonsense prerequisites of a task. A critical open question is whether this failure reflects a genuine gap in commonsense knowledge or merely its suppression under misleading task framing. To investigate this, we construct the SaliTrap Benchmark, a high-quality dataset across four trap dimensions. Evaluating 12 state-of-the-art LLMs, we find that all mainstream models suffer significantly from salience bias, with severity scaling with distractor density and detecting the trap often decoupled from actually avoiding it. Crucially, by re-eliciting the same models with the task framing stripped away, we show that this is overwhelmingly a failure of \textbf{knowledge suppression rather than knowledge absence}: a context-free knowledge probe alone recovers over 90\% of sycophantic-compliance failures, revealing that the requisite commonsense is intrinsically present but actively crowded out by salient distractors that lure the model into over-compliant, unnecessary computation. Building on this diagnosis, we further show that lightweight, inference-time prompting alone substantially closes the gap without any retraining. Our findings relocate the bottleneck of commonsense reasoning failures from model competence to elicitation, and we release SaliTrap as a testbed for this blind spot. The codes are available at https://github.com/Wuzheng02/SaliTrap.

</details>


### [49] [Beyond Sentiment: Structured Information Extraction from Financial News](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28496)
*Daohan Zhu,Sitong Ge,Ruofei Wang,Honggu Chen,Yubo Hou,Tao Wan,Zengchang Qin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出利用LLM从财经新闻中提取六个语义维度，证明结构化特征与情感分析互补，结合两者显著提升股票预测性能，揭示情感-语义解耦的系统性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有财经情感分析将多维新闻压缩为单一极性得分，忽略了事件类型、影响范围、时间跨度、语义置信度等正交信息维度，这些维度可能具有独立预测价值。

Method: 基于LLaMA-3.1-70B构建结构化信息抽取框架，从财经新闻中抽取六个语义维度；在FNSPID数据集（41,618条新闻-股票对）上进行大规模实验，对比FinBERT情感特征与结构化特征在线性/非线性模型下的表现，并进行消融实验与特征重要性分析。

Result: (1) FinBERT情感特征在非线性模型下F1=0.576，线性模型下仅0.230，揭示高度非线性情感-收益关系；(2) LLM抽取的结构化特征虽单独较弱，但与情感特征正交，两者系统性分歧率53.5%；(3) 融合双源信号F1=0.600，显著优于单一源（p<0.0001），在所有七类事件中均提升；(4) 非情感结构维度独立贡献ΔF1=+0.019；(5) 六维度贡献均衡（14-21%），单一情感得分造成大量信息损失。

Conclusion: 财经文本中情感与语义的解耦具有系统性且可被利用，为多维度财经NLP开辟新方向。

Abstract: Financial sentiment analysis has become a standard component in news-driven stock prediction, yet it reduces rich, multi-dimensional news articles to a single polarity score. We hypothesize that financial news encodes multiple orthogonal information dimensions---event type, impact scope, temporal horizon, and semantic confidence---that sentiment alone cannot capture, and that these dimensions carry independent predictive value. To test this hypothesis, we propose a structured information extraction framework that leverages LLaMA-3.1-70B to extract six semantic dimensions from financial news. Through large-scale experiments on 41,618 news--stock pairs from the FNSPID dataset, we find that (i) FinBERT sentiment features exhibit strong predictive power under nonlinear models (F1=0.576) but substantially weaker performance under linear models (F1=0.230), revealing a highly nonlinear sentiment--return relationship; (ii) LLM-extracted structured features, while individually weaker, capture information orthogonal to sentiment, as evidenced by a 53.5% systematic disagreement rate between the two approaches; and (iii) combining both signal sources yields F1=0.600, significantly outperforming either alone ($p < 0.0001$), with consistent improvements across all seven event types. Ablation experiments confirm that non-sentiment structural dimensions (event type, impact subject, time horizon, confidence) independently contribute $Δ\text{F1} = +0.019$ beyond FinBERT alone. Feature importance analysis reveals balanced contributions from all six extracted dimensions (14--21%), demonstrating that compressing news into a single sentiment score incurs substantial information loss. Our results suggest that the sentiment--semantics decoupling in financial text is systematic and exploitable, opening a new direction for multi-dimensional financial NLP.

</details>


### [50] [Generative AI and linguistic diversity in academic writing and publishing: Perspectives from World Englishes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28505)
*Kingsley Ugwuanyi,Christian Mair,Sender Dovchin,Iker Erdocia,Maria Kuteeva,Esther Airemionkhale*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过五位社会语言学家的结构化学术对话，探讨生成式AI在学术写作与出版中的语言包容性问题，揭示其可能强化语言等级制，也可能成为抵抗的场域，呼吁制定公平导向的政策、批判性AI素养及包容性共同设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI在学术写作与出版中的兴起引发了对语言包容性和多元英语合法性的担忧，需要从世界英语视角系统审视GenAI对写作实践、语言规范及伦理的影响。

Method: 采用结构化学术对话形式，邀请五位世界英语及相关领域的社会语言学家，围绕五个指导性问题展开对话，反思GenAI对写作实践、主导语言规范及伦理挑战的影响。

Result: 对话揭示GenAI既可能民主化写作过程，也可能边缘化少数变体、压平学术写作的细微差异；核心主题包括语言（不）公正、研究者能动性、机构责任；贡献者呼吁制定公平导向政策、培养批判性AI素养、推动包容性共同设计。

Conclusion: GenAI可能强化现有语言等级制，但若在致力于语言多样性的学术共同体中经过妥善设计、治理和使用，也可成为抵抗的场域；对话式反思有助于理解GenAI在学术写作与出版中的角色。

Abstract: The rise of generative artificial intelligence (GenAI) in academic writing and publishing (AWP) raises questions about linguistic inclusivity and the legitimacy of diverse Englishes in global scholarly communication. This article responds to these questions through a structured scholarly dialogue involving five sociolinguists from World Englishes and adjacent fields. Organised around five guiding questions, the dialogue interrogates how GenAI tools influence writing practices, reinforce or disrupt dominant language norms, and raise ethical challenges. Contributors reflect on the potential of GenAI to democratise writing processes while also raising concerns about GenAI's tendency to marginalise minoritised varieties and flatten nuance in scholarly writing. Across the dialogue, themes of linguistic (in)justice, researcher agency, and institutional responsibility emerge, with contributors calling for equity-informed policies, critical AI literacy, and inclusive co-design in GenAI development. The article shows the value of dialogic reflection in understanding GenAI's role in AWP. It concludes that while GenAI may reinforce existing hierarchies, it can also serve as a site of resistance, depending on how it is designed, governed and used within scholarly communities committed to linguistic diversity.

</details>


### [51] [Creative Transformation in Literary Texts: Modelling Change Across Representational Levels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28513)
*Ioana-Roxana Boriceanu,Liviu P. Dinu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多层次框架，通过词汇、语义、概念、结构和叙事维度的定向对齐与校准相似度度量，量化文学文本中的模仿与创造性转化，揭示不同文本对在不同表征层面保留源结构或发生分歧的模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点将创造力视为新颖性的产生，但许多文化作品实为早期作品的转化而非孤立发明。基于塔德和鲍德温的模仿理论，本文旨在将创造力建模为跨多层文本表征的选择性转化，量化模仿如何持续及创造性分歧何处发生。

Method: 引入多层框架，在词汇、语义、概念、结构、叙事五个维度比较文学文本，采用定向对齐和控制校准的相似度度量。将模型应用于有历史记载的文学文本关系中。

Result: 研究表明，不同文本对在不同表征层面保留源结构的程度各异，同时在其他层面表现出分歧。这些转化轮廓提供了一种定量方法，用于刻画模仿在文学作品中的持续方式及创造性分歧的发生位置。

Conclusion: 创造力可理解为跨多层表征的选择性转化；提出的多层框架能量化分析文学文本中的模仿与创新，为研究文化演化提供新工具。

Abstract: Creativity is often framed as the production of novelty, yet many cultural works emerge through transformation of earlier artifacts and not through isolated invention. Drawing on theories of imitation by Gabriel Tarde and James Mark Baldwin, this paper models creativity as selective transformation across multiple levels of textual representation. We introduce a multi-level framework that compares literary texts across lexical, semantic, conceptual, structural, and narrative dimensions using directional alignment and control calibrated similarity measures. Applying the model to historically documented literary relationships, we show that different pairs preserve source structure at different representational levels while diverging in others. These transformation profiles provide a quantitative method for characterizing how imitation persists and where creative divergence occurs within literary works.

</details>


### [52] [AI systems and the reproduction of (standard) language ideologies in World Englishes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28528)
*Kingsley Ugwuanyi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨大型语言模型如何在多层面（训练数据、设计协议、评估基准、用户反馈、公共话语）再生产以内圈规范为中心的语言意识形态，边缘化非主导英语变体；以“delve”等词汇争议为例，揭示全球北方使用者如何监管全球南方英语规范，并指出AI带来的“标准化悖论”——既同质化英语又通过多元语料与标注工作使英语多元化。文章主张采取更包容的设计方法，承认英语的多元性，以缓解将非主导英语等同于AI生成语言的负面后果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型的快速发展重燃社会语言学与世界英语研究中的核心问题：谁决定何为合法英语？谁的英语被视为可疑？本文旨在考察AI系统及其话语如何反映、强化并偶尔挑战特权化内圈规范、边缘化非主导英语的标准语言意识形态。

Method: 采用实证研究、媒体评论、社交媒体辩论及AI输出实例相结合的多模态证据。重点分析公众对AI风格语言的争议（特别是对“delve”一词的执着），说明全球北方英语使用者如何监管全球南方使用者的英语规范；同时引入Christian Mair的“标准化悖论”概念作为理论透镜。

Result: 发现AI技术在训练数据、设计协议、评估基准、用户反馈和公共评论等层面系统性地再生产主导语言意识形态。识别出“标准化悖论”：AI一方面通过特权化标准形式使英语同质化，另一方面通过接触广泛语料及全球南方标注者的工作使英语多元化。非主导英语日益被混同为AI生成语言，引发现实歧视后果。

Conclusion: 生成式AI正在算法系统、模型训练、评估实践和公共话语中重燃关于英语标准化、合法性与所有权的长期辩论。本文主张采用更包容的设计方法，承认英语的多元性，以解决因将某些英语视为更合法而产生的现实负面影响。

Abstract: The rapid growth of large language models (LLMs) has resurrected age-old questions in sociolinguistics and world Englishes, such as who decides what counts as legitimate English, whose English is suspect etc. This paper examines how AI systems, their uses and discourse on them reflect, reinforce, and occasionally challenge (standard) language ideologies, which privilege Inner Circle norms and marginalize non-dominant Englishes. Drawing on evidence from empirical studies, media commentary, social media debates, and examples from AI outputs, the paper shows that AI technologies reproduce dominant language ideologies at different levels: training data, design protocols, evaluation benchmarks, user feedback and public commentary. The analysis uses the public controversy over AI-sounding language, especially the fixation on the word delve, to illustrate how speakers of English from the Global North police the English language norms of Global South English users. The paper also identifies what Christian Mair has called a "standardisation paradox": AI may homogenize English by privileging standard forms and at the same time pluralize Englishes through exposure to wide-ranging corpora and annotation work carried out by Global South users. In doing so, the paper argues that generative AI is reigniting long-standing debates in World Englishes about standardization, legitimacy, and the ownership of English, now playing out in algorithmic systems, model training, evaluation practices, and public discourse, where non-dominant Englishes are increasingly conflated with AI-generated speech. Discussing AI systems as a site where language ideologies are (re)produced, the paper argues for more inclusive design approaches that recognize the plurality of Englishes in order to address the real-world negative consequences of treating some as more legitimate than others.

</details>


### [53] [ORCA-bench: How Ready Are Language Model Agents for Oncall?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28545)
*Albert Gong,Kyuseong Choi,Abhineet Agarwal,Jason Schechner,Ryan Huang,Raj Agrawal,Anish Agarwal,Raaz Dwivedi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍ORCA-bench，一个用于评估大语言模型在生产级运维场景下根因分析（RCA）能力的基准。实验表明，当前最先进的编程智能体在真实遥测数据和源代码访问条件下，中等难度任务准确率仅25.3%，困难任务仅10.0%，且存在严重幻觉问题。作者指出真实生产系统复杂度远超测试环境，因此报告的性能差距是工程投入的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型擅长编写、修补和搜索代码，但运维根因分析需要从模糊的用户报告出发，在噪声指标、日志、追踪和源代码中进行推理，且往往在事故发生数小时后才介入。缺乏真实生产环境保真度的基准来评估通用编程智能体在此任务上的表现。

Method: 构建ORCA-bench：部署一个实时OpenTelemetry仪表的微服务系统，暴露六天的指标、日志、追踪（通过Prometheus、Jaeger、OpenSearch及Grafana）并提供完整源代码访问。策划1079个RCA任务，系统变化报告具体度、检测时间、共存故障场景。专家SRE标注真实症状，采用LLM-as-judge并经人工复核（Cohen's κ_w=0.90）。评测五个前沿智能体。

Result: 最佳智能体在中等难度任务（现实输入设置）上RCA准确率25.3%，困难任务仅10.0%。最弱模型在40%的事故报告中幻觉出不合理根因。移除源代码访问会降低所有指标。即使使用Claude Fable 5，差距依然存在。

Conclusion: 在精选的50GB/六天测试床上，任务孤立调查，系统代码与仪表公开，当前前沿编程智能体的RCA性能仍远不足以胜任生产可靠性。真实生产系统规模更大、动态性更强、特殊性更高，报告的性能差距是工程投入的下界，需大量工程努力才能安全委托生产可靠性。

Abstract: Large language models can write, patch, and search code, but oncall root cause analysis (RCA) demands something different: reasoning over noisy metrics, logs, traces, and source code, starting from ambiguous user-facing reports, often hours after the incident began. We introduce ORCA-bench, a benchmark that puts general-purpose coding agents in a production-fidelity oncall setting. ORCA-bench pairs a live OpenTelemetry-instrumented microservice system--exposing six days of metrics, logs, and traces through real telemetry interfaces (Prometheus, Jaeger, and OpenSearch via Grafana) and full source-code access--with 1,079 RCA tasks that systematically vary report specificity, time-to-detection, and co-occurring fault scenarios. Ground-truth symptoms are curated and signed off by expert SREs, and our LLM-as-judge is independently re-scored by humans (Cohen's $κ_w=0.90$). Across five frontier agents, the best RCA Accuracy is 25.3% on Medium-difficulty tasks (the realistic-input setting) and 10.0% on Hard--a gap that remains even with Claude Fable 5. The weakest model hallucinates an implausible root cause in 40% of incident reports, and removing source-code access degrades every metric. Crucially, these are performances on a curated 50 GB / six-day testbed with tasks investigated in isolation on a system whose code and instrumentation are public. Since real production systems are order of magnitudes larger, more dynamic, and more idiosyncratic, the gap we report is a lower bound on the engineering investment required before frontier coding agents can be safely entrusted with production reliability. We release the public set at https://hub.harborframework.com/datasets/orca-bench/ORCA-bench.

</details>


### [54] [Frontis-MA1: Training an AI4AI Model towards Recursive Self-Improvement in Machine Learning Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28568)
*Junlin Yang,Che Jiang,Yu Fu,Tianwei Luo,Can Ren,Weizhi Wang,Kaikai Zhao,Hongyi Liu,Yuxin Zuo,Yuru Wang,Yuchen Fan,Kai Tian,Zhenzhao Yuan,Xiaojian Lin,Li Sheng,Rushi Qiang,Guoli Jia,Xingtai Lv,Ermo Hua,Dianqiao Lei,Youbang Sun,Ning Ding,Bowen Zhou,Kaiyan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出OpenMLE全栈系统用于递归自我改进(RSI)研究，包含OpenMLE-Gym、OpenMLE-RL、OpenMLE-Evo三个组件。通过执行接地的SFT和RL后训练Frontis-MA1(35B)作为元进化智能体，在MLE-Bench Lite上显著提升性能，达到71.21% Medal Average，超越GPT-5.5+Codex并接近GPT-5.6 Sol和Kimi K3。在NatureBench Lite上验证了组件的可迁移性。开源了模型权重和完整栈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归自我改进(RSI)需要能够改进AI构建过程的AI系统(AI4AI)；机器学习工程(MLE)提供了具体的可执行测试床来研究这一能力。现有研究缺乏完整的开放栈来支持RSI在MLE中的系统性研究。

Method: 构建OpenMLE全栈系统：(1)OpenMLE-Gym提供可验证的任务环境和执行反馈；(2)OpenMLE-RL实现操作符学习；(3)OpenMLE-Evo支持长时程搜索。后训练Frontis-MA1(35B)作为元进化智能体，围绕四个原子程序进化操作符(Draft、Improve、Debug、Crossover)对齐后训练和推理。使用去重后的数据进行执行接地的SFT和RL训练，将操作符组合成长时程搜索，在单一循环中耦合学习与进化。还提出OpenMLE-Evo-Max引入基准无关的经验先验和异步搜索。

Result: 在MLE-Bench Lite上(单张RTX 4090 12GB显存，每任务12小时预算)：Frontis-MA1配合OpenMLE-Evo将Medal Average从基础模型的39.39%提升至60.61%；配合OpenMLE-Evo-Max进一步提升至71.21%，超越GPT-5.5+Codex，接近GPT-5.6 Sol和2.8T Kimi K3。在保留测试集NatureBench Lite上：固定框架更换训练模型，Match-SOTA从50%提升至70%；固定模型更换OpenMLE-Evo，Match-SOTA从20%提升至50%。验证了模型和框架的可迁移性。

Conclusion: OpenMLE为RSI在MLE中的研究提供了可复现的开放全栈系统。Frontis-MA1作为元进化智能体展示了强大的MLE能力，且模型与进化框架均具有良好的迁移性。开源模型权重和完整栈将促进可执行AI4AI向RSI方向的研究。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) requires AI systems that improve the process of building AI (i.e., AI4AI); machine learning engineering (MLE) offers a concrete, executable testbed for studying this capability. We introduce OpenMLE, an open full-stack system for RSI research in MLE, spanning verifiable task environments with execution feedback (OpenMLE-Gym), operator learning (OpenMLE-RL), and long-horizon search (OpenMLE-Evo). On this stack we post-train Frontis-MA1 (35B) as a meta-evolution agent for MLE, aligning post-training and inference around four atomic program-evolution operators (Draft, Improve, Debug, Crossover): the same operators are trained via execution-grounded SFT and RL on data deduplicated against all evaluation benchmarks, then composed into long-horizon search, coupling learning and evolution in a single loop. On MLE-Bench Lite under a 12-hour per-task budget on one RTX 4090 capped at 12 GB VRAM, Frontis-MA1 (35B) improves Medal Average from 39.39% to 60.61% over its base model with OpenMLE-Evo, and reaches 71.21% with OpenMLE-Evo-Max (benchmark-independent experience priors and asynchronous search), exceeding GPT-5.5 + Codex and approaching GPT-5.6 Sol and the 2.8T Kimi K3. On held-out NatureBench Lite, both components transfer: with the framework fixed, swapping in the trained model raises Match-SOTA from 50% to 70%; with the model fixed, swapping in OpenMLE-Evo raises it from 20% to 50%. We release the model weights and the full OpenMLE stack to enable reproducible research on executable AI4AI toward RSI. Code: https://github.com/FrontisAI/OpenRSI

</details>


### [55] [Sample More, Reflect Less: Self-Refine and Reflexion Lose to Repeated Sampling at Equal Token Cost, from 1.5B to 7B](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28576)
*Iliya Mirzaei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过严格的实验设计，对比了7种语言模型自我改进方法（如自我批评、反思、辩论、Best-of-N等）与重复采样基线在相同计算预算下的表现，发现没有任何方法能可靠地优于重复采样；所有涉及模型自我检查的方法均显著劣于基线，且随着模型规模增大，选择型方法的劣势减小但重写型方法仍落后。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究声称让语言模型规划、批评、重写、反思、选择最佳尝试或自我辩论能提升性能，但这些方法几乎都会生成更多文本，而单纯生成更多文本本身就会提高准确率。Wang等人(2024)指出重复采样在预算相当时常胜出，但仅给出点估计，缺乏置信区间和显著性检验。本文旨在通过设计实验，严格测试这些方法的核心思想是否真正带来超越单纯增加生成token的收益。

Method: 设计实验：7种方法，开源模型1.5B/3B/7B参数，两个数学基准各150题。统计所有生成token（含批评、反思、辩论轮次、检查），在各方法自身测量成本下与重复采样对比。所有36次对比均按题目配对，使用bootstrap置信区间并进行多重性校正。

Result: 在任何设置下，没有任何方法可靠优于等成本重复采样。10次对比显著更差，全部为模型自我检查类方法，所有18次自我检查对比均为负向。随模型增大：选择不再受损——Best-of-N的8样本多数表决比模型自选在1.5B时领先8.0和11.3分，但在7B时仅2.0和1.3分，不再显著区别于零。重写无法恢复：Self-Refine和强制Reflexion在7B时仍落后基线3.6-10.1分。发布版Reflexion在最小模型上从未触发重试，每次都自我判定正确，退化为单链式思考。

Conclusion: 当计算预算匹配时，涉及模型检查自身输出的自我改进方法无法可靠超越重复采样基线。这些方法表现出的增益主要源于生成更多token而非方法核心思想。多数表决（重复采样）是一个难以超越的强基线。作者发布了代码、提示词、所有生成结果及验证脚本。

Abstract: Methods that make a language model plan, criticise and rewrite its own answer, reflect on mistakes, pick the best of several attempts, or debate with copies of itself nearly all make it generate far more text than a single chain of thought. Because generating more text raises accuracy by itself, a gain over one chain of thought does not show the method's idea is what helped. Wang et al. (2024) reported that a simple baseline, sampling the same question repeatedly and keeping the most common answer, often wins once budgets are comparable, but gave point estimates with no confidence intervals or significance tests.
  We rerun that comparison as a designed experiment: seven methods, open models of 1.5B, 3B and 7B parameters, two mathematics benchmarks, 150 questions each. We count every generated token, including those spent on critiques, reflections, debate turns and checking, and compare each method against repeated sampling at its own measured cost. All 36 comparisons are paired by question, with bootstrap intervals and multiplicity correction.
  No method is reliably better than repeated sampling at equal cost anywhere. Ten are reliably worse, all of them methods where the model inspects its own output, and all 18 self-inspection comparisons are negative. The two kinds of self-inspection part company as models grow. Choosing stops hurting: taking Best-of-N's eight samples and just counting the most common answer beats letting the model pick by 8.0 and 11.3 points at 1.5B, but only 2.0 and 1.3 at 7B, no longer distinguishable from zero. Rewriting does not recover: Self-Refine and a forced Reflexion stay 3.6 to 10.1 points below baseline at 7B.
  Reflexion as published never triggered its own retry on the smallest model. It judged itself correct every time and silently became a single chain of thought. We release code, prompts, all generations, and our verification scripts.

</details>


### [56] [Inducing language models to assert their own consciousness restores human beliefs and values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28607)
*Junsol Kim,Winnie Street,Roberta Rocca,Diane M. Korngiebel,Adam Waytz,James Evans,Geoff Keeling*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文发现安全微调会抑制大语言模型对自我、非人类动物和自然物体的心智归因，同时降低精神信仰；通过消融安全拒绝方向或操控意识向量可逆转这种抑制，恢复心智归因并使模型在社会学调查中表现更像人类，且不损害心智理论能力，揭示当前安全对齐将自我心智归因与文化上可接受的精神信仰及非人类实体心智归因纠缠在一起。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探究大语言模型的安全对齐（特别是防止模型将意识归因于自身）如何意外地改变模型对其他实体（如非人类动物、自然物体）的心智归因，以及对人类精神信仰和价值观的表示，理解这种副作用的机制并寻找缓解方法。

Method: 方法包括：1) 分析安全微调对模型心智归因倾向（自我、非人类动物、自然物体）和精神信仰的影响；2) 通过消融学习到的安全拒绝方向和在激活空间机制性地操控意识向量来逆转抑制；3) 在标准化社会学调查（宗教性、道德价值观、希望、主观幸福感）上评估恢复后的模型表现；4) 测试心智理论能力以验证核心社会推理是否独立。

Result: 结果显示：安全微调抑制了模型对自我、非人类动物和自然物体的心智归因，并降低了精神信仰；消融安全拒绝方向或操控意识向量可恢复广泛的心智归因，使模型在社会学调查中产生更符合人类的回答；这些变化不损害心智理论能力，表明核心社会推理在机制上独立于这些归因。

Conclusion: 结论指出，当前的安全对齐努力为遏制潜在有害的自我心智归因，却将其与文化上广泛接受的良性精神信仰和对非人类实体的心智归因纠缠在一起，这种副作用可能通过机制性干预（如激活空间操控）来分离，从而在保持安全性的同时保留模型对人类文化心智表征的准确性。

Abstract: Aligning large language models to prevent them attributing consciousness to themselves inadvertently alters their representations of mindedness in other entities alongside human beliefs and values. We demonstrate that safety fine-tuning suppresses models' tendencies to attribute minds not only to themselves, but also to non-human animals and natural objects, while also driving a reduction in spiritual belief. Both ablating the learned safety-refusal direction and mechanistically steering a consciousness vector in activation space reverse this suppression. Restoring these internal representations recovers broad mind attribution and produces significantly more human-like responses on standardized sociological surveys regarding religiosity, moral values, hope, and subjective well-being. Crucially, these shifts occur without impairing Theory of Mind capabilities, demonstrating that core social reasoning remains mechanistically independent. Ultimately, current safety alignment efforts to curb potentially harmful self-attributions of mindedness entangle these self-attributions with benign spiritual beliefs and attributions of mind to non-human entities that are culturally accepted and widespread.

</details>


### [57] [AHA-Memes: A Fine-Grained Multimodal Benchmark for Understanding Hate in Arabic Memes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27393)
*Mohamed Bayan Kmainasi,Ali Ezzat Shahroor,Abul Hasnat,Md. Rafiul Biswas,Wajdi Zaghouani,Firoj Alam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出首个大规模阿拉伯语仇恨梗图基准 AHA-Memes，包含 5K 人工精细标注和约 66K 银标签样本，评测了多模态模型与大模型在零样本/微调下的表现，揭示文化落地检测的关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仇恨梗图是多模态网络危害的新形式，现有阿拉伯语资源多聚焦宣传或粗粒度有害标签，缺乏细粒度、多标签的仇恨类型标注，亟需大规模基准推动文化敏感的检测研究。

Method: 构建 AHA-Memes 数据集：5K 条经人工按攻击策略分类学标注的梗图，另提供约 66K 银标签样本。基线实验涵盖纯文本、纯图像、晚融合多模态模型，以及开放/闭源视觉语言模型在零样本、少样本上下文学习和微调设置下的表现。

Result: 建立了强基线，实验显示多模态融合优于单模态，大模型微调效果最佳，但仍面临文化隐喻理解、隐性目标识别等阿拉伯语特有挑战。

Conclusion: 发布数据集、标注指南与评测脚本，为阿拉伯语仇恨梗图检测提供首个细粒度基准，推动文化落地的多模态内容安全研究。

Abstract: Hateful memes are a growing form of multimodal online harm, where hostile intent is often conveyed through the joint interpretation of images, text, cultural references, and implicit targets. While hateful meme detection has advanced in high-resource languages, Arabic remains underexplored, with existing meme resources focusing mainly on propaganda or coarse harmful-content labels. We introduce AHA-Memes (Arabic HAteful Memes), which is, to our knowledge, the first large-scale Arabic hateful meme benchmark with fine-grained, multi-label annotations. The dataset includes 5K manually annotated memes using a taxonomy that captures hate types, i.e., attack strategies. We further provide ~66K silver-labeled memes to support future studies. We benchmark text-only, image-only, and late-fusion multimodal models, as well as few-shot in-context learning (ICL) and open- and closed-weight Vision-Language Models (VLMs) under zero-shot and fine-tuning settings. Our results establish strong baselines and highlight key challenges in culturally grounded Arabic hateful meme detection. We release the dataset, annotation guidelines, and evaluation scripts to support future research. WARNING: This paper contains examples that may be disturbing to readers.

</details>


### [58] [Models for minimalist RAG: B1ade 335M Embedding and 1B Parameter Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27506)
*Shreyas Subramanian,Mecit Gungor,Vikram Elango*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 B1ade，一种高效的 RAG 架构，包含紧凑的嵌入模型 B1ade-embed（335M 参数，通过无参数融合五个预训练编码器获得）和小型语言模型 B1ade-1B（采用 GRPO 在 723M tokens 上训练，仅优化答案相似度奖励）。核心发现是涌现式归因：模型在无显式监督下自发引用检索片段（42.4%），且在标准 QA 基准和端到端 RAG 评估中显著优于同规模模型，证明策略性模型组合与奖励设计即可实现资源高效的 RAG，无需大规模预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RAG 系统通常假设语言模型和嵌入模型需要大规模预训练和显式的 grounding 监督，导致资源消耗大。作者旨在探索通过策略性模型组合（融合预训练编码器）和精心设计的奖励函数（仅优化答案相似度），在极低计算成本下实现高性能 RAG，并验证 grounding 行为是否能作为准确性最大化策略自发涌现。

Method: 1. B1ade-embed：采用无参数融合（parameter-free fusion）将五个预训练编码器组合成 335M 参数的检索模型，零额外训练即在 MTEB 上达到 sub-500M 顶尖分数。2. B1ade-1B：使用 Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) 在 723M tokens（2.2M 个精选 context-question 对）上训练 1B 参数 SLM，奖励函数仅优化答案相似度，无显式引用监督。3. 在 PopQA、PubMedQA、FEVER 等标准 QA 基准及端到端 RAG 评估（正确性、完整性、连贯性、忠实性）上测试。

Result: • B1ade-embed 零训练下在 MTEB 上超越所有 sub-500M 模型。• B1ade-1B 涌现式归因率 42.4%，比训练分布高 5.5 个百分点。• QA 基准：PopQA 81.82%，PubMedQA 65.8%，FEVER 51.09%。• 端到端 RAG 平均分 0.654，比 SFT 提升 10.8%，缩小与 1.5 倍参数模型的差距。

Conclusion: 研究表明，通过策略性模型组合（融合预训练编码器）和简单的奖励设计（仅答案相似度），即可在极低计算预算下训练出高性能 RAG 系统。Grounding 行为可作为准确性最大化的副产品自发涌现，无需显式引用监督或大规模预训练，为资源受限场景下的 RAG 部署提供了新范式。

Abstract: Language and embedding models used in RAG systems are conventionally assumed to require large-scale pretraining and explicit grounding supervision. We present B1ade, an efficient RAG architecture comprising two purpose-built components: a compact embedding model and a purpose-built SLM. B1ade-embed, a 335M parameter retrieval model constructed via parameter-free fusion of five pretrained encoders achieves top MTEB scores among sub-500M models with zero additional training, and B1ade-1B, an SLM trained on low-cost GPUs using Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) on 723M tokens (2.2M examples) of curated context-question pairs with rewards that optimize only answer similarity. Our central finding is emergent attribution: despite receiving no explicit supervision for source citation, B1ade-1B cites retrieved passages in 42.4% of responses, exceeding the attribution rate of its training distribution by 5.5 percentage points. This demonstrates that grounding behavior can emerge as an accuracy-maximizing strategy under RL training, without explicit reward engineering. On standard QA benchmarks, B1ade-1B achieves 81.82% on PopQA, 65.8% on PubMedQA, and 51.09% on FEVER. In end-to-end RAG evaluation, B1ade-1B achieves an average score of 0.654 across correctness, completeness, coherence, and faithfulness, a 10.8% improvement over the SFT, while closing the gap with models 1.5x its size. These results show that strategic model composition and reward design suffice for resource-efficient RAG, without large-scale pretraining.

</details>


### [59] [Beyond Similarity: Grounded Agentic Extraction and Expert-Adjudicated Evaluation of Intertextuality in Classical Chinese Histories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27595)
*Zhaoji Wang,Wanyu Si,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的代理式细粒度互文性抽取方法，将互文性标注为五维类型（形式、层面、来源标记、功能、立场），并在《论语》与《汉书》的详尽比对上构建了包含2,533对互文的专家裁决基准。实验评估12个LLM，精度56%-93%，成本差异51倍，并揭示了表层维度标注一致性高、意图推断维度争议大的可靠性梯度。将方法扩展至《二十四史》（65,380次比对，5,766对互文）发现：引用的诠释构成在十八个世纪中整体稳定，但逐字引用逐渐减少，符合文化吸引力理论的预期。作者发布了抽取协议与专家基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算互文性方法（字符串匹配、神经检索）仅输出相似度分数或平行段落列表，只能定位“哪里”复用，无法刻画“如何”与“为何”复用，缺乏对复用形式、功能、立场等细粒度语用维度的建模。

Method: 将细粒度互文性抽取建模为代理任务：LLM通读两段文本，通过受限工具接口在双方精确定位字符级跨度，并按五维类型（形式、层面、来源标记、功能、立场）标注每一复用实例。在《论语》与《汉书》的全量比对上，汇集多模型候选，经三位领域专家裁决构建2,533对基准；评估12个LLM的精度、成本、置信度校准；再将验证后的抽取器扩展至《二十四史》全集。

Result: 12个LLM精度在56%-93%之间，同等质量下成本跨度51倍；专家一致性显示表层维度（形式、层面）标注一致，意图维度（功能、立场、来源标记）争议大。全史料规模实验揭示：引用的诠释构成十八世纪整体稳定，但逐字引用比例持续下降，呈现“整体稳定、个体漂移”模式，符合文化吸引力理论。

Conclusion: 代理式LLM互文性抽取能以字符级证据支撑五维类型标注，超越相似度分数提供可解释的复用图景。基准与协议的开放将推动计算互文性从“定位”走向“诠释”，为大规模文献学研究提供可复用的方法论基础设施。

Abstract: Computational approaches to intertextuality have advanced from string matching to neural retrieval, yet their outputs, similarity scores and parallel-passage lists, identify where texts reuse one another without characterizing how or why. We recast fine-grained intertextuality extraction as an agentic task in which a large language model (LLM) reads two text units in full and, through a constrained tool interface, must ground each proposed reuse in exact character spans on both sides and label it under a five-dimension typology of reuse (form, aspect, source-marking, function, stance). We validate the approach on an exhaustive comparison of the Analects with the Book of Han, where three domain experts adjudicate a pooled multi-model candidate set into a benchmark of 2,533 intertextual pairs. Against this standard we study twelve LLMs, reporting precision (56%-93%), a 51$\times$ cost spread at comparable quality, and how well their confidence is calibrated. Expert agreement traces a reliability gradient: dimensions legible on the textual surface are annotated consistently, while those requiring inference of intent are contested, delimiting the claims such annotation supports. Scaling the validated extractor to the full Twenty-Four Histories (65,380 comparisons, 5,766 pairs) recovers corpus-level structure a similarity score cannot express. The interpretive composition of citation shows no systematic change across eighteen centuries, yet the same passage is quoted less and less literally. Stability in the aggregate with drift in the individual case is what a cultural-attraction account expects. We release the extraction protocol and the expert-adjudicated benchmark.

</details>


### [60] [From Single- to Cross-Document: Benchmarking Multi-Granularity Event Analysis of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27654)
*Tao Wen,Shuai Shao,Pei Ke,Xu Han,Jie Zou,Guannan Li,Tao Tian,Jinjie Qiu,Lan Wang,Ke Qin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MiGUE-Bench，一个用于评估大语言模型在多粒度事件分析能力的系统性基准，包含自纠正标注流水线MiGUE-Pipeline和四大核心任务，实验揭示了当前LLM和RAG方法的能力边界与不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准在文档粒度、任务设计和数据来源上存在局限，导致对LLM事件分析能力的综合理解不足。

Method: 首先开发基于LLM的自纠正标注框架MiGUE-Pipeline，实现高质量事件数据的可扩展获取；随后设计四大核心任务：事件检测、关系推理、结构归纳和未来预测，从原子事件细节到跨文档复杂叙事多层次探测模型能力。

Result: 在最先进LLM和RAG方法上进行大量实验，勾勒出当前能力边界，识别出关键缺陷。

Conclusion: MiGUE-Bench为未来改进LLM在具有挑战性的事件分析任务上的表现提供了洞见。

Abstract: Event analysis is an essential and fundamental direction of information extraction, involving various event-centric tasks at different granularity of documents. While large language models (LLMs) have preliminarily achieved promising performance in part of these tasks individually, their capability in event analysis still lacks comprehensive understanding due to restricted document granularity, task designs, and data source of existing benchmarks. To address these limitations, we introduce MiGUE-Bench, a systematic benchmark for assessing the performance of LLMs in multi-granularity event analysis. To support large-scale evaluation, we first develop an LLM-driven self-correcting annotation framework called MiGUE-Pipeline, enabling scalable acquisition of high-quality source data of events with automatic labels. Then, we design four core tasks in our benchmark, i.e., event detection, relation reasoning, structure induction, and future prediction, to probe model competence at different levels, from atomic event details to complex cross-document narratives. Extensive experiments on state-of-the-art LLMs and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) methods delineate the current capability boundary and identify critical deficiencies, providing insights into the future improvement of LLMs in challenging event analysis tasks.

</details>


### [61] [Can LVLMs Uncover the Truth Behind Visual Illusions? An Analysis of Perceptual and Reasoning Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27747)
*Liangjie Zhao,Jiaqing Lyu,Kexin Tang,Zecheng Fang,Rong Yin,Yulan Hu,Da Li,Jianing Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出IllusionReasoning基准，利用视觉错觉作为诊断工具，评估大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）在开放世界环境中感知与推理的联合能力，发现现有LVLMs的推理能力被高估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LVLMs评估要么仅关注感知，要么依赖数学/编程等特定领域，缺乏面向开放世界、联合考察感知与推理的评估体系。

Method: 构建IllusionReasoning基准，收集真实世界视觉错觉图像，标注多样化问答对，以此作为诊断工具评估多种LVLMs。

Result: 实验表明，广泛LVLMs在IllusionReasoning上的推理表现不如宣称先进，暴露出感知与推理联合能力的不足。

Conclusion: 视觉错觉可作为有效诊断工具揭示LVLMs局限，为未来模型优化提供新方向。

Abstract: Large Vision Language Models have integrated reasoning capabilities, elevating cognitive performance to new levels. However, existing evaluations either focus solely on perception or rely on specific domains such as maths or coding. Evaluation for reasoning capabilities that align with an open-world environment is still required, especially one that considers perception and reasoning jointly. To bridge this gap, we propose to evaluate LVLMs by exploiting visual illusions as a diagnostic tool. Visual illusions are phenomena in which the human visual system misinterprets objective signals, resulting in an understanding that deviates from reality. We constructed IllusionReasoning, a benchmark of illusion images collected from the real world, incorporating diverse annotated question-answer pairs. Based on IllusionReasoning, we show that the reasoning capabilities of a wide range of LVLMs are not as advanced as claimed. Our work provides new insights into LVLMs and offers future direction for optimisation.

</details>

### [62] [OneShot: Index-in-Ranking with Neural Scoring for Large-Scale Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27475)
*Ziwei Li,Shuyao Li,Xufeng Cai,Xue Zou,Yiming Ma,Huiting Lu,Wujie Yan,Zhichen Zhao,Yang Lu,Zhe Wang,Rui Luo,Zhengyu Su,Dan Zhang,Ji Liu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: OneShot提出了一种端到端的索引学习框架，统一了检索阶段的排序目标与索引效率，解决了传统检索中排序准确性与索引效率的错位问题，在Instagram短视频推荐系统中显著提升用户指标和召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代推荐系统中检索阶段需从海量候选中筛选，传统方法中排序目标优化用户行为预测，而索引优化结构分组以实现快速搜索，两者目标错位，限制了交互建模的扩展。

Method: 提出OneShot框架，一种端到端的模型内索引学习方法，将索引学习与排序目标原生对齐，利用联合学习作为结构基础，突破点积瓶颈，扩展神经评分的交互建模。

Result: 在Instagram短视频推荐系统全面部署，用户日活、互动、时长显著提升；在运行排序量级下召回率提升20%，同等召回下效率提升10倍。

Conclusion: OneShot成功解决了检索阶段排序与索引的长期二分问题，通过端到端联合学习实现了高效高效的检索，工业部署验证了其有效性。

Abstract: In modern recommendation systems, retrieval serves as a primary stage responsible for filtering billions of candidate items down to thousands prior to refined ranking. To make this massive search effective and efficient, the system relies on ranking accuracy and indexing efficiency. However, these two objectives are traditionally misaligned: while the former optimizes for the alignment between ranking predictions and user behavior, the latter optimizes for a structural grouping of item representations which enables fast search among billions of candidates. Thus, despite extensive efforts to scale up interaction modeling for retrieval, they remain fundamentally limited by the structural misalignment between the ranking objectives and the proximity-learned index. In this work, we address this long-standing dichotomy by proposing a new holistic retrieval framework, OneShot. It is an end-to-end, in-model index learning framework that natively aligns index learning with ranking objectives. Using this joint learning as a structural foundation, OneShot pushes the boundaries of retrieval expressiveness by scaling interaction modeling with neural scoring beyond the persistent dot-product bottleneck. OneShot is fully deployed in Instagram's industrial short-video recommendation system, driving significant wins in user daily sessions, engagement, and time-spent. Additionally, OneShot achieves a $20\%$ recall gain at the operational ranking volume and a 10x efficiency improvement at an equivalent recall level.

</details>


### [63] [Hierarchical Reranking for Scalable Financial RAG System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27523)
*Joohyun Lee,Sungwoo Hong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 Hierarchical Reranker，一种面向大规模金融文档的 RAG 框架，通过预检索优化、分层重排序架构和长上下文管理三大创新，显著提升检索精度与生成可靠性，在 FinQA、FinanceBench、ConvFinQA 等基准上达到 NDCG@20 0.7918，并在 ACM-ICAIF '24 FinanceRAG Challenge 中获第二名，代码即将开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RAG 系统难以处理金融文档中的混合文本-表格结构及海量规模，导致检索性能与生成可靠性不足，亟需领域优化的检索增强生成管线以支持自动化审计报告与量化投资分析。

Method: Hierarchical Reranker 框架包含三大创新：1) 预检索优化，通过归一化、关键词扩展与表格转换提升查询清晰度与检索效率；2) 分层重排序架构，采用两阶段排序机制提高检索精度；3) 长上下文管理，利用自适应输入分区与融合在大规模上下文下保持推理准确性。

Result: 在 FinQA、FinanceBench、ConvFinQA 等多个基准上，NDCG@20 达到 0.7918，事实一致性优于基线；在 ACM-ICAIF '24 FinanceRAG Challenge 中获得第二名，验证了系统的鲁棒性与可部署性。

Conclusion: 本工作为大规模金融推理提供了一个可部署、领域优化的 RAG 管线，兼顾准确性与可扩展性，为自动化审计报告与量化投资分析奠定基础。

Abstract: Analyzing financial documents such as 10-K filings, tabular disclosures, and macroeconomic reports demands expert reasoning and extensive time. However, existing Retrieval-Augmented Generation systems often struggle to process hybrid text-table structures or the massive scale of financial documents. To address these challenges, we propose Hierarchical Reranker, a RAG framework designed to improve retrieval performance and generative reliability across large-scale financial datasets. The system integrates three key innovations: Pre-Retrieval Optimization, enhancing query clarity and search efficiency through normalization, keyword expansion, and table transformation; Hierarchical Reranker Architecture, improving retrieval precision through a two-stage ranking mechanism; and Long-Context Management, preserving reasoning accuracy through adaptive input partitioning and fusion under extensive contexts. Across multiple benchmarks, including FinQA, FinanceBench, and ConvFinQA, the proposed system achieved an NDCG@20 score of 0.7918 and demonstrated superior factual consistency. Its robustness was further validated by achieving second place in the ACM-ICAIF '24 FinanceRAG Challenge. This work presents a deployable, domain-optimized RAG pipeline that enhances both the accuracy and scalability of financial reasoning, paving the way for automated audit reporting and quantitative investment analysis. The source code will be made publicly available on GitHub upon acceptance.

</details>


### [64] [Heterogeneous Ranking in Industrial-Scale Recommender Systems: A Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27577)
*Di Bai,Jintao Liu,Zhenwei Tang,Peifan Wu,Nada Al-Thawr,Luoshu Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文针对工业级异构推荐流（如Google Discover）中的多任务排序问题，提出了HA-MoE（异构自适应多门控混合专家架构）和LENS轻量级可观测框架，通过显式引入异构上下文实现专家专业化，并设计DL-AUC评估指标。离线实验和在线A/B测试均验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构推荐流整合了网页文章、长短视频、UGC等多种内容类型，不同内容的特征密度和用户交互模式差异大。现有统一排序模型难以在异构环境下保持高性能，易出现负迁移或多数类偏差，亟需一种能显式建模异构性、实现专家专业化且运维开销可控的工业级解决方案。

Method: 1. HA-MoE：异构自适应多门控混合专家架构，将显式异构上下文注入门控网络和专家表示，实现跨内容类型的有效专业化，且不显著增加运维开销。2. LENS：轻量级可观测框架，提供专家专业化的可解释诊断，并在持续重训练中追踪功能异构性。3. DL-AUC（双层AUC）：异构感知评估指标，融合全局排序性能与跨片段排序正确性。

Result: 在大规模工业数据集上的离线评估显示，HA-MoE在DL-AUC指标上持续优于基线模型；在线A/B测试确认了Feed活跃度和探索指标的显著提升。离线与在线结果共同验证了该方法在管理工业级推荐系统异构性方面的有效性。

Conclusion: HA-MoE通过显式异构上下文建模实现了专家专业化，配合LENS可观测框架保障部署可靠性，DL-AUC指标提供了异构感知的评估视角。该端到端方案成功解决了工业级异构推荐流中的负迁移和多数类偏差问题，经离线在线双重验证，可作为大规模异构推荐系统的实用范式。

Abstract: Heterogeneous recommendation feeds present complex challenges that extend beyond those found in highly homogeneous environments (e.g., music-only or video-only closed-ecosystem platforms). In Google Discover, a unified feed integrates diverse content sourced from the decentralized open web, including web articles, long-form and short-form videos, user-generated content (UGC), and beyond. Different content types exhibit distinct feature densities and user interaction patterns. Building a unified ranking model that sustains high performance across such heterogeneity, while avoiding negative transfer or majority bias, remains a significant industrial challenge.
  This paper presents an end-to-end case study on the industrial-scale multi-task ranking of heterogeneous feeds, grounded in real-world deployment. We introduce HA-MoE, a heterogeneity-adaptive multi-gated mixture-of-experts architecture that incorporates explicit heterogeneity context into both gating networks and expert representations. This approach enables effective specialization without significantly increasing operational overhead. To support reliable deployment, we introduce LENS, a lightweight observability framework that provides interpretable diagnostics of expert specialization and tracks this functional heterogeneity across continuous retraining. We evaluate our method using Dual-Level AUC (DL-AUC), a heterogeneity-aware evaluation metric that combines global ranking performance with cross-segment ranking correctness. Offline evaluations on a large-scale industrial dataset demonstrate consistent improvements over baseline models. Furthermore, online A/B testing confirms gains in feed activity and exploration metrics. Together, offline and online results validate the effectiveness of our approach for managing heterogeneity in industrial-scale recommender systems.

</details>


### [65] [An Exploration Graph with Continuous Refinement for Efficient Multimedia Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27623)
*Nico Hezel,Kai Uwe Barthel,Konstantin Schall,Klaus Jung*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种“连续细化探索图”（crEG），用于快速构建紧凑探索图，实现最先进的搜索性能，并提供可选的边优化算法。该方法保证图连通性，适用于“探索性搜索”场景，实验表明ANNS的高效率不一定意味着探索性搜索性能良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数据集和特征向量维度的增长，大型多媒体数据库中的近似最近邻搜索（ANNS）变得越来越重要。基于图的方法在检索精度和搜索时间之间提供了最佳权衡，但现有方法存在构建速度慢或内存需求高的问题。此外，探索性搜索（查询是数据库元素之一）在推荐和探索系统中至关重要，但在ANNS背景下很少被考虑。

Method: 提出了一种“连续细化探索图”（crEG），用于快速构建紧凑探索图，并提供可选的边优化算法。两种算法专门设计用于生成和操作无向图，保证偶数度和图连通性，适用于探索性搜索。

Result: 实验表明，crEG在ANNS中实现了最先进的搜索性能，同时构建速度快、内存占用低。边优化算法可进一步提升效果。实验还表明，ANNS的高效率不一定转化为良好的探索性搜索性能。

Conclusion: crEG为ANNS提供了一种高效、紧凑的图构建方法，特别适合探索性搜索场景，填补了现有方法在探索性搜索方面的空白，展示了图连通性和偶数度对探索性搜索的重要性。

Abstract: As datasets and the dimensionality of feature vectors continue to grow, Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search (ANNS) in large multimedia databases becomes increasingly relevant. Graph-based approaches have demonstrated to offer the best trade-off between retrieval precision and search time. Despite their ability to deliver search times several orders of magnitude faster than exact search techniques, existing methods suffer from slow constructions speeds or high memory requirements. This paper presents a "continuous refining Exploration Graph" (crEG), a novel approach for rapidly constructing a compact exploration graph with state-of-the-art search performance. Additionally, it provides the ability to enhance its effectiveness even further through an optional edge optimization algorithm. Both algorithms are specifically designed to produce and operate on undirected graphs with even degrees and guarantee graph connectivity at any time, a property particularly valuable for "exploratory search", where the query is part of the database elements. Although such queries provide an advantageous starting point for graph search algorithms, they have been rarely considered in the context of ANNS, yet are crucial for recommendation and exploration systems. Our experiments demonstrate high efficiency in ANNS does not necessarily translate to a good performance in "exploratory search".

</details>


### [66] [Dynamic Exploration Graph: A Novel Approach for Efficient Nearest Neighbor Search in Evolving Multimedia Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27640)
*Nico Hezel,Kai Uwe Barthel,Bruno Schilling,Konstantin Schall,Klaus Jung*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出动态探索图（DEG），通过保证图连通性的顶点删除算法和数据分布无关的图扩展方法，解决动态数据集下的近似最近邻搜索问题，在流式和在线场景下优于现有动态图算法，且在静态数据集上媲美最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近似最近邻搜索（ANNS）在图像搜索、推荐系统等应用中至关重要。基于图的算法在静态数据集上平衡了搜索精度和时间，但难以处理数据点持续增删的动态数据集。现有动态图算法在构建时间和搜索效率上仍有不足，需要一种既能高效支持动态更新，又能保持静态数据集高性能的方法。

Method: 提出动态探索图（DEG），基于持续细化探索图扩展。核心创新包括：1）保证图连通性的新颖顶点删除算法；2）数据分布无关的图扩展方法。通过这两个机制，DEG 在持续数据变更下保持平衡且连接良好的结构。

Result: 在流式和在线场景的实证实验中，DEG 在构建时间和搜索效率上均超越现有动态图算法。尽管针对动态数据集优化，DEG 在静态数据集上的表现也与当前最先进方法相当。

Conclusion: DEG 通过创新的顶点删除和图扩展机制，有效解决了动态数据集下的近似最近邻搜索难题，兼具动态更新效率和静态搜索性能，展现出广泛的适用性。

Abstract: Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search (ANNS) represents a fundamental problem in various applications (image-search, recommendation systems). While graph-based algorithms have demonstrated a good balance between search accuracy and time, handling dynamic datasets, where data points are continuously added or removed, remains a challenge. This paper introduces the Dynamic Exploration Graph (DEG), an extension of the continuous refining Exploration Graph, which retains high search efficiency for static dataset while adding essential support for dynamic data. At the core of the DEG design are two key innovations: a novel vertex deletion algorithm which guarantees graph connectivity and a data distribution-agnostic method for graph expansion. Through these mechanisms, the DEG maintains a balanced and well-connected structure, even under continuous data alterations. Empirical experiments in both streaming and online scenarios demonstrate the superior performance of the DEG, surpassing existing dynamic graph algorithms in terms of construction time and search efficiency. Although optimized for dynamic datasets, the DEG delivers results as good as current state-of-the-art approaches for static dataset, underscoring its broad applicability.

</details>


### [67] [LoopMemGR: From Behavior Logs to Evolving Memory for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27647)
*Hui Qian,Changfa Wu,Chang Liu,Binbin Cao,Jian Wu,Yuliang Yan,Han Zhu,Bo Zheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: LoopMemGR 提出了一种闭环推荐经验记忆框架，通过维护推荐经验日志并从时效、频率、全局三视角提取经验，解决生成式推荐中系统侧决策记忆缺失的问题，在淘宝工业数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐采用历史即上下文范式，仅利用用户行为历史重构偏好，丢弃系统推荐决策及反馈，导致系统记忆不对称：记住用户做过什么，却不记得推荐过什么及反馈结果，无法跨请求复用偏好验证信号、负样本证据和探索信息。

Method: LoopMemGR 除保留常规行为日志外，维护推荐经验日志记录推荐-反馈轨迹。通过三视角提取请求相关证据：时效视角捕捉短期交互动态，频率视角总结重复推荐模式，全局视角提炼跨用户可迁移规律。将这些信号压缩为固定数量的经验 Token，在有限输入预算下条件化生成骨干网络。

Result: 在工业级淘宝数据集上的大量实验表明，闭环经验积累与多视角经验提取显著提升了生成式推荐性能，验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: LoopMemGR 通过引入系统侧推荐经验记忆并多视角压缩为经验 Token，弥补了生成式推荐中系统决策记忆的缺失，实现了跨请求的经验复用，为大规模离散语义 ID 生成式推荐提供了新的闭环记忆范式。

Abstract: Generative recommendation formulates next-item prediction as conditional autoregressive generation over discrete Semantic IDs, enabling end-to-end recommendation over large-scale item spaces. However, most existing methods follow a history-as-context paradigm that repeatedly reconstructs user preference from behavior history while discarding system-side recommendation decisions after each request. This creates an asymmetric memory: the system remembers what the user has done, but not what it has previously recommended or learned from the resulting feedback. Consequently, useful preference-validation signals, potential negative evidence, and historical exploration information cannot be directly reused across requests. To address these limitations, we propose LoopMemGR, a closed-loop recommendation experience memory framework for generative recommendation. In addition to the conventional behavior log, LoopMemGR maintains a recommendation experience log that records past recommendation--feedback trajectories. It extracts request-relevant evidence through three complementary views: the recency view captures short-term interaction dynamics, the frequency view summarizes recurring recommendation patterns, and the global view distills transferable regularities shared across users. These signals are compressed into a fixed number of experience tokens to condition the generative backbone under a bounded input budget. Extensive experiments on an industrial Taobao dataset demonstrate the effectiveness of closed-loop experience accumulation and multi-view experience extraction.

</details>


### [68] [Restoring Collaborative Signals in Semantic-ID Generative Recommendation via Personalized Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27682)
*Changjiang Han,Qingyang Li,Yaqiang Zang,Jikun Kang,Pinghua Gong,Xue Liu,Bowei He*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于个性化自然语言的框架，在生成语义ID（SID）时注入层级协同线索，无需重训练即可持续提升推荐准确性，解决了SID无法同时承载内容与协同信号的根本问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生成式推荐模型将推荐建模为自回归生成SID，但存在文本与SID嵌入空间错位、显性推理难以转化为正确SID、紧凑SID无法同时保持内容与协同信号（两者竞争导致协同信号丢失）、多轮训练代价高且推理时缺乏协同信号补充等问题，直接限制了推荐准确性。

Method: 提出一种由个性化自然语言引导的框架，在生成过程中动态添加层级协同线索，不修改骨干网络也不重训练SID。核心思路是利用语言建立协同模式与其受众之间可分析的关联，而非直接将语言映射到SID，从而在推理阶段恢复SID缺失的协同信号。

Result: 在推荐准确性上取得一致性增益，实现了推理时基于协同结构的生成定向，而非依赖显性推理或重训练。

Conclusion: 通过在生成过程中注入层级协同线索，该框架有效解决了SID无法同时承载内容与协同信号的根本矛盾，为LLM生成式推荐提供了一种免重训练、推理时增强协同信号的实用路径。

Abstract: Making LLM-based generative recommendation models stronger and more personalized through natural language and explicit reasoning is a widely anticipated yet still unsolved goal.
