Such models cast recommendation as autoregressively generating an item's semantic-ID (SID), a short tuple of discrete codes, so that recommending well reduces to emitting the right SID. In this setting the model verbalizes its knowledge poorly, and text and SID tokens live in misaligned embedding spaces. Deep reasoning therefore rarely turns into a correct SID, and enabling explicit "thinking" often gives no gain or even hurts. The deeper cause is that a compact SID cannot hold content and collaborative signal at once: the two compete, and collaboration loses. Because a mis-predicted SID is a wrong recommendation, this caps accuracy directly. Costly multi-round training barely helps, and few methods try to supply the missing signal at inference time. What is missing is a reliable channel that carries collaborative signal into SID generation. We therefore propose a framework, guided by personalized natural language, that adds hierarchical collaborative cues as the model generates, without altering the backbone or retraining the SIDs. Rather than mapping language onto SIDs directly, it uses language to attach analyzable links between collaborative patterns and their audiences, restoring the collaborative signal that SIDs miss. The result is consistent gains in recommendation accuracy, grounding generation in collaborative structure at inference time rather than relying on explicit reasoning or retraining.

</details>


### [69] [Measuring Alignment With Reader Highlights Net of Position and Length](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27739)
*Kazuki Nakayashiki,Keisuke Watanabe*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于自然社交高亮（多人独立标注）的非循环评估方法，用于评价上下文压缩模型保留重要内容的能力。通过匹配控制句子位置和长度的混淆因子，并利用合成零假设校准，发现大语言模型重要性排序显著优于随机基线，其表现与人类读者相当，且优于经典启发式方法。同时指出先前工作中的某项声明在该语料库上无法复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文压缩评估依赖下游任务准确率，存在循环论证问题（用另一个模型判断什么重要）。自然社交高亮提供非循环基准，但直接用保留的众标句子比例作为指标受两大混淆因子影响：众标句子多集中在文档前部（位置偏向），且众标句子通常较长（长度偏向），导致任何偏好早句或长句的方法都能虚高得分。

Method: 1. 构建匹配对：将每个众标句子与同一文档中相对深度相同、文内长度排名相同的非众标句子配对，消除位置和长度混淆。2. 合成零假设校准：仅用位置和长度生成合成零分布，校准每个估计量，发现仅按深度分层会在20-36%的无效应零假设中产生假阳性。3. 在120篇网页文档（每篇≥12位独立读者）上实验，使用精确随机化检验（无聚类假设）并跨厂商复现。

Result: 大语言模型重要性排序保留38.4%的众标句子，而匹配邻居仅19.9%，富集度+0.196 [95% CI +0.148, +0.239]，p=0.0005。朴素截断（仅按位置）富集度仅+0.003。单人类读者富集度+0.182，与GPT-5.4（+0.002 [-0.081, +0.088]）无显著差异，低于Claude Opus 5。经典Luhn 1958启发式达+0.088，说明读者选择部分可通过词频恢复；加入词汇中心性仅额外解释0.010，表明一致性非源于中心性。先前工作中的某项声明在该语料库上无法复现。

Conclusion: 提出的匹配控制+合成零校准框架有效消除了位置和长度混淆，为上下文压缩提供了可靠的非循环评估基准。大语言模型重要性排序在保留人类共同关注内容方面已达人类水平，且显著优于经典启发式方法。研究还揭示了读者选择偏好主要可由简单词频特征捕获，而非语义中心性。

Abstract: Context compression discards most of a document before a language model reads it, and is normally evaluated by downstream task accuracy - which makes another model the judge of what mattered. Naturalistic social highlighting offers a non-circular reference: many people independently marking passages on the same page. But the obvious metric, the fraction of crowd-marked sentences a compressor keeps, is confounded twice: crowd marks are front-loaded and crowd-marked sentences are longer, so any method favouring early or long sentences scores well regardless of readers. We remove both by matching each marked sentence against unmarked sentences of the same document at equal relative depth and equal within-document length rank, and we calibrate every estimator on synthetic nulls built from position and length alone - a step that matters, since depth-only stratification returns a false positive on 20-36% of nulls containing no effect. On 120 web documents (at least 12 independent readers each), a language-model importance ranking keeps 38.4% of crowd-marked sentences against 19.9% of their matched neighbours: an enrichment of +0.196 [+0.148, +0.239], at p = 0.0005 under an exact randomization test that assumes nothing about clustering, and replicated cross-vendor. Naive truncation, whose keep rule is position, correctly falls to +0.003. To give the number a scale: scored identically, on the same budget, against a crowd label recomputed to exclude them, a single human reader reaches +0.182 - indistinguishable from GPT-5.4 (+0.002 [-0.081, +0.088]) and below Claude Opus 5. Classical methods are not null - Luhn's 1958 heuristic reaches +0.088 - so reader selection is partly recoverable by counting words; conditioning additionally on lexical centrality removes only 0.010, so the agreement is not centrality. We also report that a claim in our own prior work does not reproduce on this corpus.

</details>


### [70] [A Structured Knowledge Infrastructure for Domain-Specific Data Asset Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27748)
*Mengdi Chen,Yuanxin Huang,Yulin Jiang,Wei Sun*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文针对企业数据分析智能体中通用RAG检索错误资产和缺乏使用知识导致指标误解的问题，提出了在小红书商业广告数仓部署的双层解决方案：三层双用途知识库、图引导检索器(GGR)和场景感知排序器(SAR)，将Hit@10从19.1%提升至96.6%，知识覆盖率从56%提升至77%，端到端延迟仅4.84-5.33秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业数据分析智能体面临两大结构性失效：通用RAG检索错误资产(Hit@10仅19.1%)且无法提供使用知识防止指标误解，根因包括语义鸿沟、实体歧义、模式漂移和资产-使用鸿沟四大问题(C1-C4)。

Method: 提出两层解决方案：1)三层双用途知识库(179文档，八节点标注模板)服务检索和生成，具备闭环刷新流水线(日级新鲜度，一键审批，30秒热重载)；2)图引导检索器(GGR)利用2859节点知识图谱作为候选门控配合意图路由，实现71.6倍Token压缩；3)场景感知排序器(SAR)应用19类实体识别和显式场景标注，负面知识单独贡献25个百分点Hit@10增益。

Result: 在两个100题基准测试上，Hit@10从19.1%跃升至96.6%(提升77.5个百分点)，知识覆盖率从56%提升至77%，端到端延迟仅4.84-5.33秒。

Conclusion: 通过构建双用途知识库、图引导检索和场景感知排序的组合技术，有效解决了企业级数据分析中的资产检索准确性和使用知识缺失问题，在真实生产环境中实现了显著的性能提升和低延迟部署。

Abstract: Enterprise data analytics agents face two structural failures: generic RAG retrieves the wrong asset (Hit@10=19.1%) and delivers no usage knowledge to prevent metric misinterpretation---stemming from four root causes (C1--C4) ranging from semantic gap and entity ambiguity to schema drift and asset-usage gap. We present a two-layer solution deployed in the commercial advertising data warehouse at Xiaohongshu (5,300+ Hive tables, 14 domains). A three-tier dual-purpose knowledge base (179 documents, eight-section annotation template) serves both retrieval and generation, with a closed-loop refresh pipeline maintaining day-level freshness (one yes/no approval, 30s hot-reload). The Graph-Guided Retriever (GGR) uses a 2,859-node knowledge graph as a candidate gate with intent routing to deliver 71.6x token reduction. The Scene-Aware Ranker (SAR) applies 19-class entity recognition and explicit scenario annotations; negative knowledge alone contributes 25 percentage points of Hit@10 gain. On two 100-question benchmarks, Hit@10 rises from 19.1% to 96.6% (+77.5pp) and knowledge coverage from 56% to 77%, at 4.84--5.33s end-to-end latency.

</details>


### [71] [Hierarchical Latent Reasoning for LLM-based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27760)
*Peiyu Hu,Siying Gu,Weihai Lu,Zhuodong Liu,Yuntian Tang,Jiahao Liang,Yiying Xie,Jiang Rong,Zhaokai Luo,Zhiyong Wang,Jia Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出HiLaR框架，通过层次化潜在推理与层感知强化优化提升LLM推荐性能，在四个Amazon数据集上超越强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推荐中显式自然语言推理推理开销大，而潜在推理方法仅关注中间状态生成或验证，缺乏对各层偏好角色及贡献的刻画。

Method: HiLaR构建时序引导的层次化用户偏好表示，与多LLM潜在推理状态对齐，将推理过程组织为从宽泛偏好到细粒度当前意图；结合最终推荐反馈与基于边际目标似然增益的层感知过程奖励，优化推理轨迹。

Result: 在四个Amazon基准数据集上，HiLaR普遍优于强序列、生成及LLM推荐基线；消融与敏感性分析验证了层次化表征学习、潜在对齐及过程级优化的贡献。

Conclusion: HiLaR通过层次化潜在推理与层感知强化优化，有效解决了LLM推荐中的推理开销与层级贡献不清问题，显著提升了推荐效果。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong potential for recommendation by leveraging their semantic understanding and contextual modeling capabilities. Recent studies further introduce reasoning mechanisms to improve user preference modeling. However, explicit natural-language reasoning incurs substantial inference overhead, whereas existing latent reasoning methods mainly focus on generating or verifying intermediate states, leaving their layer-wise preference roles and contributions insufficiently characterized. We propose HiLaR, a Hierarchical Latent Reasoning framework with layer-aware reinforcement optimization for LLM-based recommendation. HiLaR constructs temporal-guided hierarchical user preference representations, aligns them with multiple LLM latent reasoning states, and organizes the reasoning process from broad preferences to fine-grained current intents. To further optimize the reasoning trajectory, HiLaR combines final recommendation feedback with layer-aware process rewards derived from the marginal target-likelihood gain of each state. Experiments on four Amazon benchmark datasets show that HiLaR generally outperforms strong sequential, generative, and LLM-based recommendation baselines. Ablation and sensitivity analyses further verify the contribution of hierarchical representation learning, latent alignment, and process-level optimization. Our code is available in https://github.com/hupeiyu21/HiLaR.

</details>


### [72] [From Understanding to Action: Feedback-Grounded Policy Discovery for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27789)
*Zhi Chen,Minmao Wang,Xingchen Liu,Haoqiang Liang,Huihuang Lin,Likang Wu,Hongke Zhao,Yulong Wang,Shijie Yi,Fei Pan,Peng Jiang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于反馈驱动的智能体框架，通过分离意图知识与策略知识，利用结果反馈优化推荐策略，并通过双空间关系蒸馏将其迁移至轻量级语义ID生成器，实现无LLM在线推理，在公开基准和大规模在线A/B测试中显著提升收入和ADVV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义ID生成式推荐模型主要捕捉行为共现和局部转移，而大语言模型（LLM）虽能推理异构交互历史以理解用户需求，但缺乏推荐特定的结果反馈训练，导致语言上合理的推理不一定能转化为有效的推荐决策，即存在“理解-行动鸿沟”。

Method: 提出反馈驱动智能体框架：首先诱导任务导向的意图，再根据相对于仅意图基线的增量效用发现推荐策略；候选策略基于结果反馈而非语言合理性进行评估和优化；最后通过双空间关系蒸馏将意图和策略知识迁移到轻量级语义ID生成器的两个潜在token中，实现无LLM在线推理。

Result: 在公开基准上持续优于基线模型；大规模在线A/B测试中，Revenue提升4.506%，ADVV提升4.621%。

Conclusion: 通过将意图知识与策略知识解耦，并利用结果反馈驱动的策略发现与蒸馏机制，有效弥合了LLM理解能力与推荐决策之间的鸿沟，显著提升了推荐系统的在线业务指标。

Abstract: Semantic-ID-based generative recommenders enable efficient next-item generation, but their item-level supervision mainly captures behavioral co-occurrence and local transitions. Large language models (LLMs) can complement these models by reasoning over heterogeneous interaction histories to understand the user's current demand. However, LLMs are not inherently trained with recommendation-specific outcome feedback, and linguistically plausible reasoning therefore does not necessarily lead to effective recommendation decisions. We term this mismatch the Understanding-Action Gap. Accordingly, we distinguish intent knowledge, which captures the user's current demand, from policy knowledge, which specifies the recommendation direction and rejection boundary under that demand. To bridge this gap, we propose a feedback-driven agent framework that first induces task-oriented intent and then discovers recommendation policies according to their incremental utility over an intent-only baseline. Candidate policies are evaluated and refined using outcome-derived feedback rather than linguistic plausibility. We further transfer the resulting intent and policy knowledge into two latent tokens of a lightweight Semantic-ID generator through dual-space relational distillation, enabling LLM-free online inference. Experiments on public benchmarks show consistent improvements over baselines, while large-scale online A/B tests achieve gains of 4.506% in Revenue and 4.621% in ADVV.

</details>


### [73] [Interpretable Representation via LLM-Driven Generative Disentanglement for Local-Life Service Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27944)
*Long Zhang,Hao Jiang,Sheng Yu,Fei Pan,Peng Jiang,Kun Gai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: LGRID提出了一种基于LLM的生成式解耦范式，通过联合编码、结构化解耦块、协同对齐学习和双流残差量化，生成具有显式属性对应关系的可解释语义ID，显著提升推荐性能并降低冲突率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SID生成框架遵循单一表示后量化范式，面临语义纠缠导致异构属性（如地理、品牌、类别）混合、量化时信息损失、SID质量低、严重冲突，以及黑盒表示学习缺乏显式属性语义和地理/语义含义，削弱检索可靠性和诊断控制能力。

Method: LGRID采用Encode->Disentangle->Align->Quantize流水线：联合LLM编码保留跨属性地理-语义依赖；结构化解耦块将隐藏状态路由至属性对齐槽位（地理与语义因子）；协同对齐学习使槽位既可生成式解码又具判别性用于检索；双流残差量化分别将两流离散化为紧凑SID并保持显式属性对应。

Result: 在Kuaishou和Foursquare数据集上，LGRID持续优于强SID基线，AUC相对提升高达5.44%；粗粒度地理字段属性解码准确率超99%；完整SID冲突率降至39.9%，而LGSID为97.0%。

Conclusion: LGRID通过生成式解耦范式实现了可解释的语义ID生成，兼顾推荐性能、属性解码精度和冲突率降低，为本地生活服务推荐提供了更可靠、可诊断、可控的SID建模方案。

Abstract: While large language models (LLMs) have advanced ID-based recommendation through Semantic ID (SID) modeling, existing SID generation frameworks largely follow a single-representation-then-quantization paradigm. This design faces two bottlenecks: semantic entanglement mixes heterogeneous attributes, such as geography, brand, and category, causing information loss during quantization, low-quality SIDs, and severe collisions; moreover, black-box representation learning provides neither explicit attribute semantics nor clear geographic or semantic meanings for SID positions. These limitations weaken both retrieval reliability and the ability to diagnose or control SID generation. We propose Interpretable Representation via LLM-Driven Generative Disentanglement for Local-Life Service Recommendation (LGRID). LGRID introduces a generative disentanglement paradigm through an Encode -> Disentangle -> Align -> Quantize pipeline. It first uses joint LLM encoding to preserve cross-attribute geographic-semantic dependencies, rather than encoding fields independently. A Structured Disentangled Block then routes hidden states into attribute-aligned slots for geographic and semantic factors. Synergistic Alignment Learning makes these slots both generatively decodable and discriminative for retrieval, while Dual-Stream Residual Quantization separately discretizes the two streams into compact SIDs with explicit attribute correspondence. This design yields interpretable SIDs with positions grounded in item attributes and local-service semantics. Experiments on Kuaishou and Foursquare show that LGRID consistently outperforms strong SID baselines, achieving up to a 5.44 percent relative AUC gain. It also achieves over 99 percent attribute-decoding accuracy for coarse geographic fields and reduces the full-SID collision rate to 39.9 percent, compared with 97.0 percent for LGSID.

</details>


### [74] [VIG-RL: Learning to Search and Insert for Verified Image Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28055)
*Qinhan Yu,Jun Guang,Chong Chen,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出VIG-RL框架，通过强化学习优化的动态ReAct式循环，将搜索-选择-插入工作流建模为主动决策过程，解决了现有静态管道在知识密集场景下无法动态推理何时需要外部知识及视觉资产插入位置的问题，实现了Verified Image Grounding (VIG)的新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的检索增强框架主要依赖解耦的静态管道，天然无法动态推理何时需要外部知识以及视觉资产应在何处上下文插入，导致在知识密集型场景下难以提供可靠的交织文本-图像响应。

Method: 提出VIG-RL自主智能体框架，将搜索-选择-插入工作流建模为主动决策过程，在动态ReAct式循环中运行，通过复合奖励系统引导强化学习优化，全面评估智能体的逐步工具执行和最终多模态对齐。

Result: 大量实验评估表明，VIG-RL建立了新的最先进水平，显著优于现有的静态基线方法。

Conclusion: VIG-RL通过将验证图像定位任务建模为基于强化学习的自主决策过程，成功弥补了静态管道在动态推理方面的不足，为知识密集型场景下的可靠多模态响应生成提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: In knowledge-intensive scenarios, providing reliable interleaved text-image responses requires Verified Image Grounding (VIG): the precise integration of retrieved authentic visual evidence. Existing retrieval-augmented frameworks predominantly rely on decoupled, static pipelines, inherently failing to dynamically reason about when external knowledge is required and where visual assets should be contextually inserted. To bridge this gap, we propose VIG-RL, an autonomous agentic framework that formulates the search-selection-insertion workflow as an active decision-making process. Operating within a dynamic ReAct-style loop, VIG-RL is optimized via reinforcement learning, guided by a composite reward system that holistically evaluates the agent's step-by-step tool execution and final multimodal alignment. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that VIG-RL establishes a new state-of-the-art, significantly outperforming existing static baselines.

</details>


### [75] [CCFormer: Efficient Cross-Field Interaction and Hierarchical Sequence Compression for Industrial Recommendation at Tencent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28070)
*Yunlong Wang,Huizhe Zhang,Haonan Hu,Yudong Li,Bing Wen,Jianchao Tu,Chengxiang Zhuo,Zang Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出CCFormer，一种高效Transformer骨干网络，统一跨域特征交互与压缩长序列建模，通过特征域分离交叉注意力、长序列子空间Token混合及层次化序列压缩策略，在工业推荐场景显著提升CTR和广告收益，同时加速训练2.21倍，已在腾讯生产环境全量部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐系统面临严格的延迟和资源约束，难以在计算开销与细粒度特征交互之间取得平衡；现有基于自注意力的序列推荐模型虽受益于可预测的缩放定律，但长序列建模与跨域特征交互的高效统一仍具挑战。

Method: 提出CCFormer，结合特征域分离交叉注意力与长序列子空间Token混合，利用层次化序列压缩策略（逐步扩大感受野）实现低损耗的长序列建模，统一跨异构特征域的长期偏好信号挖掘。

Result: 在两个公开基准和大规模工业数据集上持续超越SOTA基线；腾讯视频推荐和广告排序在线A/B测试分别带来3.57% CTR提升和1.71% 广告收益提升，训练加速2.21倍（相对HSTU），已全量部署服务主流量。

Conclusion: CCFormer通过创新的注意力机制与压缩策略，在保持高效推理的同时实现了跨域特征交互与长序列建模的统一，验证了其在大规模工业推荐系统中的实用性与显著业务收益。

Abstract: Recent studies in industrial recommendation systems have demonstrated that sequential recommendation models built upon self-attention can benefit from predictable scaling laws by increasing sequence length and model capacity. However, practical recommender systems impose strict latency and resource constraints, making it challenging to balance computational overhead with fine-grained feature interaction. In this paper, we propose CCFormer, an efficient Transformer backbone that unifies cross-field feature interaction and compressed long-sequence modeling for industrial recommendation. Specifically, CCFormer combines feature-field separated cross attention with long-sequence subspace token mixing to exploit long-term preference signals across heterogeneous feature domains. A hierarchical sequence compression strategy with progressively expanded receptive fields enables efficient long-sequence modeling with reduced information loss. Extensive experiments on two public benchmarks and a large-scale industrial dataset demonstrate that CCFormer consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. Online A/B tests in a video recommendation scenario and an advertising ranking scenario at Tencent further validate its industrial practicality, yielding a 3.57% CTR gain and a 1.71% advertising revenue lift, respectively, while accelerating model training by 2.21x over the strong HSTU baseline. CCFormer has been fully deployed in Tencent's production recommendation system, serving the main traffic of both scenarios.

</details>


### [76] [TCA-SIR: Learning Target-Conditioned Abstractions for Scientific Inspiration Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28498)
*Yuto Suzuki,Farnoush Banaei-Kashani*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文将科学灵感检索(SIR)重构为目标条件抽象(TCA)，提出TCA-SIR方法，学习生成针对目标问题的可迁移抽象原则并预测迁移性，在ResearchBench上显著优于现有方法，HitRate@top4%提升超10个百分点，且提供可解释的科学灵感理由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SIR方法仅基于主题相似性排序论文，未显式建模候选灵感如何迁移至目标问题，尤其对远程灵感（其价值在于可复用的问题求解原则而非主题重叠）效果有限。受人类抽象源域可迁移方面并重映射到新目标的启发，需将SIR重构为目标条件抽象任务。

Method: 提出TCA-SIR框架：将检索对象定义为从候选文献中针对特定目标提取的可迁移抽象原则；学习生成目标条件抽象，并利用其表示预测迁移性；通过目标条件抽象的表示进行检索排序。

Result: 在ResearchBench基准上，TCA-SIR优于先前SIR方法和直接LLM检索，HitRate@top4%比MOOSE-Chem提升超过10个百分点；学习到的抽象比未训练的TCA提示更清晰地恢复目标相关机制，同时实现更强检索性能和可解释的科学灵感理由。

Conclusion: 将SIR重构为目标条件抽象并学习生成针对性抽象原则，能有效捕捉远程灵感的可迁移问题求解机制，显著提升科学假设生成的灵感检索质量，并提供人类可理解的迁移理由。

Abstract: Scientific hypothesis generation for AI for Science typically involves Scientific Inspiration Retrieval (SIR) followed by hypothesis composition. Existing SIR methods rank papers by topical similarity and do not explicitly represent how a candidate inspiration transfers to a target problem. This is especially limiting for remote inspirations, whose value often lies in reusable problem-solving principles rather than topical overlap. Motivated by how humans abstract transferable aspects of a source and remap them to a new target, we reformulate SIR as target-conditioned abstraction (TCA). The retrieval object is a transferable abstract principle extracted from a candidate specifically for the target. We present TCA-SIR, which learns to generate target-conditioned abstractions and uses their representations to predict transferability. On ResearchBench, TCA-SIR outperforms prior SIR methods and direct LLM retrieval, improving HitRate@top4% over MOOSE-Chem by more than 10 percentage points. Learned abstractions also recover target-relevant mechanisms more clearly than an untrained TCA prompt, yielding both stronger retrieval and an interpretable rationale for scientific inspiration.

</details>


### [77] [MagicSelector: Joint Optimization for Agent Tool Selection via Counterfactual Decomposition and Progressive Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17751)
*HONOR Agentic Search Team,Zhengzong Chen,Lei Tang,Lijun Liu,Chuandi Jiang,Fan Yang,Keyun Chu,Chu Zhao,Shihao Liu,Minghang Li,Bo Liang,Can Wen,Hailong Wu,Jingnan Ju,Mian Liu,Nengbin Zhang,Peiqiang Wang,Penghe Nie,Qinhui Gu,Sijia Lv,Siqi Chen,Wei Zhang,Yang Xu,Yuhao Qian,Yuxiang Zhang,Zeng Cheng,Zhen Wang,Zuan Chen,Yuanyuan Zhao,Fei Huang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: MagicSelector是一个联合优化框架，集成了反事实任务分解、渐进式重排序和动态Top-K策略，旨在解决智能体工具检索中的核心挑战，显著提升检索准确率、OOD泛化能力和Token效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决智能体工具检索中的根本挑战：将模糊用户指令转化为可执行的原子子任务，并指导高精度工具检索，有效缓解域外（OOD）场景下的冗余噪声和严重上下文干扰。

Method: 三大核心贡献：(1) 基于偏好的反事实任务分解机制，利用反事实奖励量化分解对检索排序的边际因果增益，施加细粒度结构监督以保证逻辑连贯性；(2) 基于自蒸馏困难负样本挖掘的渐进式工具重排序方法，优化点级和列表级相关性，增强高度相似工具间的细粒度区分能力；(3) 双语义边界感知动态Top-K策略，自适应监控重排序得分悬崖和工具间语义漂移，动态截断候选列表，最大化相关工具召回率同时过滤长尾噪声。

Result: 在MTDTool基准（首个面向移动多轮交互且包含过程级标注的任务分解基准）上评估，MagicSelector显著优于最先进方法，在工具检索准确率、OOD泛化能力和整体Token效率三方面均取得显著提升。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明了MagicSelector框架的有效性，为智能体工具检索提供了一个有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: We present MagicSelector, a joint optimization framework integrating Counterfactual task decomposition, Progressive reranking, and Dynamic Top-K, designed to address the fundamental challenges of tool retrieval in agents. MagicSelector is a specialized framework capable of translating ambiguous user instructions into executable atomic subtasks and guiding high-precision tool retrieval, effectively mitigating redundant noise and severe context distraction in out-of-domain (OOD) scenarios. We empower MagicSelector with these capabilities through three key contributions: (1) a preference-guided counterfactual task decomposition mechanism that utilizes a counterfactual reward to quantify the marginal causal gain of decomposition on retrieval ranking, effectively imposing fine-grained structural supervision on logical coherence; (2) a progressive tool reranking method driven by self-distillation hard negative mining, which optimizes both point-wise and list-wise relevance to enhance fine-grained discrimination among highly similar tools; and (3) a dual semantic boundary-aware dynamic Top-K strategy that adaptively monitors reranking score cliffs and inter-tool semantic shifts to dynamically truncate the candidate list, maximizing relevant tool recall while filtering long-tail noise. Evaluated on MTDTool, the first task decomposition benchmark we constructed tailored for mobile multi-turn interactions with process-level annotations, MagicSelector yields promising performance. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MagicSelector significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods in terms of tool retrieval accuracy, OOD generalization capability, and overall token efficiency, thereby demonstrating the effectiveness of our proposed framework.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [78] [Recursive transformers for semiconductor thermo-mechanical reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27251)
*Kart-leong Lim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了三种递归Transformer范式，用于工程设计中的小数据代理建模，展示了权重共享递归架构在参数效率、计算成本与预测精度之间的有效权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Transformer在工程设计典型的小规模、低维数据集上参数过多，导致过拟合及不必要的显存和算力开销，因此需要转向以增加计算量而非可学习参数为核心的架构。

Method: 硬件感知评估三种递归Transformer范式：Tiny Recursive Model、Depth Recursive Transformer（本文提出）、简单递归Transformer；在热力学可靠性分析和Laplace PDE求解两个低维工程任务上，系统对比召回率、平均倒数排名、参数量、FLOPs等指标。

Result: 递归权重共享Transformer在参数效率、计算成本和预测精度之间提供了有效且可泛化的权衡，适用于资源受限场景下的小数据工程代理建模。

Conclusion: 递归权重共享Transformer架构是小数据工程代理建模中平衡预测精度、参数效率和计算成本的有效且可推广的选择。

Abstract: Transformer-based surrogate models are increasingly used to replace expensive first-principles simulation in engineering design. But conventional transformer architectures are often over parameterized for the small, low-dimensional datasets typical of engineering design spaces, where large simulation data is expensive to generate. Under these conditions, excess parameter capacity leads to overfitting rather than improved accuracy, while also incurring unnecessary memory and compute overhead. This motivates a shift towards architectures that focus on additional compute rather than additional learnable parameters. This paper presents a hardware-aware evaluation of three recursive transformer paradigms for surrogate thermo-mechanical analysis of advanced packages: a)Tiny Recursive Model, b) our proposed Depth Recursive transformer, c) and a simple recursive transformer. We systematically compare their predictive performance (Recall, Mean Reciprocal Rank), parameter count, computational complexity (FLOPs), providing practical design guidelines for selecting recursive transformer architectures under resource-constrained scenarios. We validate this principle on two low-dimensional engineering prediction tasks: 1) thermo-mechanical reliability analysis of advanced semiconductor packages, where stress and warpage from thermal cycling must be evaluated repeatedly across a design-of-experiments sweep under costly finite element analysis (FEA). 2) Laplace PDE iterative numerical solver for capacitance field. Overall, recursive weight-sharing transformers provide an effective and generalizable trade-off between prediction accuracy, parameter efficiency, and computational cost for small data engineering surrogate modeling.

</details>


### [79] [Regularizing modality contribution drift in multimodal continual learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27260)
*Zhen Zhang,Jielei Chu,Bin Liu,Tianrui Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出模态贡献漂移（MCD）概念，量化多模态持续学习中模态贡献结构的变化，并提出持续模态贡献漂移正则化（CMCDR）方法，包含基于重放和无重放两个版本，有效缓解灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态持续学习方法主要关注跨模态表示对齐或语义相似性，忽视了各模态及其交互的相对贡献在增量任务中的稳定性，导致模态贡献漂移（MCD），无法有效缓解灾难性遗忘。

Method: 提出CMCDR，通过模态子集干预作为诊断探针，保持先前任务的模态贡献结构。重放版本使用存储的旧样本对比当前模型与冻结旧模型的贡献分布；无重放版本使用当前任务样本蒸馏冻结模型的旧任务贡献响应。

Result: 在多模态类增量学习和持续视觉问答任务上实验验证，CMCDR的两个版本均表现出泛化性和有效性，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了多模态持续学习中模态贡献漂移问题，提出CMCDR有效保持模态贡献结构，为解决多模态灾难性遗忘提供了新视角和方法。

Abstract: Multimodal continual learning (MMCL) aims to learn emerging knowledge from multimodal data while preserving knowledge. To mitigate forgetting, current MMCL methods usually focus on cross-modal representation alignment or semantic similarity, but they overlook whether the relative contributions of individual modalities and their interactions remain stable across incremental tasks. We term this decision-level shift Modality Contribution Drift (MCD) and quantify it with the MCD score, which combines contribution-strength and relative-reliance changes under controlled interventions on modality subsets. Theoretical and empirical analyses further explain why current MMCL methods cannot reliably mitigate this drift. To this end, we propose Continual Modality Contribution Drift Regularization (CMCDR), which preserves the modality contribution structure of previously learned tasks. Since MMCL settings differ in whether old exemplars are available, CMCDR includes both replay-based and replay-free versions. The replay-based version uses modality-subset interventions as diagnostic probes on stored old samples, compares their contribution profiles between the current model and a frozen previous model, and constrains changes in old-sample modality-specific and interaction contributions. The replay-free version uses current-task samples as probes and distills the frozen model's old-task contribution responses, thereby regularizing the observed contribution profile without exemplars. Experiments on multimodal class-incremental learning and continual visual question answering validate the generality and effectiveness of CMCDR.

</details>


### [80] [PlatformBid: An Auto-Bidding Benchmark from a Unified Advertising Platform's Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27265)
*Shengtian Yang,Yewen Li,Peng Jiang,Zhiyi Lyu,Bo An,Peng Jiang,Qingpeng Cai,Lei Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出首个统一广告平台视角的自动竞价基准PlatformBid，包含同质竞争、异质竞争、促销竞争三种设定，系统评估经典控制、强化学习、生成式等方法，并提出基于流匹配的BidFlow方法，在快手在线实验中实现目标成本+0.68%提升，验证离线-在线一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动竞价算法仅关注DSP侧最大化广告主转化，但大型广告平台内部整合SSP、DSP和Ad Exchange，需从平台视角同时最大化广告主转化和平台总收益，现有缺乏平台中心评估框架，亟需推进自动竞价研究。

Method: 构建PlatformBid基准，定义三种代表性竞争设定：(1)同质竞争，广告主使用相同算法；(2)异质竞争，广告主采用多样化算法策略；(3)促销竞争，部分广告主在大促期间激增预算。系统评估经典控制、强化学习、生成式等现有自动竞价方法，并提出基于流匹配的BidFlow方法，利用流匹配的表达式策略表示有效应对动态竞争环境。

Result: 在三种设定下全面评估现有自动竞价方法性能；BidFlow在动态竞争环境中展现优势；快手在线实验显示目标成本指标提升+0.68%，提供离线基准与在线部署一致性的实证证据。

Conclusion: PlatformBid填补了平台视角自动竞价基准空白，为统一广告平台下的自动竞价研究提供标准化评估框架；BidFlow验证了流匹配在动态竞价策略建模中的潜力；在线实验结果证明了基准的实际部署价值和离线-在线一致性。

Abstract: Real-time bidding is central to computational advertising, comprising three elements: Supply Side Platform (SSP) selling ad impressions, Demand Side Platform (DSP) bidding for advertisers, and Ad Exchange conducting auctions between them. Traditional auto-bidding algorithms focus solely on the DSP side, maximizing advertiser conversions by adjusting bids against competitors. However, current big ad platforms, such as social media and e-commerce companies, now integrate SSP, DSP, and Ad Exchange functions internally. From such ad platforms' perspective, the goal of the auto-bidding algorithms is not only to maximize the advertisers' conversions, but also the total revenue of the platform. Given the lack of platform-centric evaluation frameworks and the pressing need to advance auto-bidding research, we propose PlatformBid - the first comprehensive benchmark designed from a unified ad platform's perspective. To accurately reflect the real-world auto-bidding scenarios, we define three representative settings: (1) homogeneous competition with identical algorithms across advertisers, (2) heterogeneous competition with diverse algorithmic strategies, and (3) promotional competition where some advertisers surge budgets for boosting sales during promotional events like Black Friday. We systematically evaluate a broad spectrum of existing auto-bidding methods across these settings, encompassing classical control methods, RL-based methods, and recent generative methods. Besides these methods, we further propose a novel auto-bidding method based on flow-matching, termed BidFlow, which leverages the flow-matching method's expressive policy representation to effectively handle dynamic competitive environments. Online experiments on Kuaishou further show a +0.68\% improvement in target cost, providing deployment evidence for the offline-online consistency of PlatformBid.

</details>


### [81] [Beyond KV Reconstruction: Functional Reconstruction for MLA Draft Models in Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27269)
*Weiye Shi,Fanxu Meng,Muhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种功能重建方法，用于将MHA/GQA模型转换为MLA模型，以提高推测解码中的草稿token接受率，而无需重新训练或验证器监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接将MHA/GQA转换为MLA会因低秩分解和RoPE处理引入注意力函数误差，降低推测解码中草稿token的接受率，需要一种方法在保持缓存效率的同时恢复注意力功能。

Method: 提出端到端的功能重建方法，优化每个转换后的MLA注意力模块，使其在校准隐藏状态上复现原始MHA/GQA的输出投影后响应。该方法与转换器无关，保留转换后的缓存和推理图，不需要验证器logits或监督。

Result: 在192种配置（四对Llama/Qwen草稿-目标模型、TransMLA和MHA2MLA、HF和vLLM、四个200提示任务）中，功能重建在64个匹配任务单元中有37个显著提高接受率，26个基本不变，1个显著下降（容差0.5个百分点）。

Conclusion: 功能重建作为一种后转换优化过程，能有效缓解MHA/GQA到MLA转换带来的注意力函数误差，显著提升推测解码的草稿token接受率，同时保持MLA的缓存效率优势，无需重新训练或验证器监督。

Abstract: Multi-head latent attention (MLA) is increasingly important for long-context LLM inference because compact latent states replace the growing key-value (KV) cache and reduce decoding memory traffic. Yet most capable open checkpoints use multi-head or grouped-query attention (MHA/GQA), so conversion is needed to obtain MLA's cache efficiency without retraining from scratch. Speculative decoding offers complementary acceleration, but its speedup depends on agreement between draft proposals and target verification. We find that direct MHA/GQA-to-MLA conversion can sharply reduce this agreement: low-rank factorization and RoPE handling introduce attention-function errors that may be tolerable for standalone generation but substantially lower draft-token acceptance. We therefore formulate MLA draft construction as functional reconstruction rather than cache compression. Our end-to-end (E2E) method optimizes each converted MLA attention module to reproduce the post-output-projection response of its original MHA/GQA counterpart on calibration hidden states. This converter-agnostic post-conversion procedure preserves the converted cache and inference graph and requires neither verifier logits nor verifier supervision. We evaluate 192 model-converter-backend-method-task configurations spanning four Llama/Qwen draft-target pairs, TransMLA and MHA2MLA, HF and vLLM, and four 200-prompt tasks. With a 0.5-percentage-point reporting tolerance, Functional Reconstruction materially improves acceptance in 37 of 64 matched task cells, leaves 26 practically unchanged, and materially decreases one. Code and evaluation artifacts are available at https://github.com/swyhahaha/FunctionalMLA.

</details>


### [82] [RLPF: Reinforcement Learning from Performance Feedback for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27271)
*Huihao Jing,Haozhe Cui,Wenbin Hu,Shaojin Chen,Haochen Shi,Changxuan Fan,Yuxuan Liu,Hanyu Yang,Sirui Zhang,Ziyi Chen,Haoran Li,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 RLPF（基于性能反馈的强化学习），通过分阶段奖励机制解决代码生成中正确性与运行效率的平衡问题，在 PerfCodeBench 上显著提升正确且可运行解的比例（11.1% → 54.6%）和相对效率（8.1% → 38.6%），并验证了模型生成参考的有效性及复合奖励的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码模型训练多以正确性为唯一信号，忽略运行时效率；系统代码中两程序通过相同测试但运行时间差异大，需训练代理偏好更快的正确实现。运行时间作为奖励脆弱：仅在正确后有意义、跨任务差异大、且大多数采样程序无法编译/运行时缺乏指导。

Method: 提出 RLPF，将执行结果转化为分阶段奖励：失败程序按执行进度排序；正确程序按相对基线向专家参考的改进程度排序。这样在正确性前提供有用反馈，正确后提供性能敏感反馈。在 PerfCodeBench 上微调 Qwen3-32B。

Result: 正确且可运行解从 11.1% 提升至 54.6%，相对效率从 8.1% 提升至 38.6%。训练模型与更强开源系统竞争，优化行为适度迁移至 EffiBench-X。消融实验显示模型生成参考提供较弱但有用的监督，完整复合奖励优于仅正确性或仅运行时基线。

Conclusion: 代码代理不仅可被训练通过测试，还可被训练优化其编写的程序性能；RLPF 的分阶段奖励机制有效解决了运行时奖励脆弱的问题，为效率感知代码生成提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Code models are increasingly trained with execution feedback, but most training signals still stop at correctness. This leaves an important gap for systems code: two programs can pass the same tests while differing greatly in runtime. We study how to train code agents to prefer faster correct implementations, rather than treating efficiency only as an evaluation metric. The key difficulty is that runtime is a fragile reward. It is meaningful only after a program is correct, varies across tasks, and gives little guidance when most sampled programs fail to compile or run. We propose \textbf{RLPF}, reinforcement learning from performance feedback, which turns execution outcomes into a staged reward. Failed programs are ordered by execution progress, while correct programs are ranked by their relative improvement from the baseline toward the expert reference. This gives useful feedback before correctness and performance-sensitive feedback after correctness. Fine-tuning Qwen3-32B with RLPF on PerfCodeBench raises correct-and-runnable solutions from $11.1\%$ to $54.6\%$ and improves relative efficiency from $8.1\%$ to $38.6\%$. The trained model becomes competitive with stronger open-weight systems, and its optimization behavior transfers modestly to EffiBench-X. Additional studies show that model-generated references provide useful but weaker supervision, and that the full composite reward is more reliable than correctness-only or runtime-only baselines. These results suggest that code agents can be trained not only to pass tests, but also to optimize the programs they write.

</details>


### [83] [SDO: Structure-Aware Data Organization for Efficient LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27273)
*Jinliang Gao,Ning Yang,Hai Wang,Baili Xiao,Pin Lyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SDO（Structure-Aware Data Organization）框架，通过基于表示空间结构的动态数据组织和曝光驱动反馈机制，解决后训练中静态数据组织导致的样本曝光不均衡问题，在SFT、DPO、GRPO等任务上显著加速收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型后训练的效率优化主要集中在样本选择或训练调度，而数据组织被视为静态预处理步骤。基于嵌入的分组方法在训练前构建固定分区，无法适应优化过程中不断变化的样本曝光，导致所有样本获得相似曝光，尽管其优化需求不同，造成部分样本冗余更新而另一些样本欠优化。

Method: SDO是一个即插即用的数据组织框架，基于冻结的外部嵌入逐轮操作，避免模型预热开销。轮内采用局部感知批次构建（KNN邻域遍历）形成连贯小批次；轮间采用曝光平衡调度记录每样本参与度，降低过度曝光样本的采样概率以保持长期覆盖。

Result: 在SFT、DPO和GRPO三种后训练范式上，SDO均能加速收敛，增益在早期到中期最大，产生更连贯的梯度和更平衡的跨题型准确率，且不永久排除任何训练样本。

Conclusion: SDO通过动态调整小批次组成和样本曝光，有效解决了静态数据组织导致的曝光不均衡问题，为大语言模型后训练提供了一个高效、通用的数据组织方案。

Abstract: Post-training of large language models is expensive, and existing efficiency improvements mainly focus on selecting informative samples or designing training schedules. However, data organization itself is usually treated as a static preprocessing step: embedding-based grouping methods construct fixed partitions before training and cannot adapt to the evolving sample exposure during optimization. As a result, all samples receive similar exposure despite their different optimization needs, leading to redundant updates for some samples while leaving others under-optimized. To address this problem, we propose SDO (Structure-Aware Data Organization), a plug-and-play data organization framework with an exposure-driven feedback mechanism that organizes mini-batch composition and sample exposure according to representation-space structure. SDO operates epoch by epoch on frozen external embeddings, avoiding model warm-up training overhead: within each epoch, locality-aware batching forms coherent mini-batches via KNN neighborhood traversal; across epochs, exposure-balanced scheduling records per-sample participation and reduces the sampling probability of over-exposed samples to preserve long-term coverage. Across SFT, DPO, and GRPO, SDO accelerates convergence, with the largest gains observed in the early-to-mid phase, producing more coherent gradients and more balanced accuracy across question types without permanently excluding training samples.

</details>


### [84] [Rethinking EEG-Based Disease Diagnosis: Decoupling Instance Representation Learning from Subject-Level Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27274)
*Zhiyuan Ma,Zeyuan Li,Zhiyi Lu,Jiacheng Hao,Youlang Du,Zhen Jiang,Xinche Zhang,Yuhao Sun,Sen Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出两阶段框架BridgeMIL，解耦实例表示学习与受试者级监督，在EEG疾病诊断任务上显著超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规流程将受试者标签继承给短实例训练实例级分类器，假设所有实例提供同等可靠的诊断证据；多实例学习(MIL)虽避免继承标签，但EEG数据集受试者数远少于实例，限制了端到端MIL的表示学习质量。

Method: 两阶段：第一阶段预训练编码器，不使用继承实例标签，通过对齐时序相邻窗口和独立采样的受试者内子包，利用方差与协方差正则化防止坍塌并降低冗余（无需负样本）；第二阶段将编码器迁移至基于注意力的MIL聚合器，仅对受试者预测施加监督，并通过特征保持机制限制表示漂移。

Result: 在三个EEG疾病数据集和五种主干网络上，BridgeMIL在14/15数据集-主干设置中取得最高平均准确率，总体平均准确率76.57%，比最强基线高4.28个百分点。进一步分析揭示继承标签可靠性在实例间差异显著，性能对受试者稀缺比实例稀缺更敏感，且学习到的表示空间更结构化，呈现明显的受试者级聚类与改进的诊断类别分离。

Conclusion: 研究强调将监督目标与受试者级预测对齐的重要性，在不给单个实例分配疾病标签的前提下利用丰富的EEG实例进行学习。

Abstract: EEG-based disease diagnosis requires one prediction per subject, yet common pipelines segment recordings into short instances, inherit the subject label for every instance, and train instance-level classifiers. This assumes that all instances provide equally reliable diagnostic evidence. Multiple instance learning (MIL) avoids inherited labels by treating each subject as a bag. However, EEG datasets contain far fewer subjects than instances, which can limit the quality of the representations learned by end-to-end MIL. We propose BridgeMIL, a two-stage framework that decouples instance representation learning from subject-level supervision. Stage 1 pretrains the encoder without inherited instance labels by aligning temporally nearby windows and independently sampled within-subject sub-bags. Variance and covariance regularization prevent collapse and reduce redundancy without negative pairs. Stage 2 transfers the encoder to an attention-based MIL aggregator, applies supervision only to subject predictions, and limits representation drift through feature retention. Across three EEG disease datasets and five representative backbones, BridgeMIL attains the highest mean accuracy in 14 of 15 dataset-backbone settings and an overall mean accuracy of 76.57%, 4.28 percentage points higher than the strongest baseline. Further analyses reveal substantial variation in inherited-label reliability across instances, greater performance sensitivity to subject scarcity than to instance scarcity, and a more structured representation space with distinct subject-wise clusters and improved separation between diagnostic classes. Together, these findings underscore the importance of aligning supervision with the subject-level prediction objective while learning from abundant EEG instances without assigning disease labels to individual instances.

</details>


### [85] [Flat Score, Amplified Failures: How the Error Budget Masks Damage in Quantized LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27275)
*Jiwon Jang,Kisu Yang,Heuiseok Lim,Hyunwoo Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 后训练4位量化在多轮工具调用代理的标准基准指标上表现为无损，实则放大了模型原有的工具名幻觉错误，通过缩减错误预算和针对性错误修复提示可揭示并消除该损伤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证后训练4位量化在多轮工具调用代理中的‘近乎无损’声称，因为该场景对量化敏感性要求最高。

Method: 在τ²-bench上测试两个开放权重模型家族（密集和MoE变体）在两个领域（八个细胞，每细胞456个回合）下，分别以16、8、4位权重进行量化；采用等价性检验、每通道错误率、错误预算缩减实验及针对性错误修复提示进行诊断。

Result: 标准指标上量化无显著分数变化（±7.5点），但量化放大了模型原有工具名幻觉错误（电信领域）及零售实体错误，错误量增至2.5倍（+17.6点/任务），几乎无新错误；错误集合在各精度下高度一致（秩相关≥0.94，0.18%新事件）。十分错误预算掩盖了额外错误；将预算缩减至两错误重现17分差距，仅出现在量化增加错误的细胞。针对性错误修复提示在五个电信模型上精准消除损伤。

Conclusion: 标准任务奖励掩盖了量化引发的错误放大，建议基准同时报告每通道错误率和缩减错误预算下的成功率以暴露真实量化影响。

Abstract: Post-training quantization to 4-bit weights is widely reported to be nearly lossless. We test this claim for multi-turn, tool-calling agents, where it now matters most. On $τ^2$-bench, across two open-weight model families in dense and MoE variants and two domains (eight cells, 456 episodes each, at 16-, 8-, and 4-bit weights), quantization indeed looks free on the standard metric. No cell shows a score change that survives multiple-comparison correction, and in the cell that carries the largest process damage, equivalence testing bounds the change within $\pm$7.5 points. The process tells a different story. Quantization amplifies the failure the model already exhibits at full precision (tool-name hallucination in telecom, with the same directional trend in retail entity errors) by up to 2.5$\times$ in volume (+17.6 points per task), while creating essentially no new failures. The failure set is the same at every precision (rank correlation $\geq$ 0.94, 0.18% novel events). The score stays flat because the benchmark's ten-error budget absorbs the extra failures. Shrinking the budget to two errors re-exposes a score gap of 17 points, and it does so only in the one cell where quantization added error volume, exactly as the masking account predicts. A targeted error-repair prompt, run for five telecom models at every precision, removes the damage exactly and only where it lives. Both diagnostics, the per-channel error rate and success under a shrinking budget, come from logs benchmarks already collect; we suggest reporting them alongside task reward.

</details>


### [86] [The Kinetics of Training: A Driven-Nucleation Rate Law for Emergence, Plasticity Loss, and Circuit Control in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27281)
*Lei Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文揭示语言模型能力形成的速率限制步骤是多部分电路的联合对齐（无部分信用），通过实验与理论证明该机制，并提出退火方法控制电路形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型中复杂能力（如联合电路）如何涌现，为何标准监控无法检测能力习得的突变点，以及SGD噪声为何导致电路学习失效。

Method: 结合实证实验（Pythia模型消融、前向/后向点火实验、查询-键切片重初始化）与理论建模（受控门控注意力模型、速率方程、波动-耗散检验），采用预注册常数验证。

Result: 发现能力形成受制于多部分电路的联合对齐（无部分信用），等待时间取决于缺失部分数而非电路规模；消融单一部分仅留17%能力（远低于部分信用预测的50-83%）；能力点火可预测（中位数误差5%），标准验证损失平滑下降却盲视突变；损伤定位于头部对基础数据的承诺，仅重初始化查询-键切片即可恢复可学习性（6/6成功）；SGD噪声不满足波动-耗散定理，退火可按计划熔化/固定电路。

Conclusion: 语言模型能力涌现的核心瓶颈是电路各部分需同时对齐的罕见事件，该机制解释了为何标准训练监控失效，并提供了通过退火控制电路形成的实用方法，适用于最高1.4B参数的Transformer联合电路。

Abstract: A capability appears in a language model when the last parts of its circuit align in one stochastic attempt, and getting all but one right is worth nothing. We show this no-partial-credit joint alignment is the rate-limiting step of capability formation. Two fingerprints: in a shortcut-free apparatus a five-part circuit missing three waits as long as a three-part circuit missing three (1.19-1.37), so the wait counts missing parts, not size; and on Pythia across seven capabilities and three scales, ablating one part leaves a median 17% of the capability in 32 of 32 discriminating cells, where partial credit predicts 50-83% (p = 2e-10), while a random non-part head leaves 100%.
  One rare event whose barrier grows with missing parts yields a rate equation -- sites x attempts x drive x exp(-beta*K), minus destruction -- read three ways, each preregistered with frozen constants. Forward: a capability flat at baseline ignites at a step of our choosing once the mix passes a concentration floor (10/10 above, 0/12 below), and while still flat its arrival is datable from its precursor to 5% median error on six held-out models. Backward: the delay to learn a withheld capability grows with waiting until, past a critical step, it never ignites -- yet validation loss falls smoothly throughout, so standard monitors are blind to it. We locate the damage (heads commit to the base data) and isolate the cure: re-initializing only the query-key slices restores learnability (6/6) while the value slices do nothing (0/6). We prove the mechanism in a controlled gated-attention model: occupation forces a deadline whose consequences need no mixing assumption. Completed: SGD's noise fails the fluctuation-dissipation test, so we install one and anneal, melt and pin circuits on schedule. Scope: conjunction circuits in transformers to 1.4B.

</details>


### [87] [Benchmarking the Residual: What Long-Horizon Evaluations Add Beyond Matched Short-Task Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27283)
*Chao Peng,Zhiheng Lyu,Peijie Dong,Hande Dong,Qiang Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出“视野残差”概念，主张长时任务基准测试需将完整任务成功率与基于短阶段的基线预测对比，以量化长时失效程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长时基准仅观察到任务越长失败越多，但未解释原因；需要区分普通错误累积、单个决策变难、以及轨迹诱导的退化（如上下文腐烂）。

Method: 定义“视野残差”为完整任务成功率与基于短阶段独立成功率预测的对数比率；要求使用相同智能体配置，预先指定阶段、检查点、信息和预算。

Result: 视野残差揭示完整任务与基线的差异，但仍需针对性实验解释具体原因。

Conclusion: 长时失效声明需严格基准对比；视野残差提供量化指标，但解释机制仍需进一步实验。

Abstract: Long-horizon benchmarks often show that agents fail more as tasks become longer. This observation is useful for deployment, but it does not by itself explain why failure occurs. More stages create more opportunities for ordinary errors to compound; longer tasks may also contain harder individual decisions or become harder as conversation history, tool outputs, and environment changes accumulate. We use trajectory-induced degradation to mean this last possibility: earlier execution makes later work harder. When the harmful accumulation is specifically the text visible to the model, it is often called context rot. In this position paper, we argue that to claim a "long-horizon failure", benchmarks must compare actual full-task success against a baseline prediction built from short, individual stages. We call the log-ratio between this prediction and actual success the horizon residual. The comparison must use the same agent configuration and specify in advance how stages, checkpoints, information, and budgets will be chosen. The residual shows that the full rollout differs from the chosen baseline; targeted experiments are still needed to explain why.

</details>


### [88] [EvoCause: LLM-Guided Evolution of Causal Graphs for Root Cause Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27290)
*Lei Zan,Keli Zhang,Shifeng Xie,Jiale Zheng,Zehao Xiao,Zhiwei Dong,Ke Zhang,Ruichu Cai,Malik Tiomoko,Lujia Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 EvoCause 方法，利用大语言模型 (LLM) 对因果图进行语义合理的编辑，并结合确定性代码验证节点身份和无环性，通过专家标注的历史事件约束源节点，实现因果图的迭代优化，从而提升根因分析 (RCA) 的准确性。同时发布了真实电信网络基准 TeleRCA。实验表明，EvoCause 在合成数据和真实数据上均显著优于基线，且告警名称的语义信息对图优化有重要贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代电信、云和微服务系统在组件故障时会产生相关的告警级联。根因分析 (RCA) 旨在识别引发每个级联的少量初始告警。常见方法是从观测日志学习因果图，并预测每个事件诱导子图中所有入度为零的告警。然而，学习到的因果图是固定的，无法从专家对历史事件的诊断中获益。本文旨在闭合这个循环：利用专家标签约束哪些告警应为源节点，但专家标签不指定满足约束所需的边编辑。

Method: EvoCause 方法：1) 使用 PC 因果发现算法从观测日志学习初始因果图。2) 专家标签提供历史事件的根因告警（源节点约束）。3) 利用大语言模型 (LLM) 基于告警名称的语义信息提出合理的图编辑（如添加/删除边）。4) 确定性代码验证节点身份和无环性，并在标注对齐集上保留最佳图。5) 测试时仅使用优化后的因果图进行透明预测，无需调用 LLM。同时发布 TeleRCA 基准，包含 485,681 个告警事件、194 种告警类型、5,621 个资源。

Result: 在合成数据上，基于 PC 算法初始化的 EvoCause 优于未优化的 PC 基线：Node F1 提升 11.59 个百分点，Case EM 提升 9.40 个百分点，Graph F1 提升 4.59 个百分点，nSHD 降低 0.2379。在 TeleRCA 基准上，将人类可读的告警标题替换为匿名标识符会导致 Node F1 和 Case EM 分别下降 6.12 和 8.04 个百分点，表明告警名称的语义信息对图优化有显著贡献。

Conclusion: EvoCause 通过结合 LLM 的语义推理能力和确定性验证，有效地将专家知识融入因果图优化，显著提升了根因分析的准确性。实验证明，告警名称的语义信息是图优化的关键因素。发布的 TeleRCA 基准为真实电信网络中的 RCA 研究提供了宝贵资源。

Abstract: Modern telecommunication, cloud, and microservice systems emit correlated alarm cascades when components fail. Root cause analysis (RCA) aims to identify the small set of alarms that initiate each cascade. A common approach learns a causal graph from observational logs and predicts all zero-in-degree alarms in each incident-induced subgraph. However, the learned graph remains fixed and cannot benefit from expert diagnoses of historical incidents. We close this loop with EvoCause. Expert labels constrain which alarms should be source nodes but do not specify the edge edits needed to satisfy those constraints. EvoCause uses a large language model (LLM) to propose semantically plausible graph edits, while deterministic code validates node identities and acyclicity and retains the best graph on a labeled alignment set. At test time, the refined graph alone produces transparent predictions without an LLM call. We also release TeleRCA, an expert-annotated benchmark from a production telecommunication network containing $485{,}681$ alarm events spanning $194$ alarm types over $5{,}621$ resources. On synthetic data, EvoCause initialized with the PC causal discovery algorithm outperforms the unrefined PC baseline, raising Node F1, Case EM, and Graph F1 by $11.59$, $9.40$, and $4.59$ percentage points, respectively, while reducing nSHD by $0.2379$. On TeleRCA, replacing human-readable alarm titles with anonymous identifiers lowers Node F1 and Case EM by $6.12$ and $8.04$ percentage points, respectively, indicating that alarm-name information contributes to graph refinement.

</details>


### [89] [Position, Not Provenance: Separating Reasoning Mediation from Sycophancy in Medical Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27304)
*Supratik Bhowal,Subhrajyoti Basu,Aritra Gir Mahanta,Anik Pal Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CoT-Mediate框架，通过扰动模型生成推理中的临床属性来测量推理对预测的因果影响，发现注入推理的机制显著影响忠实度，上下文位置而非声明来源是决定医学VLM是否使用生成推理的主要因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学视觉语言模型在回答临床问题前生成思维链推理，但该推理是否因果影响预测尚不清楚，需要一种方法来量化推理的中介作用。

Method: 提出CoT-Mediate行为框架，扰动模型自发生成推理中的单个临床有意义属性，测量预测是否遵循编辑后的推理。采用双臂协议比较重新提示证据与前缀强制延续，并结合来源控制干预，仅改变相同推理的归属来源，以区分推理中介与迎合行为。在LLaVA-Med和MedGemma上各评估1000个VQA-RAD样本。

Result: 前缀强制延续持续产生比重新提示更高的中介忠实度；来源分析揭示了模型特定的遵从行为差异。两模型中，移除视觉证据增加了对注入推理的依赖，而侧别性是最不忠实跟踪的临床属性。

Conclusion: 注入推理的机制显著影响测量到的忠实度；上下文位置而非声明的来源是决定医学VLM是否使用其生成推理的主要决定因素。

Abstract: Medical vision-language models (VLMs) generate chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning before answering clinical questions, but whether this reasoning causally influences predictions remains unclear. We present CoT-Mediate, a behavioral framework that perturbs a single clinically meaningful attribute within a model's own generated reasoning and measures whether the resulting prediction follows the edited reasoning. Our framework combines a dual-arm protocol comparing re-prompted evidence with prefix-forced continuation, together with a provenance-controlled intervention that varies only the attributed source of identical reasoning to disentangle reasoning mediation from sycophancy. We evaluate LLaVA-Med and MedGemma on 1,000 VQA-RAD samples each. Prefix-forced continuation consistently yields higher mediation faithfulness than re-prompting, while the provenance analysis reveals distinct model-specific deference behaviors. Across both models, removing visual evidence increases reliance on injected reasoning, whereas laterality is the least faithfully tracked clinical attribute. These results show that the mechanism used to inject reasoning substantially affects measured faithfulness and that contextual position, rather than stated provenance, is the primary determinant of whether medical VLMs use their generated reasoning.

</details>


### [90] [ZUNA1.1: A more flexible EEG foundation model for Denoising and Super-resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27308)
*Christopher Warner,Jonas Mago,JR Huml,Beren Millidge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ZUNA1.1 是一个 3.8 亿参数的扩散自编码器，用于灵活的 EEG 信号重建，支持变长序列（最长 30 秒）、任意通道数与头皮位置、任意时间区间重建，性能至少持平前代 ZUNA1 且远超球面样条插值等标准方法，已按 Apache 2.0 开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 EEG 重建方法（如 MNE 包中普遍部署的球面样条插值）灵活性不足，难以处理变长序列、任意通道数/位置以及通道内任意时间区间的重建需求，亟需一种更通用、高性能的重建模型。

Method: 提出 ZUNA1.1，一个 3.8 亿参数的扩散自编码器，采用扩散模型框架对 EEG 信号进行端到端重建，支持任意长度（≤30s）、任意通道数、任意头皮位置及通道内任意时间区间的灵活重建。

Result: 实验表明 ZUNA1.1 性能至少与早期 ZUNA1 持平，同时具备更强灵活性，能胜任广泛重建任务；在标准 EEG 去噪与重建基准上显著优于球面样条插值等传统方法。模型已按 Apache 2.0 许可开源。

Conclusion: ZUNA1.1 通过扩散自编码器实现了高灵活、高性能的 EEG 信号重建，填补了现有方法在变长、多通道、任意空间时间重建方面的空白，并以开源形式推动了 EEG 分析工具的普及。

Abstract: We introduce ZUNA1.1, a 380M-parameter diffusion autoencoder for flexible EEG signal reconstruction. ZUNA1.1 is capable of reconstructing variable length sequences of up to 30s, with an arbitrary number of EEG channels at arbitrary scalp locations, and can reconstruct arbitrary temporal intervals within channels in addition to reconstructing entire channels. We demonstrate that ZUNA1.1 performs at least on par with our earlier ZUNA1 model, while being far more flexible and capable of handling a wide range of reconstruction tasks. ZUNA1.1 continues to substantially outperform standard EEG denoising and reconstruction methods such as spherical spline interpolation, which is ubiquitously deployed in the MNE package. The ZUNA1.1 model is released open source under the permissive Apache 2.0 license.

</details>


### [91] [Good Rankers, Bad Objectives: Bilinear Contrastive Critics under Expressive Policy Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27422)
*Ayushman Singh,Siddharth Aphale*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 对比评论家在动作排序上表现良好，但不适合直接最大化用于动作选择，因为范数漂移、分数饱和和支撑集内误排序会导致虚假正例；Bellman训练的TD-Q表现更好；对比评论家仍可作为兼容性排序器，但动作选择需要值校准的标量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究对比评论家在最佳K选择、规划和评论家引导生成中的价值型目标作用，揭示其在动作最大化时的安全性问题，特别是范数漂移和余弦界限失效的原因。

Method: 通过受控支撑集分解分析原始双线性遗憾归因；在四个OGBench导航任务上评估对比分数校准和排序能力；与Bellman训练的TD-Q对比，包括参数匹配的函数类控制；使用模拟器展开测量实际成本；训练/读出分解追踪排序丢失至余弦训练目标。

Result: 对比分数在前十分位数弱校准或倒置，无法按价值排序固定查询动作；TD-Q成功；PointMaze和精确Q*玩具任务显示单步选择成本，AntMaze和HumanoidMaze因控制器自我修正而无显著成本；原始训练嵌入在推理时归一化后保留弱排序；候选最大化可利用虚假正例。

Conclusion: 对比评论家适合作为导航和操作任务的兼容性排序器，但动作选择需要值校准的标量评论家；直接最大化对比评论家不安全。

Abstract: Good action rankings do not make a contrastive critic safe to maximize. These critics increasingly act as value-like objectives for best-of-$K$ selection, planning, and critic-guided generation. Unbounded bilinear scores can let large embedding norms inflate off-support values, but cosine bounding does not remove the failure. A controlled support decomposition attributes most raw bilinear regret to norm drift. Cosine and hybrid critics nevertheless select off-support actions from most pools and incur comparable regret. Contrastive scores are weakly calibrated or inverted in the top score decile across four OGBench navigation tasks, and they fail to order fixed-query actions by value. Bellman-trained TD-Q succeeds, including in a parameter-matched function-class control. Realized costs depend on the task: simulator rollouts reveal single-step selection costs on PointMaze and the exact-$Q^*$ toy but well-powered nulls on AntMaze and HumanoidMaze, where the controller can self-correct. A training/readout decomposition traces the lost ordering to the cosine training objective; raw-trained embeddings retain weak ordering after inference-time normalization. Candidate maximization can therefore exploit false positives caused by norm drift, score saturation, or in-support misranking. Contrastive critics remain useful compatibility rankers on navigation and manipulation tasks, but action selection requires a value-calibrated scalar.

</details>


### [92] [SE(3)-MeanFlow: Few-Step Protein Backbone Generation on Lie Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27431)
*Yikun Bai,Binghang Lu,Yikai Liu,Elaheh Akbari,Soheil Kolouri,Linxuan Wang,Ping He,Shuchan Wang,Ruqi Zhang,Guang Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 SE(3)-MeanFlow，一种在蛋白质骨架生成任务中将 MeanFlow 从欧几里得空间推广到 SE(3) 李群几何的少步生成框架，通过在李代数 so(3) 和 R^3 中推导闭式平均速度恒等式，结合 alpha-Flow 预热和小 t 稳定化 MeanFlow 损失，实现了比扩散/流匹配基线更少采样步数下相当或更优的生成质量，并在超少步 regime 中凭借修正机制全面领先，多样性损失较小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 SE(3)^N 的扩散和流匹配模型虽然能生成高质量蛋白质骨架，但推理需要数百步网络评估并涉及李群指数映射，成为高通量设计的瓶颈。需要一种能在极少步数下保持生成质量的几何感知生成框架。

Method: 1. 在李代数 so(3) 和 R^3 原生空间推导旋转与平移的闭式平均速度恒等式，获得无仿真的训练目标。2. 引入 SE(3) alpha-Flow 目标，消除旋转分支的雅可比-向量积，作为预热阶段。3. 切换到小 t 稳定化的 MeanFlow 损失完成预训练并进行基于修正的后训练。整个框架原生处理蛋白质帧的 SE(3) 几何。

Result: 在蛋白质骨架生成基准上，SE(3)-MeanFlow 以比流匹配基线少数倍的采样步数达到相当或更优的生成质量；在超少步 regime 中，修正机制使其在每个匹配预算下均领先，多样性仅有适度下降。

Conclusion: SE(3)-MeanFlow 成功将 MeanFlow 推广到蛋白质骨架的李群几何，通过几何感知的闭式训练目标和两阶段训练策略，显著降低了推理步数并保持了生成质量，为高通量从头蛋白质设计提供了实用的少步生成范式。

Abstract: Generative modeling of protein backbones promises the de novo design of proteins with prescribed structural and functional properties. Existing diffusion and flow-matching models produce high-quality backbones on SE(3)^N, but inference requires numerically integrating an ODE over hundreds of network evaluations, each involving a Lie group exponential map - a bottleneck for high-throughput design campaigns. We introduce SE(3)-MeanFlow, a few-step generative framework that extends MeanFlow from Euclidean space to the Lie group geometry of protein frames. Working natively in the Lie algebra so(3) and in R^3, we derive closed-form average-velocity identities for rotations and translations, giving simulation-free training targets. We further introduce an SE(3) alpha-Flow objective that removes the Jacobian-vector product from the rotation branch and serves as a warm-up stage, after which training switches to a small-t stabilized MeanFlow loss that is used for the remainder of pretraining and for rectification-based post-training. In protein backbone generation, SE(3)-MeanFlow matches or exceeds flow-matching baselines that use several times more sampling steps, and its advantage widens in the few-step regime, where rectification lets it lead at every matched budget - at a modest cost in diversity.

</details>


### [93] [Neural Network-Assisted CLEAN for Channel Modeling in Low-SNR Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27450)
*Chaofan Deng,Linyu Sun,Jaeho Lee,Arijit Raychowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出神经网络辅助CLEAN（NN-CLEAN）混合框架，将多头残差网络嵌入迭代CLEAN循环，替代穷举网格搜索，实现低信噪比下多径参数的高精度、低复杂度实时估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统最大似然估计算法（如CLEAN）虽提供高分辨率参数提取，但穷举网格搜索导致计算复杂度过高；纯数据驱动深度学习方法缺乏物理约束，难以在多变多径密度和离网参数下泛化。需要一种兼具物理建模准确性和神经网络计算效率的混合方案。

Method: 提出NN-CLEAN框架：将多头残差网络直接嵌入迭代CLEAN提取循环，利用神经网络快速并行前向传播替代穷举网格搜索，同时保留精确数学模型进行残差减法，避免非物理误差累积。

Result: 蒙特卡洛仿真显示，NN-CLEAN在5 dB信噪比下估计准确率超96%，匹配传统网格搜索CLEAN基线，计算复杂度大幅降低，显著优于子空间方法和独立单次神经网络；且随批量规模增加，执行时间和内存消耗呈近平坦扩展，确立实时MIMO信道估计能力。

Conclusion: NN-CLEAN通过物理模型与神经网络的深度融合，解决了传统高分辨率估计算法的复杂度瓶颈和纯数据驱动方法的泛化难题，为低信噪比多径参数实时估计提供了鲁棒高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate multipath parameter estimation is critical for modern wireless communication systems, particularly in challenging low-SNR environments. Traditional Maximum Likelihood Estimation algorithms, such as CLEAN, provide high-resolution parameter extraction but suffer from prohibitive computational complexity due to exhaustive grid search. Conversely, purely data-driven deep learning approaches lack physical grounding and struggle to generalize across variable multipath densities and off-grid parameters. To address these limitations, this paper proposes Neural Network-Assisted CLEAN (NN-CLEAN), a hybrid framework that embeds a multi-head residual network directly into the iterative CLEAN extraction loop. By replacing the exhaustive grid search with rapid, parallelizable forward passes while delegating residual subtraction to exact mathematical models, NN-CLEAN isolates physical multipath parameters without accumulating non- physical errors. Extensive Monte Carlo simulations demonstrate that NN-CLEAN achieves estimation accuracy exceeding 96% at 5 dB SNR, matching the traditional Grid-Search CLEAN (GS- CLEAN) baseline, while providing a massive reduction in computational complexity and substantially outperforming subspace methods and standalone one-shot neural networks. Crucially, NN-CLEAN exhibits a near-flat scaling in execution runtime and memory consumption as batch sizes increase. This highly efficient parallelization establishes NN-CLEAN as a robust, real- time solution for channel estimation in MIMO systems.

</details>


### [94] [FADEx: Feature Attribution and Distortion-based Explanation of Dimensionality Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27463)
*Lucas Greff Meneses,Evandro S. Ortigossa,Claudio Silva,Luis Gustavo Nonato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FADEx，一种基于局部线性近似（一阶泰勒展开+奇异值分解）的逐实例特征归因方法，用于解释降维（DR）结果。该方法模型无关，无需域外样本映射，同时提供局部特征归因和失真分析。实验表明其解释鲁棒可靠，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性降维技术本身往往是不透明的变换，难以理解单个特征如何影响实例在降维空间中的位置。现有的DR解释方法存在每个特征多重归因、仅适用于特定降维方法等局限，阻碍了其应用。

Method: FADEx利用一阶泰勒展开和奇异值分解进行局部线性近似，通过加权最小二乘计算局部线性模型，消除了对域外样本映射的需求，使其对降维方法具有模型无关性，同时输出局部特征归因和失真分析。

Result: 通过定性和定量评估、与现有方法的比较以及案例研究，证明了FADEx在提供解释和分析资源方面的有效性和通用性。结果显示FADEx产生的解释鲁棒可靠，在多个方面优于现有方法。

Conclusion: FADEx解决了现有DR解释方法的局限性，提供了一种通用的、模型无关的局部解释方法，能够同时给出特征归因和失真分析，为降维结果的解释和分析提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Dimensionality Reduction (DR) is a fundamental tool for high-dimensional data exploration, reducing the complexity of latent spaces of machine learning models, and assisting in the explanation of complex opaque models. However, non-linear DR techniques often function as opaque transformations themselves, making it challenging to understand how individual features influence instance positioning in the reduced space. This lack of transparency complicates the analysis and interpretation of structural patterns, hindering the ability to reason about the organization of high-dimensional data based on the projected layout. In order to address this challenge, dimensionality reduction explanation methods have shown promise in improving the understanding of the observed groups and cluster structures. Unfortunately, existing DR explanation approaches tend to suffer from limitations such as multiple attributions per feature and restricted applicability to specific dimensionality reduction methods, which hinder their use. In this work, we propose FADEx, a novel local per-instance feature attribution method that leverages local linear approximation via first-order Taylor expansion and Singular Value Decomposition to provide explanations. FADEx computes the local linear models via weighted least squares, eliminating the need for out-of-sample data mapping, making it agnostic to the DR method, while simultaneously providing local feature attributions and distortion analysis. Through qualitative and quantitative evaluations, comparisons with existing methods, and case studies, we demonstrate FADEx's effectiveness and versatility in providing explanations and analytical resources for analyzing the behavior of DR methods. The results indicate FADEx yields robust and reliable explanations, outperforming existing approaches in several aspects.

</details>


### [95] [Schreier-Coset Graph Rewiring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27479)
*Aryan Mishra,Randy Martinez,Lizhen Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于群论的 Schreier-Coset 图重连方法 (SCGR)，通过引入特殊线性群导出的 Schreier-Coset 图增强原始图，理论上保证谱间隙和有界有效电阻，为长距离通信提供低电阻旁路，实验显示有效电阻降低 5-40%，缓解连接瓶颈且保持竞争性准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN 中的信息流受限于过压现象，结构瓶颈阻碍长距离信息传播。现有图重连方法虽缓解该问题，但常引入结构和计算瓶颈，破坏原图关键属性，且大量增加边数。

Method: 提出 Schreier-Coset 图重连 (SCGR)，一种群论重连方法，利用特殊线性群导出的 Schreier-Coset 图增强输入图，提供理论保证：图表现出谱间隙和有界有效电阻，形成长距离通信的低电阻旁路。

Result: 实验评估表明 SCGR 在各类学习任务中将有效电阻降低 5-40%，有效缓解连接瓶颈，同时保持有竞争力的准确率。

Conclusion: SCGR 通过群论构造的重连图，在理论上保证谱间隙和有界有效电阻，实践中显著降低有效电阻，缓解过压问题，且不牺牲模型性能，为 GNN 长距离信息传播提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: The information flow in the graph neural networks (GNNs) is fundamentally constrained by over-squashing, where structural bottlenecks impede long range information propagation. Graph-rewiring methods, which modify graph topology, have been extensively used to alleviate this. However, existing approaches often introduce prohibitive structural and computational bottlenecks, fail to preserve the critical properties of original graphs, and increase the edge counts massively. We introduce a novel method Schreier-Coset Graph Rewiring , a group-theoretic rewiring method that augments the input graph with a Schreier-Coset graph derived from a special linear group. Our method provides theoretical guarantees, a graph that exhibits spectral gap and a bounded effective resistance, creating a low-resistance bypass for long-range communication. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that SCGR reduces effective resistance by 5-40% across various learning tasks, effectively mitigating connectivity bottlenecks while maintaining competitive accuracy.

</details>


### [96] [Latent States in Neural Networks: Recovering the Temporal Structure of Drifting Data from Model Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27482)
*Kevin Guan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出利用隐马尔可夫模型（HMM）对按时间顺序训练的模型权重轨迹建模，从而恢复时序漂移数据流中的离散潜在状态，并在多模态虚假信息检测（Fakeddit）和情感分析（Yelp）两个任务上验证了该方法的有效性。实验表明，同一潜在状态内的窗口间迁移泛化优于跨状态窗口，且该优势在控制时间邻近性和类别分布偏移后依然显著，说明潜在状态捕捉到了超越数据分布的可迁移结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序数据流的分布漂移往往呈现离散的 regime 变化而非连续演化。现有方法多关注连续适应或基于数据分布的变化点检测，但模型权重作为训练过程的压缩表示，可能蕴含更丰富的时序结构信息。作者旨在探究：仅从按时间顺序训练的模型权重轨迹中，能否无监督地恢复出有意义的离散潜在状态，并验证这些状态是否对跨时间窗口的迁移泛化具有指导价值。

Method: 1. 将时间轴划分为连续的时间窗口，在每个窗口上训练分类器（Fakeddit 用多模态模型，Yelp 用文本分类器）。 2. 对齐各窗口模型的权重（如通过 Procrustes 对齐），得到按时间顺序的权重轨迹。 3. 在权重轨迹上拟合隐马尔可夫模型（HMM），推断离散的潜在状态序列，从而将时间线分割为若干连贯相位。 4. 评估分类器在同状态窗口与跨状态窗口上的泛化性能差异，并引入时间邻近性控制、等大小连续分区基线、类别分布偏移残余化、置换检验等严格对照实验。

Result: 在两个数据集上均观察到：同一潜在状态内的窗口间迁移泛化显著优于跨状态窗口（within-state transfer advantage）。该优势在控制时间邻近性后依然存在，且优于朴素的等大小连续分区基线。潜在状态与数据的类别分布变化相关性强于权重空间几何本身。在残余化类别分布偏移和滞后效应后，优势仍超过置换零分布，表明状态捕捉到了超越数据分布的可迁移结构。所有效应在两个任务上均复现，但在 Yelp 上减弱，因为其标签分布时间稳定性更高。

Conclusion: 模型权重的时序轨迹蕴含着数据流潜在 regime 结构的关键信息。基于权重轨迹的 HMM 能无监督地恢复出与迁移泛化相关的离散潜在状态，这些状态不仅对应类别分布的宏观变化，还编码了影响跨时间泛化的细微结构。该方法为时序漂移场景下的模型选择、适应策略设计及分布变化理解提供了新视角。

Abstract: A temporally drifting data stream may pass through discrete regimes rather than changing continuously. We ask whether such regimes are recoverable from the weights of models trained on the stream, using a hidden Markov model (HMM) fit to the chronologically ordered trajectory of those weights. We study this question in two domains known to drift over time: multimodal misinformation detection, using the Fakeddit dataset; and sentiment analysis, using the Yelp dataset. We train classifiers on consecutive temporal windows and fit an HMM to the trajectory of their aligned weights, recovering latent states that partition each timeline into coherent phases. On both datasets, classifiers generalize better to data from windows sharing the state of their training window than to windows across state boundaries. This within-state transfer advantage survives a control for temporal proximity and modestly exceeds the advantage recovered by a naive partition into contiguous states of equal size. Although the states are estimated solely from model weights, they correlate more strongly with shifts in the data's class distribution than with the weight-space geometry used to estimate them. After class divergence and lag are residualized out, the within-state advantage exceeds its permutation null on both tasks, indicating that the states recover structure relevant to transfer beyond the data distribution. Every effect replicates on both tasks but is attenuated on Yelp, whose label distribution is more temporally stable.

</details>


### [97] [A Lightweight Foundation Model for Collider Physics with Multi-Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27501)
*Liangyu Wu,Qibin Liu,Alexander Yue,Julia Gonski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NEXUS 是一个轻量级基础模型，利用大型强子对撞机（LHC）的无监督预训练，仅用约 300 万参数的全连接自编码器，在下游任务（如运动学回归、事件分类）中优于从头训练，且泛化到引力波、洪水预测、神经活动等跨域任务，计算效率高，适合边缘部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决科学领域基础模型的轻量化、跨域泛化和计算效率问题，利用大规模无标签对撞机数据预训练，减少下游任务对大量标注数据的依赖，并探索跨科学领域的迁移学习潜力。

Method: 采用全连接自编码器架构（约 300 万参数），在 LHC 带电粒子轨迹特征的大规模碰撞数据集上进行无监督预训练；随后在下游任务（运动学回归、事件分类）上微调预训练权重，并通过潜在空间解释分析预训练益处；进一步将预训练模型应用于引力波、洪水预测、神经活动等跨域数据集；对比 Transformer 同等规模模型，展示计算简洁性。

Result: 预训练模型在仅使用少量标注数据的情况下，显著提升下游任务准确率；潜在空间解释揭示预训练学到的物理有意义特征；跨域实验表明 NEXUS 能有效迁移至引力波、洪水预测、神经活动等任务；计算复杂度远低于同规模 Transformer，支持能效推理和实时/边缘部署。

Conclusion: NEXUS 证明了轻量级自编码器基础模型在科学数据上的有效性，通过无监督预训练实现跨域泛化，且计算高效，为科学实验中的实时和边缘基础模型应用铺平道路。

Abstract: We present a lightweight approach to foundation modeling (\textbf{NEXUS}) that leverages pre-trained learning from collider physics data towards out-of-domain tasks in other scientific datasets, using a fully connected autoencoder model with approximately 3 million parameters. The model pre-trains with no supervision over a large-scale collision dataset from the Large Hadron Collider modeled by charged particle track features. Downstream tasks for collider analyses, such as kinematic regression and event classification, are developed on pre-trained model weights and achieve improved accuracy with only small labeled datasets when compared to equivalent architectures trained from scratch. The benefits of pre-training are additionally investigated through latent space interpretation and application to other domains, including gravitational waves, flood forecasting, and neural activity. Furthermore, the relative computational simplicity of NEXUS is demonstrated compared to transformer approaches at comparable scale, opening the door to power-efficient inference and real-time or edge applications of foundation models in scientific experiments.

</details>


### [98] [Sparsity Induced Identifiability in Matrix Tri-Factorisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27507)
*Tingting Mu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次建立了广义实值矩阵三因子分解中稀疏诱导可识别性的严格理论框架，提出了一种新颖的分解策略将原问题转化为两个耦合的辅助因子分解问题，并推导了恢复保证和结构一致性结果，蒙特卡洛实验验证了理论与实证的高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵三因子分解相比传统双因子模型具有更大的建模灵活性，稀疏约束常能提升可解释性和恢复性能。然而，尽管稀疏性在双因子矩阵分解中已被广泛研究，但对于广义实值矩阵三因子分解，严格的理论保证仍 largely unexplored，亟需填补这一理论空白。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的分解策略，将原始的矩阵三因子分解问题转化为两个耦合的辅助因子分解问题，同时保留了从观测中恢复原始因子矩阵所需的结构信息。基于此分解，推导了恢复保证和结构一致性结果，表征了系数稀疏性如何影响充分恢复条件、收敛行为、谱近似误差、高概率界以及结构保持。

Result: 建立了稀疏诱导可识别性的严格理论框架，给出了恢复保证和结构一致性结果，包括充分恢复条件、收敛行为、谱近似误差、高概率界和结构保持的完整表征。综合蒙特卡洛实验验证了理论结果，并展示了理论预测与实证观察的高度一致。

Conclusion: 本文填补了广义实值矩阵三因子分解稀疏诱导可识别性理论研究的空白，为稀疏约束下的矩阵三因子分解提供了首个严格的理论保证，其提出的分解策略和理论分析框架为后续相关研究奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: Matrix factorisation is a fundamental tool for exploiting low-dimensional structure in high-dimensional data, with applications such as data compression, denoising, structure discovery, interpretable representation learning, and dimensionality reduction. Compared to conventional two-factor models, matrix tri-factorisation provides greater modelling flexibility, while sparsity constraints often improve both interpretability and recovery performance. Although the role of sparsity has been extensively studied for two-factor matrix factorisation, rigorous theoretical guarantees for general real-valued matrix tri-factorisation remain largely unexplored. To address this gap, we establish, to the best of our knowledge, the first rigorous theoretical study for sparsity-induced identifiability in general real-valued matrix tri-factorisation. Our analysis is enabled by a novel decomposition strategy that transforms the original problem into two coupled auxiliary factorisation problems, while preserving the structural information necessary to the recovery of the original factor matrices from the observations. Building upon this decomposition, we derive recovery guarantees and structural consistency results that characterise how coefficient sparsity influences the sufficient recovery conditions, convergence behaviour, spectral approximation error, high-probability bounds, and structure preservation. Comprehensive Monte Carlo experiments validate the proposed theory and demonstrate close agreement between the theoretical results and empirical observations.

</details>


### [99] [Expanding Data-Agnostic Pivotal Instances Selection Models with Proximity Trees and Ensemble Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27522)
*Alessio Cascione,Mattia Setzu,Cristiano Landi,Paolo Maria Mancarella,Riccardo Guidotti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受决策树启发的分层可解释枢轴选择模型，基于枢轴与输入实例的相似性进行构建，支持枢轴对、集成方法且模态无关，利用预训练网络进行数据变换，在多种数据类型上实现了优于现有实例选择策略且与最先进可解释模型竞争的性能，同时保持极少数量的枢轴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习方法往往依赖难以解释的决策过程，而人类自然通过将新案例与少量代表性示例（枢轴）比较来做决策，因此需要设计一种能选择此类枢轴以构建可解释预测模型的方法。

Method: 提出分层、设计即可解释的枢轴选择模型，基于枢轴与输入实例的相似性，受决策树启发。该方法既作为枢轴选择技术也可作为独立预测模型。扩展至枢轴对（用于邻近树和斜交树）及集成方法，增强通用性和有效性。方法具有数据模态无关性，利用预训练网络进行数据变换。

Result: 在表格、文本、图像、时间序列等多样化数据集上实验表明，该方法优于替代实例选择策略，达到与最先进可解释模型具有竞争力的结果，同时保持极少数量的枢轴。

Conclusion: 所提出的分层可解释枢轴选择模型有效平衡了可解释性与性能，跨数据模态表现出色，为可解释机器学习提供了一种通用且有效的方法。

Abstract: As decision-making processes grow more complex, machine learning tools have become essential for tackling business and societal challenges. However, many existing methods rely on decision-making procedures that are difficult to interpret. Since humans naturally make decisions by comparing new cases with a few representative examples, we aim to design an approach that selects such pivots to construct an interpretable predictive model. Inspired by decision trees, we propose a hierarchical, interpretable-by-design pivot selection model based on the similarity between pivots and input instances. Our method functions both as a pivot selection technique and a standalone predictive model. Extending beyond single pivots, we incorporate pairs of pivots that are used by proximity and oblique trees, as well as ensembles, which enhance the versatility and effectiveness of our proposal. Additionally, our approach is data modality-agnostic, leveraging pre-trained networks for data transformation. Experiments across diverse datasets, including tabular data, text, images, and time series, demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach, outperforming alternative instance selection strategies and achieving competitive results against state-of-the-art interpretable models while maintaining a minimal number of pivots.

</details>


### [100] [Latent-Kernel Discrete Flow Maps for Few-Step Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27529)
*Mansoor Ahmed,Yue-Tsz Fan,Hemanth Venkateswara,Murray Patterson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了潜核离散流映射(LKF)，通过引入共享潜变量将多个因子化组件耦合，实现原生相关步骤的离散扩散/流匹配，在少步生成中显著优于基线并超越蒸馏/校正方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散扩散和流匹配模型在每步因子化转移、独立决定每个token，导致难以捕捉位置间相关性（如主谓一致），少步生成时需大量函数评估修复错配；现有少步方法通过蒸馏或校正慢速教师模型恢复相关性，但继承了教师质量上限。

Method: 提出LKF模型，是一种从头训练的流映射核，由M个因子化组件混合而成，通过单一共享潜变量绑定。条件于潜变量时每个组件计算廉价，混合模型可对小M闭式求和。单步采样仅需为每个序列抽取一次潜变量并在整个去噪轨迹中复用，时间复杂度与因子化模型相同。MDLM是M=1时的特例。

Result: 在One-Billion-Word和WikiText-103无条件文本生成实验中，LKF学习到强异质组件，生成困惑度比似然基线提升2.1-3.3倍且不损失多样性；增益随M增长，M=8时超越蒸馏和校正的少步采样器。

Conclusion: LKF通过原生建模token间相关性，在不增加采样复杂度的前提下显著提升少步离散生成质量，为从头训练的高质量少步离散扩散/流匹配提供了新范式。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion and flow-matching models denoise a sequence over many steps, but to keep each step cheap, they factorize the transition across positions and decide every token independently. This makes few-step generation challenging for text when the target couples two positions, such as a subject and a verb that must agree. An independent update commits to them separately, and many function evaluations are spent repairing the mismatch. Existing few-step methods buy back the lost correlation by distilling or rectifying a slow teacher, and so inherit the teacher's quality ceiling. We ask instead whether a model can express correlated steps natively, and answer with Latent-Kernel Discrete Flow Maps (LKF), a from-scratch flow-map kernel that is a mixture of M factorized components tied by a single shared latent. Conditioned on the latent, each component is cheap, and the mixture is summed over the latent in closed form for small M. We show that a single step places mass on correlated completions with the same sampling time complexity as a factorized model, since one latent is drawn per sequence and reused across the entire denoising trajectory. We also show that the Masked Diffusion Language Model (MDLM) is a special case of our LKF model at M=1. The experiments for unconditional text generation on the One-Billion-Word (LM1B) and WikiText-103 benchmarks show that our LKF model learns strongly heterogeneous components and improves generative perplexity by 2.1x to 3.3x over the likelihood baselines without losing diversity. The gain grows with M, and at M=8, it surpasses distilled and rectified few-step samplers. The source code is available at: https://github.com/mansoor181/lkf.git

</details>


### [101] [When Does Explicit View Routing Work? A Controlled Study of Multi-View Graph-Text Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27530)
*Xiao Yue,Guangzhi Qu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究图文检索中的语义路由问题，提出受控的MV-GTA模型，在BBBP和BACE数据集上验证正确路由显著提升检索性能，但拓扑视图不一致，且证据仅限于显式标签和属性路由，未证明自由形式路由或专家模型等效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图文检索将图和描述映射为单一嵌入，即使查询仅涉及单一语义方面（如类别标签或分子属性）。多头机制可分离这些方面，但查询头的改变可能改变检索结果，即使发送错误文本，这体现架构通道化而非语义路由。需探究何种条件下能解决这一区别。

Method: 提出受控版MV-GTA，使用确定性可验证文本段、隔离文本编码器、视图特定图头，以及基于外部标签或RDKit描述符的相关性。通过正确路由和逐样本错位训练形成因果测试，检验检索是否依赖内容。在BBBP和BACE数据集上实验，对比联合模型与单独训练的专家模型，并测试属性改写增强和一致性/硬模板扩展。

Result: 正确路由使标签和属性nDCG比错位训练提升0.305-0.685；预期图头比最佳错误头高0.303-0.453。拓扑视图在两数据集上不一致。三种子对比中，联合模型拓扑/标签/属性nDCG为0.720/1.000/0.877，三单独专家为0.633/0.976/0.859。属性改写增强使未见模板nDCG提升0.140和0.147。一致性和硬模板扩展在某些设置下降低规范检索。

Conclusion: 证据仅限于显式、外部锚定的标签和属性路由及观察到的多接口整合。未建立自由形式路由、一致的三视图专业化、与专家模型的统计等效性或更优的下游预测。

Abstract: Graph-text retrieval typically maps a graph and its description to a single embedding, even when a query concerns only one semantic aspect, such as a class label or molecular property. Multiple heads can separate these aspects, but a change in the query head may alter retrieval even when the wrong text is sent to that head. Such behavior demonstrates architectural channelization, not necessarily semantic routing. We examine the conditions under which this distinction can be resolved. Our controlled version of MV-GTA uses deterministic, verifiable text segments; isolated text encoders; view-specific graph heads; and relevance derived from external labels or RDKit descriptors. Correct routing and per-sample derangements form a causal test of whether retrieval depends on content. On BBBP and BACE, correct routing improves label and property nDCG by 0.305 to 0.685 over deranged training. The expected graph head exceeds the best wrong head by 0.303 to 0.453. Topology does not specialize consistently across the two datasets. In a matched three-seed comparison, one joint model obtains mean topology, label, and property nDCG of 0.720/1.000/0.877; three separately trained Single specialists obtain 0.633/0.976/0.859. Property paraphrase augmentation also improves unseen-template nDCG by 0.140 and 0.147 over a matched-exposure canonical control. Consistency and hard-template extensions, however, reduce canonical retrieval in some settings. The evidence is therefore limited to explicit, externally grounded label and property routing and observed multi-interface consolidation.
